### [1] [Parametrizing Reads-From Equivalence for Predictive Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06533)
*Azadeh Farzan,Umang Mathur*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出k-切片重排序作为并发程序预测运行时监控的参数化框架，在表达能力和计算成本之间提供可调节的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测运行时监控需要在表达能力（重排序范围）和计算效率之间取得平衡。现有方法要么过于复杂（如读自等价导致不可判定），要么过于受限（如Mazurkiewicz迹等价表达能力有限）。

Method: 引入切片重排序及其推广形式k-切片重排序。ρ是σ的k-切片重排序，如果σ可以被划分为k+1个有序子序列，这些子序列的连接产生ρ，同时保持程序顺序和读自约束。

Result: 1) k-切片重排序形成严格递增的层次结构，随着k增长收敛到读自等价；2) 对于任意固定的k，针对任何正则规范的预测监控模k-切片重排序都允许常数空间流算法。

Conclusion: k-切片重排序为预测运行时监控提供了原则性的参数化框架，使表达能力可以系统地与计算成本进行权衡，为并发程序分析提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: Predictive runtime monitoring asks whether an execution $σ$ of a concurrent program can be used to \emph{soundly predict} the existence of a reordering $ρ$ of $σ$ that satisfies a property $\varphi$.
  Its effectiveness and efficiency depend on two factors: (a) the complexity of $\varphi$, and (b) the expressive power of the reorderings considered. At one extreme, allowing all reorderings induced by \emph{reads-from equivalence} makes predictive monitoring intractable, even for simple properties such as data races. At the other extreme, restricting to commutativity-based reorderings (Mazurkiewicz trace equivalence) yields efficient algorithms for simple properties, but remains intractable for general regular specifications and offers limited predictive power.
  We address this tradeoff via \emph{parametrization}. We introduce \emph{sliced reorderings} and their generalization, \emph{$k$-sliced reorderings}. Informally, $ρ$ is a $k$-sliced reordering of $σ$ if $σ$ can be partitioned into $k+1$ ordered subsequences whose concatenation yields $ρ$, while preserving program order and reads-from constraints.
  Our results are twofold. First, $k$-sliced reorderings form a strictly increasing hierarchy that converges to reads-from equivalence as $k$ grows. Second, for any fixed $k$, predictive monitoring modulo $k$-sliced reorderings against any regular specification admits a constant-space streaming algorithm.
  Together, these results establish $k$-sliced reorderings as a principled alternative to existing equivalences, enabling a uniform parametrized framework where expressive power can be systematically traded off against computational cost.

</details>


### [2] [Branching Out: Existential External Choice in Effpi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06875)
*Benjamin Robinson,Nobuko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Effpi框架扩展：在Scala中通过引入分支操作、多通道消息接收和超时处理机制增强强类型消息传递协议的表达能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Effpi工具包在表达能力上存在限制，需要扩展以支持更复杂的协议模式，特别是外部选择和超时处理机制

Method: 1. 引入分支操作实现外部选择；2. 支持在多通道上接收消息；3. 添加"catch timeout"操作处理消息缺失；4. 通过编译器插件将行为类型编码为CCS变体进行协议验证

Result: 扩展后的Effpi框架表达能力显著增强，能够处理更复杂的协议场景，并通过Raft共识算法等示例证明了其实际应用价值

Conclusion: 通过引入分支操作、多通道消息接收和超时处理机制，成功扩展了Effpi框架的表达能力，使其能够支持更复杂的分布式系统协议实现和验证

Abstract: Effpi is a framework for writing strongly-typed message-passing programs in Scala, where the compiler enforces the conformance of process implementations to specified protocol types. A compiler plugin is provided to verify properties of protocols, such as deadlock-freedom and liveness, by encoding the behavioural types into a variant of CCS.
  To address limitations in the expressiveness of the existing toolkit, we extend Effpi with external choice by introducing a branching operation. Upon accepting a message via a branch, protocols enforce a continuation which depends on the label (type) of the received message. We equip the branching operation with the ability to accept messages over more than one channel. Additionally, we introduce a "catch timeout" operation to allow processes to gracefully handle a lack of incoming messages. The enhanced expressiveness of Effpi is demonstrated through a number of examples, including an implementation of the Raft consensus algorithm.

</details>


### [3] [Towards Multiparty Session Types for Highly-Concurrent and Fault-Tolerant Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06878)
*Richard Casetta,Nils Gesbert,Pierre Genevès*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种增强的多方会话类型框架，为Web应用中的通信故障处理和动态参与提供形式化语义


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web应用结合了持久状态更新、并发交互和不可靠的外部服务通信。故障（如超时）可能在部分状态更改后发生，导致临时不一致性，其解决依赖于实践中通常未验证的活性属性。虽然形式化方法为复杂软件推理提供了严格保证，但由于其感知复杂性和缺乏实际自动化，在企业环境中很少采用。多方会话类型提供通信安全性保证，但未考虑状态演化、动态工作流结构和故障行为之间的相互作用，而这些对于推理真实Web应用的正确性至关重要。

Method: 引入一个全局类型框架，为多方会话类型配备显式故障语义和动态参与机制。定义了这些增强全局类型的语法和操作语义，并建立了核心属性，包括一致性保持。

Result: 建立了一个形式化基础，能够在可能发生故障的Web应用中推理通信行为，为未来的有状态扩展和活性属性的自动化验证奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该研究通过增强多方会话类型框架，为解决Web应用中通信故障处理和动态参与的形式化验证问题提供了理论基础，填补了现有方法在状态演化、动态工作流和故障行为综合推理方面的空白。

Abstract: Modern web applications combine persistent state updates, concurrent interactions, and unreliable communication with external services. Failures such as timeouts can occur after partial state changes, producing temporary inconsistencies whose resolution depends on liveness properties that are often not verified in practice. Although formal methods offer rigorous guarantees for reasoning about complex software, they remain rarely adopted in enterprise settings due to their perceived complexity and lack of practical automation. Multiparty Session Types (MPST) offer strong guarantees for communication safety, yet they do not account for the interplay between state evolution, dynamic workflow structure, and failure behaviour that are essential for reasoning about the correctness of real web applications.
  This paper introduces a global-type framework that equips MPST with explicit failure semantics and dynamic participation. We define the syntax and operational semantics of these enriched global types and establish core properties, including coherence preservation. This foundation enables formal reasoning about communications in web applications where failures may occur, and lays the groundwork for future stateful extensions and automated verification of liveness properties.

</details>


### [4] [Determinacy with Priorities up to Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06879)
*Luigi Liquori,Michael Mendler,Claude Stolze*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 扩展CCS以支持优先级保护和时钟，引入相干性概念来编码同步编程语言


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Milner的CCS汇合理论无法表达因果关系和共享内存多线程，特别是对缺席的反应，不能以组合方式编码Esterel等同步编程语言

Method: 扩展CCS，添加优先级保护动作和时钟，并基于此丰富Milner的汇合概念，引入新的相干性概念

Result: 能够以组合方式编码Esterel等同步编程语言，解决了CCS汇合理论在表达因果关系和共享内存多线程方面的局限性

Conclusion: 通过扩展CCS并引入相干性概念，成功建立了同步编程语言与进程代数理论之间的桥梁，为确定性并发提供了更丰富的理论基础

Abstract: In Milner's seminal book on communication and concurrency introducing CCS, a process algebra inherently non-deterministic, chapter 11 was completely devoted to introduce the notion of determinacy and confluence in order to identify a subcalculus of CCS in which all definable agents are confluent. At the same time, or shortly later, determinate semantics were given for programming languages that reconcile concurrency and determinacy, such as Esterel by Berry and Gonthier, or SL by Boussinot and de Simone. These dedicated semantics do not easily map to Milner's confluence theory for CCS, which is unable to express causality and shared memory multi-threading with reaction to absence in a compositional way. We present an extension of CCS with priority-guarded actions and clocks, and we exploit the added expressiveness to enrich Milner's original notion of confluence by the new concept of coherence which permits us to encode, in a compositional fashion, synchronous programming languages such as Esterel.

</details>

### [5] [Toward a Uniform Algorithm and Uniform Reduction for Constraint Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06335)
*Libor Barto,Maximilian Hadek,Dmitriy Zhuk*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架来刻画约束满足问题（CSP）中高级算法（如k-一致性、Sherali-Adams LP层次、仿射IP层次）的能力，并引入了一个新的基于Z_p向量空间的SDP类松弛层次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究约束满足问题中各种高级算法（如一致性检查、线性规划层次、整数规划层次）的统一理论框架，以更系统地理解这些算法的计算能力及其相互关系。

Method: 1. 开发统一框架，使用多态minion理论描述CSP模板的算法可解性；2. 引入新的SDP类向量松弛层次，在Z_p向量空间中施加k元组正交性约束；3. 证明该松弛与AIP-Z_p层次的第k级等价；4. 应用该框架分析具体CSP实例。

Result: 1. 证明了固定模板CSP或Promise CSP在给定层次的可解性仅取决于模板的多态minion；2. 获得了k-一致性约简的minion理论描述；3. 新Z_p向量松弛与AIP-Z_p层次等价；4. 该松弛解决了二面体群D_4的CSP（这是欺骗单例BLP+AIP算法的最小CSP）；5. Z_p松弛的第p级解决了模p^2的线性方程组。

Conclusion: 本文建立了CSP高级算法的统一minion理论框架，揭示了算法能力与模板代数结构之间的深刻联系。新引入的Z_p向量松弛层次不仅与现有层次等价，还能解决传统算法难以处理的CSP实例，为理解约束满足问题的算法复杂性提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a unified framework to characterize the power of higher-level algorithms for the constraint satisfaction problem (CSP), such as $k$-consistency, the Sherali-Adams LP hierarchy, and the affine IP hierarchy. As a result, solvability of a fixed-template CSP or, more generally, a Promise CSP by a given level is shown to depend only on the polymorphism minion of the template. Similarly, we obtain a minion-theoretic description of $k$-consistency reductions between Promise CSPs.
  We introduce a new hierarchy of SDP-like vector relaxations with vectors over $\mathbb Z_{p}$ in which orthogonality is imposed on $k$-tuples of vectors. Surprisingly, this relaxation turns out to be equivalent to the $k$-th level of the AIP-$\mathbb{Z}_p$ relaxation. We show that it solves the CSP of the dihedral group $\mathbf{D}_4$, the smallest CSP that fools the singleton BLP+AIP algorithm. Using this vector representation, we further show that the $p$-th level of the $\mathbb{Z}_p$ relaxation solves linear equations modulo $p^2$.

</details>


### [6] [The complexity of bisimilarity on pointmass processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06443)
*Martín Santiago Moroni,Pedro Sánchez Terraf*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非确定性标记马尔可夫过程（NLMP）中双模拟关系的描述复杂性，证明了在有限支撑测度分配下双模拟是解析的，在良基过程中是Borel的，并给出了复杂性下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非确定性标记马尔可夫过程（NLMP）中双模拟关系的描述复杂性，特别是在不可数标准Borel空间上的复杂性分类，这对于理解行为等价关系的可计算性和逻辑可定义性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用描述集合论方法分析双模拟关系的复杂性：1）证明在有限支撑测度分配下双模拟是解析的；2）证明在可数Kripke框架上双模拟可由可数结构分类；3）证明良基过程的双模拟是Borel的；4）通过将二元序列最终相等关系E_0归约到双模拟来提供复杂性下界。

Result: 1）在有限支撑测度分配下，双模拟关系是解析的；2）可数Kripke框架上的双模拟可由可数结构分类；3）良基过程的双模拟是Borel的；4）存在复杂性下界：没有可数片段的基本模态逻辑（含可数合取）能够刻画小秩过程的双模拟。

Conclusion: 双模拟关系在NLMP中的描述复杂性取决于过程的结构特性：在有限支撑测度下是解析的，在良基过程中是Borel的，且存在严格的复杂性下界。这些结果为理解行为等价关系的逻辑可定义性和计算复杂性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We assess the descriptive complexity of *bisimilarity* or "equality of behavior" on a family of Markov decision processes over uncountable standard Borel spaces, namely *nondeterministic labelled Markov processes* (NLMP).
  We show that bisimilarity is analytic for processes with a uniform assignment of finitely-supported measures to each state and label. More finely, we obtain that bisimilarity on the space of countable Kripke frames (or labelled transition systems) is classifiable by countable structures.
  We show that bisimilarity of well-founded ("terminating") processes is Borel. We also provide a lower complexity bound by reducing the relation of eventual equality of binary sequences $E_0$ to the former. As a consequence, there is no countable fragment of basic modal logic with denumerable conjunctions that characterizes bisimilarity for processes of small rank.
  We finally apply the previous Borel definability to study the well-founded part of discrete uniform processes over uncountable spaces.

</details>


### [7] [Tractable Hyperproperties for MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06859)
*Lina Gerlach,Tobias Winkler,Erika Ábrahám,Borzoo Bonakdarpour,Sebastian Junges*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究概率超性质的片段验证问题，关注MDP中不同事件概率之间的关系，提供高效算法并证明计算复杂性结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率超逻辑（如HyperPCTL和PHL）在MDP中的模型检测是不可判定的，因此需要研究可验证的概率超性质片段，特别是那些描述不同事件概率关系的性质

Method: 研究关系概率性质，包括可达性、安全性、Büchi和coBüchi目标，这些性质可以组合使用。为相关类别的关系性质提供高效算法，同时证明其他类别的计算复杂性和完备性结果

Result: 实现了该方法，在属于研究片段的基准测试中，性能比更通用的概率超逻辑求解器高出数量级。提供了相关类别关系性质的高效算法，并证明了计算复杂性结果

Conclusion: 通过专注于可验证的概率超性质片段，特别是关系概率性质，可以在保持表达力的同时实现高效验证，为信息安全等应用中的概率关系验证提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Probabilistic hyperproperties describe probabilistic relations between multiple sets of executions in a stochastic system. Prominent examples include information-theoretic characterizations of security and privacy policies. However, model checking for existing probabilistic hyperlogics, such as HyperPCTL and PHL, is undecidable in Markov decision processes (MDPs). In this paper, we study an underexplored problem: the verification of fragments of probabilistic hyperproperties that relate the probabilities of different events to each other, possibly across independent executions of an MDP. Representative verification questions include: Can two different target states be reached from the same initial state with the same probability? (different events), Can a given target state be reached from two different initial states with the same probability? (same event, independent executions), and natural combinations of these forms. Besides reachability, our relational probabilistic properties cover safety, Büchi, and coBüchi objectives. They can also be combined conjunctively, thereby generalizing standard multi-objective MDP properties. We provide efficient algorithms for relevant classes of relational properties, while proving computational hardness and completeness results for others. An implementation of our approach outperforms solvers for more general probabilistic hyperlogics by orders of magnitude on the subset of their benchmarks that lies within our fragment.

</details>


### [8] [Asynchronous Multiparty Sessions with Mixed Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06872)
*Franco Barbanera,Mariangiola Dezani-Ciancaglini*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出支持混合选择（输入和输出前缀）的异步多方会话演算，扩展了Simple MultiParty Session框架，通过基于全局类型和通信标签集一致性的类型系统确保会话安全属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多方会话类型框架主要基于投影方法，缺乏对混合选择（同时包含输入和输出前缀的非确定性选择）的充分支持。需要开发新的类型系统来处理异步通信环境中的混合选择，确保会话的正确性和安全性。

Method: 扩展Simple MultiParty Session框架，引入支持混合选择的异步演算。定义配备归纳定义标签转换系统的全局类型，基于通信标签集一致性概念构建类型系统。该方法与传统的基于投影的方法正交，通过一致性条件确保会话安全。

Result: 证明了类型化多方会话的基本定理，包括主题归约和会话保真性。这些性质意味着类型化会话既是无锁的也是无孤儿消息的。还研究了类型系统扩展的事件接收性属性，并通过示例展示了混合选择在异步多方协议中的表达能力和类型规则的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的支持混合选择的异步多方会话演算和类型系统为异步通信环境中的非确定性选择提供了有效的类型化方法。该方法与传统的投影方法正交，能够确保会话的安全属性，包括无锁性、无孤儿消息性和事件接收性，扩展了多方会话类型的表达能力。

Abstract: We present an asynchronous calculus for multiparty sessions with mixed choice, which extends the Simple MultiParty Session framework in order to support nondeterministic choices with both input and output prefixes. Global types -- equipped with a coinductively defined labelled transition system -- form the basis of a type system that exploits the key notion of coherence of communication label sets. Roughly, a coherent set contains either all the communications enabled in the session, or all the actions currently exhibited by a participant,  provided that at least one input is enabled for each expected sender.  Our approach to the typing of multiparty sessions is orthogonal to the well-established, projection-based approaches of the MultiParty Session Type framework. We prove fundamental theorems for typable multiparty sessions, including Subject Reduction and Session Fidelity. These properties imply that typable sessions are both Lock-Free and Orphan-Message-Free. We also investigate the Eventual Reception property for an extension of the type system. Some  examples demonstrate the expressiveness of mixed choice in asynchronous multiparty protocols and the effectiveness of the proposed type discipline.

</details>


### [9] [Predicate Subtypes in VerCors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06877)
*Tycho Dubbeling,Marieke Huisman,Ömer Şakar*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 为VerCors程序验证器添加谓词子类型支持，自动从子类型声明生成规范，支持组合多个子类型，引入严格模式进行溢出检查


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谓词子类型为变量声明的范围约束提供了有吸引力的规范机制，但需要将其集成到现有程序验证器中以增强验证能力

Method: 在VerCors验证器中添加谓词子类型支持，自动从子类型声明生成适当规范，支持单变量声明的多子类型组合，引入严格模式确保所有子表达式都在声明的子类型范围内

Result: 开发了原型实现并集成到VerCors验证器中，实现了谓词子类型的自动化规范生成和验证支持

Conclusion: 成功为VerCors验证器添加了谓词子类型支持，提供了自动规范生成、多子类型组合和严格溢出检查功能

Abstract: Predicate subtypes provide an attractive mechanism to specify range constraints on variable declarations. This paper discusses how we add support for predicate subtypes to the VerCors program verifier. Our approach automatically generates appropriate specifications from predicate subtype declarations. It provides support to easily combine multiple subtypes for a single variable declaration. Moreover, in order to use predicate subtypes for overflow checking, a special strict mode is introduced, where every subexpression also has to stay within the declared subtype. A prototype implementation is integrated into the VerCors verifier.

</details>

### [10] [Multiprotocol Wireless Timer Synchronization for IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07199)
*Ziyao Zhou,Tiancheng Cao,Chen Shen,Jiaqi Zhang,Yuting Liu,Hen-Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种协议无关的无线定时器同步方法，利用专用无线电模式通过时隙传输精确时间戳信标，实现纳秒级同步精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网系统需要精确时间同步以支持协调感知和数据融合，但传统同步方法存在传输延迟不确定性、精度有限或双向功能受限等问题

Method: 通过解耦同步与上层数据包重传，利用硬件定时无线电事件，在专用无线电模式下使用时隙传输精确时间戳信标

Result: 1000Hz同步频率下无通信栈活动时延迟约20ns，大多数实际BLE流量条件下保持亚500ns精度；较大连接间隔、较低应用吞吐量和较高RSSI能改善同步质量

Conclusion: 该方法为资源受限物联网系统提供通用高精度同步解决方案，显著减少调度不确定性并实现纳秒级同步精度

Abstract: Accurate time synchronization is essential for Internet of Things (IoT) systems, where multiple distributed nodes must share a common time base for coordinated sensing and data fusion. However, conventional synchronization approaches suffer from nondeterministic transmission latency, limited precision, or restricted bidirectional functionality. This paper presents a protocol-independent wireless timer synchronization method that exploits radio timeslots to transmit precisely timestamped beacons in a proprietary radio mode. By decoupling synchronization from upper-layer packet retransmissions and leveraging hardware-timed radio events, the proposed approach significantly reduces scheduling uncertainty and achieves nanosecond-level synchronization accuracy. Comprehensive experiments evaluate the impacts of synchronization frequency, RSSI, BLE connection interval, and throughput on synchronization performance. The results demonstrate that an optimal synchronization frequency of 1000 Hz yields an approximately 20 ns delay in the absence of communication stack activity while maintaining sub-500 ns accuracy under most realistic BLE traffic conditions. Furthermore, larger connection intervals, lower application throughput, and higher RSSI consistently improve synchronization quality by reducing radio resource contention and packet loss. The proposed scheme provides a general and high-precision synchronization solution suitable for resource-constrained IoT systems.

</details>

### [11] [The Traveling Thief Problem with Time Windows: Benchmarks and Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06724)
*Helen Yuliana Angmalisang,Frank Neumann*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文研究带时间窗口约束的旅行小偷问题（TTP-TW），提出新启发式算法并创建基准实例，实验表明新算法优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的优化问题通常具有多个相互依赖的组件，传统优化研究往往孤立处理。旅行小偷问题（TTP）作为多组件问题已被广泛研究，但实际应用中物品收集通常受时间窗口限制，因此需要研究带时间窗口约束的TTP变体。

Method: 1）将现有TTP和带时间窗口旅行商问题（TSP-TW）的方法适配到TTP-TW；2）设计新的启发式算法；3）基于文献中现有TTP实例创建带时间窗口的TTP基准实例；4）通过实验评估不同方法的性能。

Result: 实验评估表明，新设计的启发式算法在广泛的基准实例上优于其他方法，验证了该算法对TTP-TW问题的有效性。

Conclusion: TTP-TW是现实世界应用中重要的多组件优化问题变体，新提出的启发式算法为解决该问题提供了有效方法，创建的基准实例为未来研究提供了评估基础。

Abstract: While traditional optimization problems were often studied in isolation, many real-world problems today require interdependence among multiple optimization components. The traveling thief problem (TTP) is a multi-component problem that has been widely studied in the literature. In this paper, we introduce and investigate the TTP with time window constraints which provides a TTP variant highly relevant to real-world situations where good can only be collected at given time intervals. We examine adaptions of existing approaches for TTP and the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) with time windows to this new problem and evaluate their performance. Furthermore, we provide a new heuristic approach for the TTP with time windows. To evaluate algorithms for TTP with time windows, we introduce new TTP benchmark instances with time windows based on TTP instances existing in the literature. Our experimental investigations evaluate the different approaches and show that the newly designed algorithm outperforms the other approaches on a wide range of benchmark instances.

</details>


### [12] [Block-Bench: A Framework for Controllable and Transparent Discrete Optimization Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06973)
*Furong Ye,Frank Neumann,Thomas Bäck,Niki van Stein*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出了一种构建离散优化基准的新方法，通过块函数、权重因子和邻接图精细控制问题属性，支持在目标空间和变量表示层面分析算法行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散优化基准通常缺乏对问题属性的精细控制，难以深入分析算法行为。需要一种能够支持在目标空间和变量表示层面分析算法行为的基准构建方法，特别是在大规模多模态问题中分析离散启发式算法。

Method: 基于块函数构建基准问题，每个块函数将变量子集映射到实数值。通过块函数集合、权重因子和表示块函数间依赖关系的邻接图来实例化问题。通过分析中间块值，可以在目标空间和变量表示层面分析算法行为。

Result: 该方法能够精细控制问题属性，支持在大规模多模态问题中分析离散启发式算法。框架能够分析算法在目标空间和变量表示层面的行为，为自适应性、多样性控制等研究提供启发，并支持动态算法配置和多目标优化等更广泛领域的研究。

Conclusion: 提出的基准构建方法提供了对问题属性的显式控制，增强了基准研究的实际相关性。该方法不仅支持离散算法行为的深入分析，还能启发进化算法中的自适应性、多样性控制等相关研究，并适用于更广泛的优化领域。

Abstract: We present a novel approach for constructing discrete optimization benchmarks that enables fine-grained control over problem properties, and such benchmarks can facilitate analyzing discrete algorithm behaviors. We build benchmark problems based on a set of block functions, where each block function maps a subset of variables to a real value. Problems are instantiated through a set of block functions, weight factors, and an adjacency graph representing the dependency among the block functions. Through analyzing intermediate block values, our framework allows to analyze algorithm behavior not only in the objective space but also at the level of variable representations in the obtained solutions. This capacity is particularly useful for analyzing discrete heuristics in large-scale multi-modal problems, thereby enhancing the practical relevance of benchmark studies. We demonstrate how the proposed approach can inspire the related work in self-adaptation and diversity control in evolutionary algorithms. Moreover, we explain that the proposed benchmark design enables explicit control over problem properties, supporting research in broader domains such as dynamic algorithm configuration and multi-objective optimization.

</details>


### [13] [Anytime Analysis on BinVal: Adaptive Parameters Help](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06976)
*Timo Kötzing,Jurek Sander*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 论文分析了进化算法和分布估计算法在BinVal适应度函数上的固定目标运行时间，针对优化长度为n的比特串中最重要的k位（k∈o(n)），比较了不同算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论分析主要关注找到全局最优解的期望评估次数，而本文关注进化算法和分布估计算法的"随时性能"（anytime performance），即在不同时间点的优化进展。通过分析固定目标运行时间，可以更全面地评估算法在不同优化阶段的表现。

Method: 使用BinVal作为适应度函数，分析不同算法优化比特串中最重要k位的固定目标运行时间。比较了三种算法：1）标准(1+1)EA（固定变异率1/n）；2）sig-cGA分布估计算法；3）自调整变异率的(1+1)EA。分析不仅针对固定k，而且同时适用于所有k∈o(n)。

Result: 1）标准(1+1)EA的固定目标运行时间为Θ(n log k)；2）sig-cGA的期望评估次数为Θ(k log n)；3）自调整变异率的(1+1)EA的运行时间为O(k^{1+ε})（ε>0任意小）。特别地，自调整算法的运行时间与n无关，同时适用于所有k∈o(n)，且接近已知最佳固定变异率(1+1)EA的Θ(k log k)运行时间。

Conclusion: 自调整变异率的(1+1)EA在固定目标运行时间方面表现优异，其运行时间独立于问题规模n，仅与目标精度k相关，且接近理论最优性能。这表明自调整机制能够有效适应不同的优化阶段，为进化算法设计提供了有价值的理论指导。

Abstract: While most theoretical run time analyses of discrete randomized search heuristics provide bounds on the expected number of evaluations to find the global optimum, we consider the anytime performance of evolutionary and estimation-of-distribution algorithms. For this purpose, we analyze the fixed-target run time of various algorithms using BinVal as fitness function and bound the run time to optimize the most significant $k \in o(n)$ bits of a bit string with length $n$. We analyze the run times such that they hold not only for a fixed $k$, but simultaneously for all $k \in o(n)$.
  For the standard (1+1) EA with fixed mutation rate $1/n$, we show that the fixed-target run time for all $k \in o(n)$ is in $Θ(n \log k)$. Using an EDA instead, we get an expected number of evaluations of $Θ(k \log n)$ for the sig-cGA. Replacing in the standard (1+1) EA the fixed mutation rate with a self-adjusting rate, we show that the fixed-target run time for $k \in o(n)$ and a constant $\varepsilon >0$ arbitrarily close to zero is in $\mathcal{O}\left(k^{1+\varepsilon}\right)$ for this algorithm. In particular, this run time is independent of $n$, holds simultaneously for all $k \in o(n)$, and is close to the run time of $Θ(k \log k)$ for the (1+1) EA with the best fixed mutation rate if $k$ is known.

</details>

### [14] [Code Sharing In Prediction Model Research: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06212)
*Thomas Sounack,Raffaele Giancotti,Catherine A. Gao,Lasai Barreñada,Hyeonhoon Lee,Hyung-Chul Lee,Leo Anthony Celi,Karel G. M. Moons,Gary S. Collins,Charlotta Lindvall,Tom Pollard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了预测模型研究中代码共享的现状，发现仅有12.2%的文章共享代码，且共享的代码在可重复性方面存在明显不足，为制定TRIPOD-Code扩展指南提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管分析代码对于预测模型研究的可重复性至关重要，但已发表文献中的代码可用性仍然有限。TRIPOD声明虽然为预测模型方法报告设定了标准，但未明确规范代码仓库结构和文档标准。本研究旨在量化当前代码共享实践，为制定专注于代码共享的TRIPOD-Code扩展报告指南提供依据。

Method: 对截至2025年8月11日PubMed索引的引用TRIPOD或TRIPOD+AI的文章进行范围综述，仅限于可通过PubMed Central开放获取API检索的研究。纳入开发、更新或验证多变量预测模型的研究。开发了大型语言模型辅助的流程来筛选文章并提取代码可用性声明和仓库链接。使用相同LLM根据14个预定义的可重复性相关特征评估仓库。

Result: 在3,967篇符合条件的文章中，仅12.2%包含代码共享声明。代码共享率随时间增长，2025年达到15.8%，且引用TRIPOD+AI的研究比引用TRIPOD的研究共享率更高。共享率因期刊和国家而异。仓库评估显示可重复性特征存在显著异质性：大多数仓库包含README文件（80.5%），但较少指定依赖项（37.6%；版本约束的仅21.6%）或采用模块化结构（42.4%）。

Conclusion: 预测模型研究中的代码共享仍然相对少见，即使共享的代码也往往达不到可重复使用的标准。这些发现为TRIPOD-Code扩展提供了实证基线，并强调需要超越代码可用性本身，建立更清晰的期望，包括文档、依赖项规范、许可和可执行结构等方面的标准。

Abstract: Analytical code is essential for reproducing diagnostic and prognostic prediction model research, yet code availability in the published literature remains limited. While the TRIPOD statements set standards for reporting prediction model methods, they do not define explicit standards for repository structure and documentation. This review quantifies current code-sharing practices to inform the development of TRIPOD-Code, a TRIPOD extension reporting guideline focused on code sharing.
  We conducted a scoping review of PubMed-indexed articles citing TRIPOD or TRIPOD+AI as of Aug 11, 2025, restricted to studies retrievable via the PubMed Central Open Access API. Eligible studies developed, updated, or validated multivariable prediction models. A large language model-assisted pipeline was developed to screen articles and extract code availability statements and repository links. Repositories were assessed with the same LLM against 14 predefined reproducibility-related features. Our code is made publicly available.
  Among 3,967 eligible articles, 12.2% included code sharing statements. Code sharing increased over time, reaching 15.8% in 2025, and was higher among TRIPOD+AI-citing studies than TRIPOD-citing studies. Sharing prevalence varied widely by journal and country. Repository assessment showed substantial heterogeneity in reproducibility features: most repositories contained a README file (80.5%), but fewer specified dependencies (37.6%; version-constrained 21.6%) or were modular (42.4%).
  In prediction model research, code sharing remains relatively uncommon, and when shared, often falls short of being reusable. These findings provide an empirical baseline for the TRIPOD-Code extension and underscore the need for clearer expectations beyond code availability, including documentation, dependency specification, licensing, and executable structure.

</details>


### [15] [All LCA models are wrong. Are some of them useful? Towards open computational LCA in ICT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06290)
*Vincent Corlay,David Bekri,Marie-Anne Lacroix,Maxime Pelcat,Maxime Peralta,Pierre-Yves Pichon,Leo Saillenfest,Olivier Weppe,Sebastien Rumley*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文分析了ICT系统生命周期评估中模型使用的现状问题，提出了提高评估可信度的四个关键要求和实施框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICT系统的环境影响评估严重依赖模型，但当前实践在模型构建、校准、集成和解释方面缺乏足够的严谨性，导致评估结果可信度不足。

Method: 首先回顾LCA基本原理，指出ICT LCA本质上是模型系统；通过典型模型误用案例和结构性问题分析，识别当前实践的挑战；基于分析提出可信ICT LCA的四个关键要求，并设计实施框架。

Result: 识别了数据库选择、范围不匹配、不透明聚合和模型集成等结构性问题；提出了明确模型谱系、清晰定义模型范围、端到端可追溯性和管理非过时性四个关键要求；设计了基于显式依赖图、开放版本化模型库、自动完整性约束执行和明确定义模型分类的实施框架。

Conclusion: ICT LCA需要更高水平的严谨性，提出的框架通过系统化方法解决当前实践中的问题，能够提高ICT环境影响评估的可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: Life Cycle Assessment (LCA) is increasingly used to quantify and regulate the environmental impacts of Information and Communication Technology (ICT) systems. Since direct biosphere measurements are complicated to perform, we claim that the environmental impact assessment of ICT relies heavily on models. In this paper, we first revisit the fundamentals of LCA: we emphasize that ICT LCAs effectively form systems of models, and we argue that such systems require an extra-high level of carefulness in construction, calibration, integration, and interpretation. We then document how this level of rigor is challenging to achieve with current practices. This is illustrated with emblematic examples of model misuse and an analysis of structural challenges related to database choice, scope mismatches, opaque aggregation, and model integration. From this analysis, we derive four key requirements for credible ICT LCA: explicit model lineage, clearly defined model scope, end-to-end traceability, and managed non-obsolescence. Finally, we propose a framework that operationalizes these requirements using explicit dependency graphs, an open and versioned LCA-oriented model repository, automatic enforcement of integrity constraints, and a well-defined model taxonomy.

</details>


### [16] [A Survey of Algorithm Debt in Machine and Deep Learning Systems: Definition, Smells, and Future Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06363)
*Emmanuel Iko-Ojo Simon,Chirath Hettiarachchi,Fatemeh Fard,Alex Potanin,Hanna Suominen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对算法债务(AD)进行了系统性文献综述，分析了42项研究，扩展了AD定义，识别了其存在形式、气味特征，并提出了未来研究方向，旨在提高ML/DL系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习和深度学习技术的采用引入了维护挑战，导致技术债务(TD)。算法债务(AD)是影响ML/DL系统性能和可扩展性的一种TD类型，需要系统性的理解和研究。

Method: 通过对42项主要研究进行系统性文献综述，扩展了算法债务的定义，揭示了其隐含存在形式，识别了相关的气味特征，并分析了未来研究方向。

Result: 研究明确了算法债务的概念框架，识别了其在ML/DL系统中的具体表现形式和气味特征，为后续针对性研究提供了理论基础和方向指导。

Conclusion: 该综述为算法债务研究奠定了基础，其发现将指导后续AD专项研究，有助于提高ML/DL系统的可靠性和可维护性，减少技术债务对系统性能的影响。

Abstract: The adoption of Machine and Deep Learning (ML/DL) technologies introduces maintenance challenges, leading to Technical Debt (TD). Algorithm Debt (AD) is a TD type that impacts the performance and scalability of ML/DL systems. A review of 42 primary studies expanded AD's definition, uncovered its implicit presence, identified its smells, and highlighted future directions. These findings will guide an AD-focused study, enhancing the reliability of ML/DL systems.

</details>


### [17] [ExplainFuzz: Explainable and Constraint-Conditioned Test Generation with Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06559)
*Annaëlle Baiget,Jaron Maene,Seongmin Lee,Benjie Wang,Guy Van den Broeck,Miryung Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ExplainFuzz：基于概率电路（PCs）的可解释、可控的语法感知测试生成框架，通过学习和查询结构化分布来生成更真实、更符合约束的测试输入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试输入生成方法（包括基于语法的模糊测试、概率上下文无关文法和大型语言模型）存在关键限制：经常产生不符合现实数据分布的畸形输入，难以捕捉上下文相关的概率依赖关系，且缺乏可解释性

Method: ExplainFuzz利用概率电路（PCs）学习和查询基于语法的测试输入的结构化分布。从上下文无关文法开始，编译语法感知的PC并在现有输入上训练，然后通过采样生成新输入。利用PC的条件化能力整合测试特定约束，实现受约束的概率采样

Result: ExplainFuzz显著提高了生成输入的连贯性和真实性，相比pCFGs、语法无关PCs和LLMs实现了显著困惑度降低。通过条件化能力，显著增强了满足用户约束的输入多样性。相比语法感知变异模糊测试，在SQL中将bug触发率从35%提升到63%，在XML中从10%提升到100%

Conclusion: 概率电路有潜力作为语法感知、可控测试生成的基础，能够捕捉上下文相关的概率依赖关系，展示了学习输入分布相比变异模糊测试的优势，后者通常局限于种子输入的局部邻域探索

Abstract: Understanding and explaining the structure of generated test inputs is essential for effective software testing and debugging. Existing approaches--including grammar-based fuzzers, probabilistic Context-Free Grammars (pCFGs), and Large Language Models (LLMs)--suffer from critical limitations. They frequently produce ill-formed inputs that fail to reflect realistic data distributions, struggle to capture context-sensitive probabilistic dependencies, and lack explainability. We introduce ExplainFuzz, a test generation framework that leverages Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) to learn and query structured distributions over grammar-based test inputs interpretably and controllably. Starting from a Context-Free Grammar (CFG), ExplainFuzz compiles a grammar-aware PC and trains it on existing inputs. New inputs are then generated via sampling. ExplainFuzz utilizes the conditioning capability of PCs to incorporate test-specific constraints (e.g., a query must have GROUP BY), enabling constrained probabilistic sampling to generate inputs satisfying grammar and user-provided constraints.
  Our results show that ExplainFuzz improves the coherence and realism of generated inputs, achieving significant perplexity reduction compared to pCFGs, grammar-unaware PCs, and LLMs. By leveraging its native conditioning capability, ExplainFuzz significantly enhances the diversity of inputs that satisfy a user-provided constraint. Compared to grammar-aware mutational fuzzing, ExplainFuzz increases bug-triggering rates from 35% to 63% in SQL and from 10% to 100% in XML. These results demonstrate the power of a learned input distribution over mutational fuzzing, which is often limited to exploring the local neighborhood of seed inputs. These capabilities highlight the potential of PCs to serve as a foundation for grammar-aware, controllable test generation that captures context-sensitive, probabilistic dependencies.

</details>


### [18] [It's Not About Whom You Train: An Analysis of Corporate Education in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06580)
*Rodrigo Siqueira,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究调查了巴西软件工程专业人员对培训质量与效果感知的影响因素，发现个人背景变量影响有限，而职业轨迹变量和培训自愿性才是关键决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业教育是软件行业的战略投资，但不同专业背景人员如何感知这些培训计划尚不清楚。研究旨在探究社会人口统计学和职业变量是否影响软件工程领域企业培训的质量和效果感知。

Method: 采用非参数显著性检验分析282名巴西专业人员的调查数据，将27个感知项目与9个社会人口统计学变量（性别、年龄、教育水平、所在州、经验、职业级别、公司规模、工作领域和参与性质）进行交叉分析，共测试243种组合。

Result: 243个测试组合中仅35个显示统计显著性。培训强制性是主导因素，影响27个项目中的24个。工作经验长度呈现非线性描述模式，3-6年经验区间为低参与度区域。不同工作领域显示高级技术角色在软技能培训方面存在显著差距。个人背景变量和公司规模未产生相关显著差异。

Conclusion: 个人背景变量不决定培训质量和效果感知，而职业轨迹变量（经验、级别、工作领域）产生局部差异。参与自愿性仍是决定性因素，与文献一致。女性占23%的样本中未发现性别差异，表明障碍存在于培训前的获取和代表性阶段，而非学习体验过程中。

Abstract: Context: Corporate education is a strategic investment in the software industry, but little is known about how different professional profiles perceive these initiatives. Objective: To investigate whether sociodemographic and professional variables influence the perception of quality and effectiveness of corporate training in Software Engineering (SE). Method: Non-parametric significance tests were applied to data from a survey with 282 Brazilian professionals, crossing 27 perception items with 9 sociodemographic variables (gender, age, education level, state, experience, professional level, company size, area of work, and nature of participation), totaling 243 combinations. Results: Of the 243 combinations tested, only 35 showed statistical significance. Training mandatoriness was the dominant factor, affecting 24 of 27 items. Length of experience revealed a non-linear descriptive pattern with a low-engagement zone between 3 and 6 years. Differences by area of work indicated an expressive gap in soft skills training for advanced technical roles. Personal profile variables and company size produced no relevant significant differences. Conclusion: Personal profile variables do not determine the perception of quality and effectiveness, while professional trajectory variables (experience, level, area of work) produce localized differences. The voluntariness of participation remains a determining factor, in line with the literature. The absence of gender differences in a sample with 23\% women suggests that barriers operate before training, in access and representation, not during the learning experience.

</details>


### [19] [Stabilization Without Simplification: A Two-Dimensional Model of Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06709)
*Masaru Furukawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出基于图的离散时间概率框架，将软件演化中的结构负担与不确定性分离，定义了预期努力为负担、努力方差为不确定性，解释了软件系统如何在不简化结构的情况下变得更可预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统在规模、复杂性和相互依赖性方面持续增长，但许多大型系统仍能保持稳定，这与结构负担持续存在形成矛盾。这表明一维的软件演化观点存在局限性，需要新的理论框架来解释"稳定而不简化"的现象。

Method: 引入基于图的离散时间概率框架，将变更努力建模为随机变量，由变更实体的依赖邻域和残差变异性决定。定义结构负担为预期努力，不确定性为努力方差。在结构负载非递减平均、结构正则化、过程稳定化和协方差控制等明确假设下，分析不确定性减少而结构负担不减少的机制。

Result: 在特定假设条件下，存在一种机制使得不确定性减少而结构负担不减少，这形式化了"稳定而不简化"的现象。该框架为软件系统如何在不简化结构的情况下变得更可预测提供了最小理论解释。

Conclusion: 提出的框架分离了结构负担与不确定性，为理解软件演化中的"稳定而不简化"现象提供了理论基础，并为软件演化的进一步理论和实证研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Software systems are widely observed to grow in size, complexity, and interdependence over time, yet many large-scale systems remain stable despite persistent structural burden. This apparent tension suggests a limitation in one-dimensional views of software evolution. This paper introduces a graph-based, discrete-time probabilistic framework that separates structural burden from uncertainty. Change effort is modeled as a stochastic variable determined by the dependency neighborhood of the changed entity and by residual variability. Within this framework, burden is defined as expected effort and uncertainty as variance of effort. We show that, under explicit assumptions on non-decreasing average structural load, structural regularization, process stabilization, and covariance control, there exists a regime in which uncertainty decreases while structural burden does not. This regime formalizes the phenomenon of stabilization without simplification. The proposed framework provides a minimal theoretical explanation for how software systems can become more predictable over time without necessarily becoming structurally simpler, and offers a foundation for further theoretical and empirical studies of software evolution.

</details>


### [20] [Fine-grained Approaches for Confidence Calibration of LLMs in Automated Code Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06723)
*Hong Yi Lin,Chunhua Liu,Haoyu Gao,Patanamon Thongtanunam,Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出针对自动代码修订任务的细粒度置信度校准方法，通过局部Platt缩放改进LLM输出的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在软件工程中广泛使用但存在输出错误问题，传统的全局置信度校准方法在自动代码修订任务中效果不佳，需要针对这类任务的细粒度特性设计更好的校准方案

Method: 提出局部Platt缩放方法，分别应用于三种不同的细粒度置信度分数，针对自动代码修订任务中的局部编辑决策进行校准

Result: 细粒度置信度分数在3个不同任务和14个不同规模模型上均能获得更低的校准误差，覆盖更广的概率区间，结合全局Platt缩放效果更佳

Conclusion: 细粒度置信度校准为自动代码修订任务提供了实用的解决方案，使不完美模型的使用更加可靠和高效

Abstract: In today's AI-assisted software engineering landscape, developers increasingly depend on LLMs that are highly capable, yet inherently imperfect. The tendency of these models to produce incorrect outputs can reduce developer productivity. To this end, a canonical mitigation method is to provide calibrated confidence scores that faithfully reflect their likelihood of correctness at the instance-level. Such information allows users to make immediate decisions regarding output acceptance, abstain error-prone outputs, and better align their expectations with the model's capabilities. Since post-trained LLMs do not inherently produce well-calibrated confidence scores, researchers have developed post-hoc calibration methods, with global Platt-scaling of sequence-level confidence scores proving effective in many generative software engineering tasks but remaining unreliable or unexplored for automated code revision (ACR) tasks such as program repair, vulnerability repair, and code refinement. We hypothesise that the coarse-grained nature of this conventional method makes it ill-suited for ACR tasks, where correctness is often determined by local edit decisions and miscalibration can be sample-dependent, thereby motivating fine-grained confidence calibration. To address this, our study proposes local Platt-scaling applied separately to three different fine-grained confidence scores. Through experiments across 3 separate tasks and correctness metrics, as well as 14 different models of various sizes, we find that fine-grained confidence scores consistently achieve lower calibration error across a broader range of probability intervals, and this effect is further amplified when global Platt-scaling is applied. Our proposed approaches offer a practical solution to eliciting well-calibrated confidence scores, enabling more trustworthy and streamlined usage of imperfect models in ACR tasks.

</details>


### [21] [Babbling Suppression: Making LLMs Greener One Token at a Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06755)
*Lola Solovyeva,Fernando Castor*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出Babbling Suppression方法，通过测试执行来抑制LLM代码生成中的冗余输出，显著降低能耗而不影响准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成中常产生冗余输出（"babbling"），导致认知、经济和能源成本增加，需要一种实用且模型无关的方法来减少不必要输出

Method: 引入Babbling Suppression方法，将测试执行集成到LLM生成过程中，评估中间输出并在解决方案通过所有测试时终止生成，防止过度令牌生成

Result: BS显著降低能耗：Python最高65%，Java最高62%；40个模型-基准对中，29个显示平均能耗降低，22个降低超过20%；35个对中生成令牌数减少

Conclusion: BS能使AI辅助编程更高效和可持续，降低能耗和开发者的认知负担，其模型无关设计便于集成，具有广泛适用性

Abstract: Context: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in modern software development, aiding in code generation, code completion, and refactoring through AI-powered assistants. While they accelerate development workflows, they often produce extraneous output, referred to as "babbling", which incurs additional cognitive, economic, and energy costs. Objective: This work investigates the problem of babbling in LLM-based code generation and proposes a practical, model-agnostic approach to reduce unnecessary output without compromising solution accuracy. Method: We introduce Babbling Suppression (BS), a method that integrates test execution into the LLM generation process by evaluating intermediate outputs and terminating generation once a solution passes all tests. This prevents excessive token generation while having no impact on model accuracy. An empirical study was conducted across two Python and two Java benchmarks, targeting four 3-4B parameter models and six 6-7B parameter models. Results: Our findings show that babbling occurs across all tested models, with higher frequency in Java than in Python. Applying BS significantly reduces energy consumption by up to 65% for Python and 62% for Java in models prone to babbling. Across 40 model-benchmark pairs, 29 showed reduced mean energy consumption, with reductions exceeding 20% in 22 cases. Generated token count decreased in 35 pairs, while the GPU energy-per-token overhead of BS remained below 10% for 26 pairs, decreased for 2, and reached a maximum of 24%, yielding net energy savings in most cases. Implications: BS can make AI-assisted programming more efficient and sustainable by reducing energy consumption and minimizing cognitive effort by developers. Its model-agnostic design allows easy integration, suggesting broad applicability.

</details>


### [22] [Improving Random Testing via LLM-powered UI Tarpit Escaping for Mobile Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06763)
*Mengqian Xu,Yiheng Xiong,Le Chang,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan,Weikai Miao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LLM赋能的随机GUI测试方法，通过监控UI相似性识别测试陷阱，利用LLM建议事件来逃逸，显著提升移动应用测试覆盖率和缺陷发现能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机GUI测试在移动应用测试中广泛应用，但存在UI探索陷阱问题，测试容易被困在局部UI区域，限制了测试覆盖率和缺陷发现效果

Method: 提出LLM赋能的随机GUI测试混合方法：1) 监控UI相似性识别测试陷阱；2) 查询LLM获取逃逸测试陷阱的有前景事件；3) 在Monkey和Droidbot两种AIG工具上实现HybridMonkey和HybridDroidbot

Result: 在12个流行真实应用上评估：HybridMonkey平均覆盖率提升54.8%，HybridDroidbot提升44.8%，均优于所有基线；共发现75个独特缺陷，其中34个是先前未知的，26个已确认并修复；在微信应用上，HybridMonkey相比随机测试获得更高活动覆盖率和更多缺陷

Conclusion: LLM赋能的随机GUI测试能有效缓解UI测试陷阱问题，显著提升移动应用测试的覆盖率和缺陷发现能力，在实际工业级应用中验证了其有效性

Abstract: Random GUI testing is a widely-used technique for testing mobile apps. However, its effectiveness is limited by the notorious issue -- UI exploration tarpits, where the exploration is trapped in local UI regions, thus impeding test coverage and bug discovery. In this experience paper, we introduce LLM-powered random GUI Testing, a novel hybrid testing approach to mitigating UI tarpits during random testing. Our approach monitors UI similarity to identify tarpits and query LLMs to suggest promising events for escaping the encountered tarpits. We implement our approach on top of two different automated input generation (AIG) tools for mobile apps: (1) HybridMonkey upon Monkey, a state-of-the-practice tool; and (2) HybridDroidbot upon Droidbot, a state-of-the-art tool. We evaluated them on 12 popular, real-world apps. The results show that HybridMonkey and HybridDroidbot outperform all baselines, achieving average coverage improvements of 54.8% and 44.8%, respectively, and detecting the highest number of unique crashes. In total, we found 75 unique bugs, including 34 previously unknown bugs. To date, 26 bugs have been confirmed and fixed. We also applied HybridMonkey on WeChat, a popular industrial app with billions of monthly active users. HybridMonkey achieved higher activity coverage and found more bugs than random testing.

</details>


### [23] [Evaluating Repository-level Software Documentation via Question Answering and Feature-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06793)
*Xinchen Wang,Ruida Hu,Cuiyun Gao,Pengfei Gao,Chao Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWD-Bench是一个用于评估仓库级软件文档质量的新型基准测试，通过文档驱动开发理念，使用功能驱动的问答任务来评估文档质量，而非直接评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件文档评估基准存在两个关键局限：1）缺乏整体仓库级评估；2）依赖不可靠的评估策略（如LLM-as-a-judge），存在标准模糊和仓库级知识有限的问题。

Method: 采用文档驱动开发理念，通过评估LLM使用文档理解和实现功能的能力来衡量文档质量。构建包含三个相互关联的问答任务：功能检测、功能定位和功能完成。通过挖掘高质量Pull Requests并丰富仓库级上下文，构建了包含4,170个条目的基准数据集。

Result: 实验揭示了当前文档生成方法的局限性，并显示源代码提供补充价值。最佳性能方法生成的文档将SWE-Agent的问题解决率提高了20.00%，证明了高质量文档在支持文档驱动开发中的实际价值。

Conclusion: SWD-Bench为仓库级软件文档评估提供了更可靠和实用的基准，通过功能驱动的问答任务克服了现有评估方法的局限性，展示了高质量文档对AI辅助开发工具的实际提升效果。

Abstract: Software documentation is crucial for repository comprehension. While Large Language Models (LLMs) advance documentation generation from code snippets to entire repositories, existing benchmarks have two key limitations: (1) they lack a holistic, repository-level assessment, and (2) they rely on unreliable evaluation strategies, such as LLM-as-a-judge, which suffers from vague criteria and limited repository-level knowledge. To address these issues, we introduce SWD-Bench, a novel benchmark for evaluating repository-level software documentation. Inspired by documentation-driven development, our strategy evaluates documentation quality by assessing an LLM's ability to understand and implement functionalities using the documentation, rather than by directly scoring it. This is measured through function-driven Question Answering (QA) tasks. SWD-Bench comprises three interconnected QA tasks: (1) Functionality Detection, to determine if a functionality is described; (2) Functionality Localization, to evaluate the accuracy of locating related files; and (3) Functionality Completion, to measure the comprehensiveness of implementation details. We construct the benchmark, containing 4,170 entries, by mining high-quality Pull Requests and enriching them with repository-level context. Experiments reveal limitations in current documentation generation methods and show that source code provides complementary value. Notably, documentation from the best-performing method improves the issue-solving rate of SWE-Agent by 20.00%, which demonstrates the practical value of high-quality documentation in supporting documentation-driven development.

</details>


### [24] [An empirical study of LoRA-based fine-tuning of large language models for automated test case generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06946)
*Milad Moradi,Ke Yan,David Colwell,Rhona Asgari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对使用LoRA进行参数高效微调从自然语言需求生成测试用例进行了全面实证研究，评估了开源和专有模型，发现微调后8B开源模型性能可媲美未微调的GPT-4.1模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言需求自动生成测试用例面临需求歧义性和需要生成结构化可执行测试工件的挑战。虽然LLM在该任务上显示出潜力，但其效果依赖于任务特定适应和高效微调策略。需要研究参数高效微调方法（特别是LoRA）在此任务上的有效性。

Method: 采用统一实验流程评估多个LLM家族（包括开源和专有模型）。系统探索LoRA关键超参数（秩、缩放因子、dropout）对下游性能的影响。提出基于GPT-4o的自动化评估框架，从九个质量维度评估生成的测试用例。

Result: LoRA微调显著提升了所有开源模型的性能，其中Ministral-8B表现最佳。微调后的8B开源模型性能可媲美未微调的GPT-4.1模型。GPT-4.1模型整体性能最高，但微调后专有与开源模型间的性能差距显著缩小。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合精心设计的微调方法，成本效益高、可本地部署的开源模型可作为专有系统的可行替代方案。研究结果为自动测试生成的模型选择、微调策略和评估方法提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Automated test case generation from natural language requirements remains a challenging problem in software engineering due to the ambiguity of requirements and the need to produce structured, executable test artifacts. Recent advances in LLMs have shown promise in addressing this task; however, their effectiveness depends on task-specific adaptation and efficient fine-tuning strategies. In this paper, we present a comprehensive empirical study on the use of parameter-efficient fine-tuning, specifically LoRA, for requirement-based test case generation. We evaluate multiple LLM families, including open-source and proprietary models, under a unified experimental pipeline. The study systematically explores the impact of key LoRA hyperparameters, including rank, scaling factor, and dropout, on downstream performance. We propose an automated evaluation framework based on GPT-4o, which assesses generated test cases across nine quality dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that LoRA-based fine-tuning significantly improves the performance of all open-source models, with Ministral-8B achieving the best results among them. Furthermore, we show that a fine-tuned 8B open-source model can achieve performance comparable to pre-fine-tuned GPT-4.1 models, highlighting the effectiveness of parameter-efficient adaptation. While GPT-4.1 models achieve the highest overall performance, the performance gap between proprietary and open-source models is substantially reduced after fine-tuning. These findings provide important insights into model selection, fine-tuning strategies, and evaluation methods for automated test generation. In particular, they demonstrate that cost-efficient, locally deployable open-source models can serve as viable alternatives to proprietary systems when combined with well-designed fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [25] [Assessing REST API Test Generation Strategies with Log Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07073)
*Nana Reinikainen,Mika Mäntylä,Yuqing Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出三种日志覆盖率指标评估黑盒REST API测试效果，比较进化计算、LLM和人工测试策略在OAuth2微服务上的表现，发现Claude Opus表现最佳且不同策略覆盖的运行时行为具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑盒测试环境中评估REST API测试有效性面临挑战，因为无法获取源代码覆盖率指标且技术栈多样化。需要新的评估指标来量化测试质量。

Method: 提出三种日志覆盖率指标（平均、最小、最大）来处理多次运行中的多样化测试生成结果和运行时行为。使用这些指标在Light-OAuth2授权微服务系统上实证评估三种REST API测试生成策略：进化计算（EvoMaster v5.0.2）、LLM（Claude Opus 4.6和GPT-5.2-Codex）以及人工编写的Locust负载测试。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6测试平均比人工测试多发现28.4%的唯一日志模板，而EvoMaster和GPT-5.2-Codex分别少发现26.1%和38.6%。组合测试策略显示互补性：人工测试与Claude Opus组合使总日志覆盖率分别提高78.4%和38.9%；Locust测试与EvoMaster组合提高30.7%和76.9%；与GPT-5.2-Codex组合提高26.1%和105.6%。

Conclusion: 不同测试生成策略主要覆盖不同的运行时行为，具有显著互补性。Claude Opus在黑盒REST API测试中表现最佳。未来工作将扩展到多个系统进行研究。

Abstract: Assessing the effectiveness of REST API tests in black-box settings can be challenging due to the lack of access to source code coverage metrics and polyglot tech stack. We propose three metrics for capturing average, minimum, and maximum log coverage to handle the diverse test generation results and runtime behaviors over multiple runs. Using log coverage, we empirically evaluate three REST API test generation strategies, Evolutionary computing (EvoMaster v5.0.2), LLMs (Claude Opus 4.6 and GPT-5.2-Codex), and human-written Locust load tests, on Light-OAuth2 authorization microservice system. On average, Claude Opus 4.6 tests uncover 28.4% more unique log templates than human-written tests, whereas EvoMaster and GPT-5.2-Codex find 26.1% and 38.6% fewer, respectively. Next, we analyze combined log coverage to assess complementarity between strategies. Combining human-written tests with Claude Opus 4.6 tests increases total observed log coverage by 78.4% and 38.9% in human-written and Claude tests respectively. When combining Locust tests with EvoMaster the same increases are 30.7% and 76.9% and when using GPT-5.2-Codex 26.1% and 105.6%. This means that the generation strategies exercise largely distinct runtime behaviors. Our future work includes extending our study to multiple systems.

</details>


### [26] [Compact Constraint Encoding for LLM Code Generation: An Empirical Study of Token Economics and Constraint Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07192)
*Hanzhang Tang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 紧凑约束头能显著减少提示令牌消耗（约71%），但对约束满足率无显著影响；约束设计和任务领域比编码形式对合规性影响更大


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究是否能用紧凑、结构化的约束头来减少代码生成中LLM提示的令牌消耗，同时不降低约束合规性，以优化工程效率

Method: 通过6轮实验，涵盖11个模型、16个基准任务和830多次LLM调用，比较三种编码形式和四种传播模式，使用统计方法分析约束满足率差异

Result: 紧凑头将约束部分令牌减少约71%，完整提示令牌减少25-30%，但约束满足率无统计学显著差异；约束类型和任务领域是合规性方差的主要来源

Conclusion: 紧凑约束编码的主要优势是令牌减少而非合规性提升；工程努力应更关注约束设计而非提示格式化，因为约束理解和执行存在差距

Abstract: LLMs used for code generation are typically guided by engineering constraints--technology choices, dependency restrictions, and architectural patterns--expressed in verbose natural language. We investigate whether compact, structured constraint headers can reduce prompt token consumption without degrading constraint compliance.
  Across six experimental rounds spanning 11 models, 16 benchmark tasks, and over 830 LLM invocations, we find that compact headers reduce constraint-portion tokens by approximately 71% and full-prompt tokens by 25--30%, replicated across three independent rounds. However, we detect no statistically significant differences in constraint satisfaction rate (CSR) across three encoding forms or four propagation modes; observed effect sizes are negligible (Cliff's $δ$ < 0.01, 95% CI spanning $\pm$2.6 percentage points). This null pattern holds across two models from different capability tiers. A supplementary experiment with four non-CSS tasks provides additional cross-domain support for the encoding null result.
  The largest observed sources of compliance variance are constraint type ($Δ$ = 9 percentage points between normal and counter-intuitive constraints) and task domain: counter-intuitive constraints opposing model defaults fail at 10--100%, while conventional constraints achieve 99%+ compliance regardless of encoding. Model self-assessments systematically overestimate compliance relative to rule-based scoring, revealing a gap between constraint understanding and execution. Under the tested conditions, the primary benefit of compact constraint encoding is token reduction rather than compliance improvement, and engineering effort toward compliance is better directed at constraint design than prompt formatting.

</details>


### [27] [ReCodeAgent: A Multi-Agent Workflow for Language-agnostic Translation and Validation of Large-scale Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07341)
*Ali Reza Ibrahimzada,Brandon Paulsen,Daniel Kroening,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReCodeAgent：首个完全自主的多智能体方法，用于语言无关的仓库级代码翻译和验证，显著超越现有技术


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码翻译和验证技术通常只针对单一编程语言对，需要大量工程适配工作。编程智能体可以实现语言无关性，但现有方法尚未提供完全自主的智能体方法用于大规模程序的仓库级代码翻译和验证。

Method: 提出ReCodeAgent，一种自主的多智能体方法，用户只需提供源语言项目和目标语言，系统即可自动翻译和验证整个仓库。该方法利用智能体跨多种语言合成代码，并自主使用特定于每种语言的现有分析工具。

Result: 在118个真实项目（平均1975行代码，43个翻译单元）上评估，覆盖6种编程语言和4个语言对。ReCodeAgent在翻译正确性上持续优于现有技术，测试通过率提高60.8%，平均成本15.3美元。多智能体架构相比单智能体使测试通过率下降40.4%，轨迹长度增加28%。

Conclusion: ReCodeAgent是首个实现跨多种编程语言高翻译成功率的完全自主智能体方法，证明了多智能体架构在仓库级代码翻译和验证任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Most repository-level code translation and validation techniques have been evaluated on a single source-target programming language (PL) pair, owing to the complex engineering effort required to adapt new PL pairs. Programming agents can enable PL-agnosticism in repository-level code translation and validation: they can synthesize code across many PLs and autonomously use existing tools specific to each PL's analysis. However, state-of-the-art has yet to offer a fully autonomous agentic approach for repository-level code translation and validation of large-scale programs. This paper proposes ReCodeAgent, an autonomous multi-agent approach for language-agnostic repository-level code translation and validation. Users only need to provide the project in the source PL and specify the target PL for ReCodeAgent to automatically translate and validate the entire repository. ReCodeAgent is the first technique to achieve high translation success rates across many PLs.
  We compare the effectiveness of ReCodeAgent with four alternative neuro-symbolic and agentic approaches to translate 118 real-world projects, with 1,975 LoC and 43 translation units for each project, on average. The projects cover 6 PLs (C, Go, Java, JavaScript, Python, and Rust) and 4 PL pairs (C-Rust, Go-Rust, Java-Python, Python-JavaScript). Our results demonstrate that ReCodeAgent consistently outperforms prior techniques on translation correctness, improving test pass rate by 60.8% on ground-truth tests, with an average cost of $15.3. We also perform process-centric analysis of ReCodeAgent trajectories to confirm its procedural efficiency. Finally, we investigate how the design choices (a multi-agent vs. single-agent architecture) influence ReCodeAgent performance: on average, the test pass rate drops by 40.4%, and trajectories become 28% longer and persistently inefficient.

</details>
