### [65] [Predictive Representations for Skill Transfer in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07016)
*Ruben Vereecken,Luke Dickens,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态抽象的强化学习迁移新方法——结果预测状态表示（OPSRs），通过预测环境结果的任务无关紧凑观察来构建智能体中心的状态抽象，并引入基于OPSR的技能实现跨任务知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习规模化面临的关键挑战是泛化学习行为。如果智能体无法迁移已获得的知识，则每个任务都必须从头开始学习。本文旨在通过状态抽象开发新的迁移形式化方法，实现跨任务的知识重用。

Method: 提出结果预测状态表示（OPSRs）作为智能体中心、任务无关的状态抽象，基于对环境结果（任务无关的紧凑观察）的预测构建。进一步引入基于OPSR的技能（基于选项的抽象动作），这些技能作为状态抽象的结果可以在任务间重用。方法包括从演示中学习OPSR技能，并在新任务中直接应用这些技能。

Result: 理论和实证研究表明，OPSRs具有有限但最优的迁移潜力。通过引入OPSR技能克服了这一权衡限制。在系列实证研究中，从演示中学习的OPSR技能显著加速了全新未见任务的学习过程，且无需任何预处理。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架是强化学习迁移领域的有前景进展，特别是通过结合状态和动作抽象实现迁移。OPSR技能能够有效促进跨任务知识重用，加速新任务学习。

Abstract: A key challenge in scaling up Reinforcement Learning is generalizing learned behaviour. Without the ability to carry forward acquired knowledge an agent is doomed to learn each task from scratch. In this paper we develop a new formalism for transfer by virtue of state abstraction. Based on task-independent, compact observations (outcomes) of the environment, we introduce Outcome-Predictive State Representations (OPSRs), agent-centered and task-independent abstractions that are made up of predictions of outcomes. We show formally and empirically that they have the potential for optimal but limited transfer, then overcome this trade-off by introducing OPSR-based skills, i.e. abstract actions (based on options) that can be reused between tasks as a result of state abstraction. In a series of empirical studies, we learn OPSR-based skills from demonstrations and show how they speed up learning considerably in entirely new and unseen tasks without any pre-processing. We believe that the framework introduced in this work is a promising step towards transfer in RL in general, and towards transfer through combining state and action abstraction specifically.

</details>


### [66] [Learning to Query History: Nonstationary Classification via Learned Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07027)
*Jimmy Gammell,Bishal Thapaliya,Yoon Jung,Riyasat Ohib,Bilel Fehri,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种处理非平稳分类问题的方法：将分类任务重构为时间序列预测，通过检索历史标记样本来增强分类器对分布漂移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际分类场景中普遍存在非平稳性，导致已部署模型即使对训练时的留出集泛化良好，在实际应用中仍表现不佳。需要解决分布漂移问题。

Method: 将非平稳分类重构为时间序列预测：分类器不仅基于当前输入，还基于历史标记样本序列。引入学习的离散检索机制，通过输入相关查询采样相关历史样本，使用基于分数的梯度估计器与分类器端到端训练。允许历史数据全集在训练和部署期间保留在任意文件系统中。

Result: 在合成基准和Amazon Reviews '23（电子产品类别）上的实验表明，相比标准分类器，该方法对分布漂移具有更好的鲁棒性，且VRAM随历史数据序列长度增加可预测地扩展。

Conclusion: 通过将非平稳分类重构为时间序列预测并引入可扩展的检索机制，能够有效处理分布漂移问题，提高模型在实际部署中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Nonstationarity is ubiquitous in practical classification settings, leading deployed models to perform poorly even when they generalize well to holdout sets available at training time. We address this by reframing nonstationary classification as time series prediction: rather than predicting from the current input alone, we condition the classifier on a sequence of historical labeled examples that extends beyond the training cutoff. To scale to large sequences, we introduce a learned discrete retrieval mechanism that samples relevant historical examples via input-dependent queries, trained end-to-end with the classifier using a score-based gradient estimator. This enables the full corpus of historical data to remain on an arbitrary filesystem during training and deployment. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and Amazon Reviews '23 (electronics category) show improved robustness to distribution shift compared to standard classifiers, with VRAM scaling predictably as the length of the historical data sequence increases.

</details>


### [67] [MoE Routing Testbed: Studying Expert Specialization and Routing Behavior at Small Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07030)
*Tobias Falke,Nicolas Anastassacos,Samson Tan,Chankrisna Richy Meas,Chandana Satya Prakash,Nitesh Sekhar,M Saiful Bari,Krishna Kompella,Gamaleldin F. Elsayed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoE路由测试平台，通过可区分域的数据混合和理想路由参考，在小规模下量化评估专家专业化，发现平衡范围是关键因素


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家架构在大型语言模型中日益流行，但存在路由复杂性和训练挑战。现有方法缺乏评估专家专业化的标准指标，且小规模性能不能反映大规模行为

Method: 提出MoE路由测试平台：1) 使用具有明显可区分域的数据混合；2) 基于这些域提供理想路由的参考路由器；3) 建立明确的性能上界用于比较；4) 实现专家专业化的量化测量

Result: 通过测试平台比较多种MoE路由方法，发现平衡范围是允许专家专业化同时保持高专家利用率的关键因素。这一观察在35倍更大的模型中得到了验证

Conclusion: MoE路由测试平台为评估路由动态提供了有效框架，揭示了平衡范围对专家专业化的重要性，且该发现具有可扩展性

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly popular for frontier large language models (LLM) but they introduce training challenges due to routing complexity. Fully leveraging parameters of an MoE model requires all experts to be well-trained and to specialize in non-redundant ways. Assessing this, however, is complicated due to lack of established metrics and, importantly, many routing techniques exhibit similar performance at smaller sizes, which is often not reflective of their behavior at large scale. To address this challenge, we propose the MoE Routing Testbed, a setup that gives clearer visibility into routing dynamics at small scale while using realistic data. The testbed pairs a data mix with clearly distinguishable domains with a reference router that prescribes ideal routing based on these domains, providing a well-defined upper bound for comparison. This enables quantifiable measurement of expert specialization. To demonstrate the value of the testbed, we compare various MoE routing approaches and show that balancing scope is the crucial factor that allows specialization while maintaining high expert utilization. We confirm that this observation generalizes to models 35x larger.

</details>


### [68] [Epistemic Robust Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07072)
*Abhilash Reddy Chenreddy,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个离线强化学习框架，用紧凑的不确定性集合替代离散集成，解决数据覆盖不足导致的认知不确定性问题，并在风险敏感行为策略下展示更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习面临的主要挑战是认知不确定性，特别是当行为策略系统性地避免某些动作时，会导致价值估计不准确和不可靠的泛化。现有的集成方法（如SAC-N）虽然通过集成最小值保守估计Q值，但需要大型集成且经常混淆认知不确定性和偶然不确定性。

Method: 提出一个统一且可泛化的框架，用紧凑的Q值不确定性集合替代离散集成。进一步引入基于Epinet的模型，直接塑造不确定性集合以在鲁棒贝尔曼目标下优化累积奖励，而不依赖集成。

Result: 引入了一个用于评估风险敏感行为策略下离线RL算法的基准测试，并在表格和连续状态域中证明该方法相比基于集成的基线方法实现了更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出的不确定性集合框架有效解决了离线强化学习中的认知不确定性问题，相比传统集成方法具有更好的性能和泛化能力，特别是在风险敏感行为策略场景下。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning learns policies from fixed datasets without further environment interaction. A key challenge in this setting is epistemic uncertainty, arising from limited or biased data coverage, particularly when the behavior policy systematically avoids certain actions. This can lead to inaccurate value estimates and unreliable generalization. Ensemble-based methods like SAC-N mitigate this by conservatively estimating Q-values using the ensemble minimum, but they require large ensembles and often conflate epistemic with aleatoric uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose a unified and generalizable framework that replaces discrete ensembles with compact uncertainty sets over Q-values. %We further introduce an Epinet based model that directly shapes the uncertainty sets to optimize the cumulative reward under the robust Bellman objective without relying on ensembles. We also introduce a benchmark for evaluating offline RL algorithms under risk-sensitive behavior policies, and demonstrate that our method achieves improved robustness and generalization over ensemble-based baselines across both tabular and continuous state domains.

</details>


### [69] [Mining Electronic Health Records to Investigate Effectiveness of Ensemble Deep Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07085)
*Manar D. Samad,Yina Hou,Shrabani Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种集成深度聚类方法，通过聚合多个嵌入维度的聚类分配来改善电子健康记录数据中的患者聚类性能，在传统聚类和深度聚类方法中取得了最佳综合表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中患者聚类和疾病亚型识别对阐明病理生理学和辅助临床决策至关重要。当前医疗信息学中的聚类仍主要基于传统方法（如K-means），在应用于自编码器学习的嵌入表示时效果有限。深度学习方法主要针对图像聚类设计，与表格型EHR数据存在显著差异。

Method: 提出集成深度聚类方法：1）采用集成嵌入策略，聚合从多个嵌入维度获得的聚类分配，而非依赖单一固定嵌入空间；2）将传统聚类与深度聚类结合在新型集成框架中；3）使用All of Us研究计划的真实EHR数据，在心力衰竭患者队列中评估14种不同聚类方法。

Result: 提出的集成嵌入深度聚类方法在14种不同聚类方法和多个患者队列中获得了最佳综合性能排名。传统聚类方法表现稳健，而集成框架结合传统和深度聚类的方法优于单一方法。研究强调了基于生物性别特异性聚类EHR数据的重要性。

Conclusion: 集成嵌入深度聚类方法在EHR数据聚类中表现出色，强调了结合传统和深度聚类方法的优势。研究结果支持在医疗信息学中采用集成方法，并凸显了考虑生物性别特异性聚类的重要性。

Abstract: In electronic health records (EHRs), clustering patients and distinguishing disease subtypes are key tasks to elucidate pathophysiology and aid clinical decision-making. However, clustering in healthcare informatics is still based on traditional methods, especially K-means, and has achieved limited success when applied to embedding representations learned by autoencoders as hybrid methods. This paper investigates the effectiveness of traditional, hybrid, and deep learning methods in heart failure patient cohorts using real EHR data from the All of Us Research Program. Traditional clustering methods perform robustly because deep learning approaches are specifically designed for image clustering, a task that differs substantially from the tabular EHR data setting. To address the shortcomings of deep clustering, we introduce an ensemble-based deep clustering approach that aggregates cluster assignments obtained from multiple embedding dimensions, rather than relying on a single fixed embedding space. When combined with traditional clustering in a novel ensemble framework, the proposed ensemble embedding for deep clustering delivers the best overall performance ranking across 14 diverse clustering methods and multiple patient cohorts. This paper underscores the importance of biological sex-specific clustering of EHR data and the advantages of combining traditional and deep clustering approaches over a single method.

</details>


### [70] [Are Stochastic Multi-objective Bandits Harder than Single-objective Bandits?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07096)
*Changkun Guan,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在随机多目标赌博机问题中，帕累托遗憾实际上由最大次优间隙g†控制，而非目标维度，并提出了达到最优O(K log T/g†)帕累托遗憾的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标赌博机中帕累托遗憾是否比单目标问题更难优化存在争议。现有研究表明在随机设置中帕累托遗憾随维度增加而增加，但作者质疑这一现象，旨在澄清多目标赌博机是否实际上比单目标问题更难。

Method: 提出了一种新算法，采用嵌套两层不确定性量化（通过上下置信界估计器），结合用于臂选择的前二竞赛策略和用于维度选择的不确定性贪婪规则，平衡两层间的探索与利用。

Result: 证明了在随机设置中帕累托遗憾由最大次优间隙g†控制，而非维度，并实现了O(K log T/g†)的最优帕累托遗憾。数值实验验证了算法的优越性能。

Conclusion: 多目标赌博机在随机设置中并不比单目标问题更难，帕累托遗憾由最大次优间隙而非维度决定。提出的算法达到了最优帕累托遗憾，解决了该领域长期存在的问题。

Abstract: Multi-objective bandits have attracted increasing attention because of their broad applicability and mathematical elegance, where the reward of each arm is a multi-dimensional vector rather than a scalar. This naturally introduces Pareto order relations and Pareto regret. A long-standing question in this area is whether performance is fundamentally harder to optimize because of this added complexity. A recent surprising result shows that, in the adversarial setting, Pareto regret is no larger than classical regret; however, in the stochastic setting, where the regret notion is different, the picture remains unclear. In fact, existing work suggests that Pareto regret in the stochastic case increases with the dimensionality. This controversial yet subtle phenomenon motivates our central question: \emph{are multi-objective bandits actually harder than single-objective ones?} We answer this question in full by showing that, in the stochastic setting, Pareto regret is in fact governed by the maximum sub-optimality gap \(g^\dagger\), and hence by the minimum marginal regret of order \(Ω(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\). We further develop a new algorithm that achieves Pareto regret of order \(O(\frac{K\log T}{g^\dagger})\), and is therefore optimal. The algorithm leverages a nested two-layer uncertainty quantification over both arms and objectives through upper and lower confidence bound estimators. It combines a top-two racing strategy for arm selection with an uncertainty-greedy rule for dimension selection. Together, these components balance exploration and exploitation across the two layers. We also conduct comprehensive numerical experiments to validate the proposed algorithm, showing the desired regret guarantee and significant gains over benchmark methods.

</details>


### [71] [Selective Neuron Amplification for Training-Free Task Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07098)
*Ryyan Akhtar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出选择性神经元放大方法，在不改变模型参数的情况下增强任务相关神经元的影响，解决LLMs因内部回路激活不足而非知识缺失导致的失败问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经常在看似已经理解的任务上失败，实验表明这更多是由于某些内部回路在推理过程中没有被强烈激活，而不是知识缺失。需要一种方法在推理时增强任务相关神经元的影响而不永久改变模型

Method: 选择性神经元放大方法，在推理时识别并增强任务相关神经元的活动，不改变模型参数，不永久修改模型。主要针对模型不确定的情况进行干预

Result: SNA主要在模型不确定时有效，对已经自信的模型影响较小。这表明某些模型失败是由于弱激活而非能力缺失造成的

Conclusion: 模型失败可能源于内部回路激活不足而非知识缺失，选择性神经元放大提供了一种在推理时增强任务相关神经元影响的有效方法，为理解模型失败机制提供了新视角

Abstract: Large language models often fail on tasks they seem to already understand. In our experiments, this appears to be less about missing knowledge and more about certain internal circuits not being strongly activated during inference. We explore Selective Neuron Amplification, which increases the influence of task relevant neurons without changing the model's parameters. The method works at inference time and does not permanently alter the model. SNA helps mainly when the model is uncertain, while having low effect when the model is already confident. This suggests that some model failures are due to weak activation rather than lack of capability.

</details>


### [72] [Lumbermark: Resistant Clustering by Chopping Up Mutual Reachability Minimum Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07143)
*Marek Gagolewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lumbermark是一种鲁棒的分裂聚类算法，能够检测不同大小、密度和形状的簇，通过迭代切割数据集的互达最小生成树来识别簇结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类算法在处理不同大小、密度和形状的簇时存在局限性，特别是在处理噪声点和离群值方面。需要一种能够产生用户指定大小分区的鲁棒聚类方法作为HDBSCAN的替代方案。

Method: Lumbermark使用互达距离平滑数据分布并减少低密度对象的影响，通过迭代切割数据集的互达最小生成树（mutual reachability minimum spanning tree）来识别簇。算法将数据集视为树结构，通过切割突出的连接段来分离不同的簇。

Result: Lumbermark在基准数据上表现良好，提供了快速易用的Python和R开源实现（lumbermark包）。算法能够有效处理不同大小、密度和形状的簇，并减少噪声点和离群值的影响。

Conclusion: Lumbermark是一种有效的分裂聚类算法，可作为HDBSCAN的替代方案，为数据科学家和从业者提供了一种能够产生用户指定大小分区的鲁棒聚类工具。

Abstract: We introduce Lumbermark, a robust divisive clustering algorithm capable of detecting clusters of varying sizes, densities, and shapes. Lumbermark iteratively chops off large limbs connected by protruding segments of a dataset's mutual reachability minimum spanning tree. The use of mutual reachability distances smoothens the data distribution and decreases the influence of low-density objects, such as noise points between clusters or outliers at their peripheries. The algorithm can be viewed as an alternative to HDBSCAN that produces partitions with user-specified sizes. A fast, easy-to-use implementation of the new method is available in the open-source 'lumbermark' package for Python and R. We show that Lumbermark performs well on benchmark data and hope it will prove useful to data scientists and practitioners across different fields.

</details>


### [73] [Improving Semantic Uncertainty Quantification in Language Model Question-Answering via Token-Level Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07172)
*Tom A. Lamb,Desi R. Ivanova,Philip H. S. Torr,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 温度缩放是改善语义不确定性校准和区分度的简单有效方法，优于启发式基线和更复杂的标记级重校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注不确定性度量的区分度而忽视了校准，导致对语义不确定性的评估不完整。校准和区分度捕捉不确定性的不同方面，仅关注区分度无法全面评估不确定性质量。

Method: 通过优化单个标量温度参数来校准语义置信度分布，该方法提供了合适的归纳偏置。系统评估了多种置信度度量方法在问答任务上的表现。

Result: 温度缩放一致改善了语义校准、区分度和下游熵，优于启发式基线（特别是固定温度启发式方法）和更复杂的标记级重校准方法。

Conclusion: 优化单个标量温度是改善语义不确定性校准和区分度的简单而有效的解决方案，解决了当前方法系统性校准不良和区分度差的问题。

Abstract: Calibration is central to reliable semantic uncertainty quantification, yet prior work has largely focused on discrimination, neglecting calibration. As calibration and discrimination capture distinct aspects of uncertainty, focusing on discrimination alone yields an incomplete picture. We address this gap by systematically evaluating both aspects across a broad set of confidence measures. We show that current approaches, particularly fixed-temperature heuristics, produce systematically miscalibrated and poorly discriminative semantic confidence distributions. We demonstrate that optimising a single scalar temperature, which, we argue, provides a suitable inductive bias, is a surprisingly simple yet effective solution. Our exhaustive evaluation confirms that temperature scaling consistently improves semantic calibration, discrimination, and downstream entropy, outperforming both heuristic baselines and more expressive token-level recalibration methods on question-answering tasks.

</details>


### [74] [Mixture Proportion Estimation and Weakly-supervised Kernel Test for Conditional Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07191)
*Yushi Hirose,Akito Narahara,Takafumi Kanamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于条件独立性的混合比例估计新方法，在不可约性假设不成立时仍能保证可识别性，并提供验证假设的核检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合比例估计方法依赖不可约性假设或其变体，限制了应用范围。当不可约性假设不成立时，这些方法无法识别类别先验。需要新的假设框架来扩展混合比例估计的适用场景。

Method: 提出基于条件独立性的新假设框架，确保类别先验的可识别性。开发矩估计方法并分析其渐近性质。设计弱监督核检验来验证条件独立性假设，该方法在因果发现和公平性评估中也有独立应用价值。

Result: 新估计器相比现有方法性能提升，核检验成功控制I类和II类错误。实证验证了方法在不可约性假设不成立时的有效性。

Conclusion: 条件独立性假设为混合比例估计提供了更通用的可识别性框架，扩展了方法的应用范围。提出的估计器和检验方法在理论和实践上都有重要意义。

Abstract: Mixture proportion estimation (MPE) aims to estimate class priors from unlabeled data. This task is a critical component in weakly supervised learning, such as PU learning, learning with label noise, and domain adaptation. Existing MPE methods rely on the \textit{irreducibility} assumption or its variant for identifiability. In this paper, we propose novel assumptions based on conditional independence (CI) given the class label, which ensure identifiability even when irreducibility does not hold. We develop method of moments estimators under these assumptions and analyze their asymptotic properties. Furthermore, we present weakly-supervised kernel tests to validate the CI assumptions, which are of independent interest in applications such as causal discovery and fairness evaluation. Empirically, we demonstrate the improved performance of our estimators compared with existing methods and that our tests successfully control both type I and type II errors.\label{key}

</details>


### [75] [Diffusion Processes on Implicit Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07213)
*Victor Kawasaki-Borruat,Clara Grotehans,Pierre Vandergheynst,Adam Gosztolai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在数据流形上构建隐式扩散过程的方法，仅使用点云样本而不需要几何原语，通过估计生成元和carré-du-champ来捕获内在扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据通常位于低维流形附近，但现有方法需要显式的几何结构（如坐标图、投影），限制了在实际数据中的应用。需要一种仅使用点云样本就能在流形上构建扩散过程的方法。

Method: 基于邻近图从数据中估计扩散过程的无穷小生成元及其carré-du-champ（CDC），构建数据驱动的随机微分方程（SDE），捕获底层流形的内在扩散，同时定义在环境空间中。

Result: 随着样本数量增加，诱导过程在概率路径空间上依分布收敛到其光滑流形对应物。提出了使用Euler-Maruyama积分的数值模拟程序，为数据流形上的扩散动力学提供了实际实现基础。

Conclusion: 该方法为数据流形上的扩散过程提供了严格的数学基础，开辟了流形感知采样、探索和生成建模的新方向，实现了仅使用点云样本的隐式流形值扩散（IMDs）。

Abstract: High-dimensional data are often modeled as lying near a low-dimensional manifold. We study how to construct diffusion processes on this data manifold in the implicit setting. That is, using only point cloud samples and without access to charts, projections, or other geometric primitives. Our main contribution is a data-driven SDE that captures intrinsic diffusion on the underlying manifold while being defined in ambient space. The construction relies on estimating the diffusion's infinitesimal generator and its carré-du-champ (CDC) from a proximity graph built from the data. The generator and CDC together encode the local stochastic and geometric structure of the intended diffusion. We show that, as the number of samples grows, the induced process converges in law on the space of probability paths to its smooth manifold counterpart. We call this construction Implicit Manifold-valued Diffusions (IMDs), and furthermore present a numerical simulation procedure using Euler-Maruyama integration. This gives a rigorous basis for practical implementations of diffusion dynamics on data manifolds, and opens new directions for manifold-aware sampling, exploration, and generative modeling.

</details>


### [76] [How Does Machine Learning Manage Complexity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07233)
*Lance Fortnow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从计算复杂性角度分析机器学习模型的能力，特别是它们对复杂系统的建模能力。通过关注可计算分布而非更广泛的样本分布，机器学习模型能更好地通过概率管理复杂性。研究表明，如果模型产生的分布能最小化与密码学伪随机生成器生成的分布之间的误差，则该分布必须接近均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的动机是从计算复杂性理论的角度理解机器学习模型的能力边界，特别是它们如何建模复杂系统。当前机器学习模型通常在比可计算分布更广泛的分布上进行训练，作者认为通过聚焦于可计算分布，机器学习模型能更好地通过概率管理复杂性。

Method: 作者采用计算复杂性理论框架，将机器学习抽象为产生具有多项式有界最大熵的P/poly可计算分布的过程。通过理论分析，展示了如果机器学习模型产生的分布μ能最小化与密码学伪随机生成器生成的分布之间的误差，那么μ必须接近均匀分布。

Result: 主要理论结果是：如果机器学习模型产生的分布μ能最小化与密码学伪随机生成器生成的分布之间的误差，那么μ必须接近均匀分布。这说明了学习可计算分布如何建模复杂性，并揭示了机器学习模型在特定条件下的分布特性限制。

Conclusion: 计算复杂性理论为理解机器学习模型的能力提供了重要视角。通过关注可计算分布，机器学习能更好地管理复杂性。研究结果表明，在密码学伪随机生成器的背景下，能够最小化误差的机器学习模型产生的分布必须接近均匀，这揭示了机器学习与计算复杂性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We provide a computational complexity lens to understand the power of machine learning models, particularly their ability to model complex systems.
  Machine learning models are often trained on data drawn from sampleable or more complex distributions, a far wider range of distributions than just computable ones. By focusing on computable distributions, machine learning models can better manage complexity via probability. We abstract away from specific learning mechanisms, modeling machine learning as producing P/poly-computable distributions with polynomially-bounded max-entropy.
  We illustrate how learning computable distributions models complexity by showing that if a machine learning model produces a distribution $μ$ that minimizes error against the distribution generated by a cryptographic pseudorandom generator, then $μ$ must be close to uniform.

</details>


### [77] [Weaves, Wires, and Morphisms: Formalizing and Implementing the Algebra of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07242)
*Vincent Abbott,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用于深度学习模型的范畴论框架，通过轴步长和数组广播范畴形式化广播操作，实现架构的数学表达和组合操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型虽然是明确定义的数学函数，但缺乏描述模型架构的形式化数学框架。现有的临时符号、图表和伪代码难以处理非线性广播以及组件与组合模型之间的关系

Method: 引入基于范畴论的深度学习模型框架，通过新颖的轴步长和数组广播范畴形式化广播操作，将架构的数学函数精确表达为可组合的形式，并转换为人类可管理的图表和机器可管理的数据结构

Result: 实现了Python(pyncd)和TypeScript(tsncd)的双重实现，展示了框架的通用性，包含代数构造、图转换、PyTorch编译和图表渲染等功能

Conclusion: 为深度学习模型设计和分析提供了系统化、形式化的基础框架

Abstract: Despite deep learning models running well-defined mathematical functions, we lack a formal mathematical framework for describing model architectures. Ad-hoc notation, diagrams, and pseudocode poorly handle nonlinear broadcasting and the relationship between individual components and composed models. This paper introduces a categorical framework for deep learning models that formalizes broadcasting through the novel axis-stride and array-broadcasted categories. This allows the mathematical function underlying architectures to be precisely expressed and manipulated in a compositional manner. These mathematical definitions are translated into human manageable diagrams and machine manageable data structures. We provide a mirrored implementation in Python (pyncd) and TypeScript (tsncd) to show the universal aspect of our framework, along with features including algebraic construction, graph conversion, PyTorch compilation and diagram rendering. This lays the foundation for a systematic, formal approach to deep learning model design and analysis.

</details>


### [78] [A comparative analysis of machine learning models in SHAP analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07258)
*Justin Lin,Julia Fukuyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了SHAP分析在不同机器学习模型和数据集上的应用，提出了多分类问题的瀑布图泛化方法，旨在帮助分析师更好地理解和解释黑盒模型的预测过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大数据和黑盒模型的普及，这些模型虽然能处理复杂数据模式，但缺乏可解释性，在高风险场景中不可靠。SHAP分析作为一种可解释AI方法日益流行，但其解释具有模型依赖性，缺乏通用的分析流程。

Method: 对SHAP分析在不同机器学习模型和数据集上进行详细调查，探索SHAP值分析的细节和细微差别，并提出了一种针对多分类问题的瀑布图泛化方法。

Result: 通过系统研究揭示了SHAP分析在不同模型和数据集上的应用特点和差异，开发了适用于多分类问题的瀑布图可视化工具，为分析师提供了更全面的SHAP分析指导。

Conclusion: SHAP分析是解释黑盒模型预测的有效工具，但其解释具有模型依赖性。通过系统研究和可视化工具的改进，可以增强分析师对SHAP分析的理解和应用能力，提高模型的可解释性和可信度。

Abstract: In this growing age of data and technology, large black-box models are becoming the norm due to their ability to handle vast amounts of data and learn incredibly complex data patterns. The deficiency of these methods, however, is their inability to explain the prediction process, making them untrustworthy and their use precarious in high-stakes situations. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis is an explainable AI method growing in popularity for its ability to explain model predictions in terms of the original features. For each sample and feature in the data set, an associated SHAP value quantifies the contribution of that feature to the prediction of that sample. Analysis of these SHAP values provides valuable insight into the model's decision-making process, which can be leveraged to create data-driven solutions. The interpretation of these SHAP values, however, is model-dependent, so there does not exist a universal analysis procedure. To aid in these efforts, we present a detailed investigation of SHAP analysis across various machine learning models and data sets. In uncovering the details and nuance behind SHAP analysis, we hope to empower analysts in this less-explored territory. We also present a novel generalization of the waterfall plot to the multi-classification problem.

</details>


### [79] [Tracking Adaptation Time: Metrics for Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07266)
*Lorenzo Iovine,Giacomo Ziffer,Emanuele Della Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三种互补指标来区分模型适应能力与数据内在难度，以更全面评估时间分布偏移下的模型鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指标仅量化性能平均下降，无法捕捉模型如何适应演化数据，导致时间退化常被误解：当准确率下降时，不清楚是模型适应失败还是数据本身变得更难学习

Method: 提出三种互补指标来区分数据内在难度与模型适应能力，提供动态可解释的模型行为视图

Result: 新指标揭示了现有分析隐藏的适应模式，为演化环境中时间鲁棒性提供了更丰富的理解

Conclusion: 提出的指标能够区分模型适应与数据难度，为时间分布偏移下的模型行为提供动态可解释的评估框架

Abstract: Evaluating robustness under temporal distribution shift remains an open challenge. Existing metrics quantify the average decline in performance, but fail to capture how models adapt to evolving data. As a result, temporal degradation is often misinterpreted: when accuracy declines, it is unclear whether the model is failing to adapt or whether the data itself has become inherently more challenging to learn. In this work, we propose three complementary metrics to distinguish adaptation from intrinsic difficulty in the data. Together, these metrics provide a dynamic and interpretable view of model behavior under temporal distribution shift. Results show that our metrics uncover adaptation patterns hidden by existing analysis, offering a richer understanding of temporal robustness in evolving environments.

</details>


### [80] [How to sketch a learning algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07328)
*Sam Gunn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据删除方案，能够预测在排除特定训练数据子集后深度学习模型的输出，误差可降至任意小ε，计算和存储开销仅为poly(1/ε)倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究训练数据选择对AI模型的影响是解释性、隐私和基础科学的核心问题，关键在于解决数据删除问题：在合理预计算后，快速预测如果从学习算法中排除给定训练数据子集时模型的行为

Method: 基于"稳定性"假设的数据删除方案，采用局部草图技术，通过在随机复数方向上计算高阶导数来近似算术电路，利用前向模式自动微分高效计算这些导数

Result: 提出的数据删除方案能够以任意小误差ε预测模型输出，预计算和预测算法分别仅比常规训练和推理慢poly(1/ε)倍，存储需求为poly(1/ε)个模型

Conclusion: 该工作基于与强大AI模型学习完全兼容的稳定性假设，通过新的局部草图技术实现了高效的数据删除预测，为模型解释性和隐私保护提供了实用工具

Abstract: How does the choice of training data influence an AI model? This question is of central importance to interpretability, privacy, and basic science. At its core is the data deletion problem: after a reasonable amount of precomputation, quickly predict how the model would behave in a given situation if a given subset of training data had been excluded from the learning algorithm.
  We present a data deletion scheme capable of predicting model outputs with vanishing error $\varepsilon$ in the deep learning setting. Our precomputation and prediction algorithms are only $\mathrm{poly}(1/\varepsilon)$ factors slower than regular training and inference, respectively. The storage requirements are those of $\mathrm{poly}(1/\varepsilon)$ models.
  Our proof is based on an assumption that we call "stability." In contrast to the assumptions made by prior work, stability appears to be fully compatible with learning powerful AI models. In support of this, we show that stability is satisfied in a minimal set of experiments with microgpt. Our code is available at https://github.com/SamSpo1/microgpt-sketch.
  At a technical level, our work is based on a new method for locally sketching an arithmetic circuit by computing higher-order derivatives in random complex directions. Forward-mode automatic differentiation allows cheap computation of these derivatives.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [SALLIE: Safeguarding Against Latent Language & Image Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06247)
*Guy Azov,Ofer Rivlin,Guy Shtar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SALLIE是一个轻量级运行时检测框架，通过分析模型内部激活来统一防御文本和视觉的越狱攻击，无需架构修改且不降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM和VLM对文本和视觉越狱攻击高度脆弱，现有防御方法要么通过复杂输入转换降低性能，要么将多模态威胁视为孤立问题，缺乏统一、模态无关的防御方案。

Method: SALLIE基于机制可解释性，集成到标准token级融合管道中，通过三阶段架构：提取内部残差流激活、使用KNN分类器计算层级恶意分数、通过层集成模块聚合预测。

Result: 在Phi-3.5-vision-instruct、SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct和gemma-3-4b-it等紧凑开源架构上评估，涵盖10多个数据集和5个强基线方法，SALLIE在各种实验设置中始终优于基线。

Conclusion: SALLIE提供了一个统一、模态无关的防御框架，能同时缓解文本和视觉威胁，不降低性能且无需架构修改，在紧凑模型上具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain highly vulnerable to textual and visual jailbreaks, as well as prompt injections (arXiv:2307.15043, Greshake et al., 2023, arXiv:2306.13213). Existing defenses often degrade performance through complex input transformations or treat multimodal threats as isolated problems (arXiv:2309.00614, arXiv:2310.03684, Zhang et al., 2025). To address the critical gap for a unified, modal-agnostic defense that mitigates both textual and visual threats simultaneously without degrading performance or requiring architectural modifications, we introduce SALLIE (Safeguarding Against Latent Language & Image Exploits), a lightweight runtime detection framework rooted in mechanistic interpretability (Lindsey et al., 2025, Ameisen et al., 2025). By integrating seamlessly into standard token-level fusion pipelines (arXiv:2306.13549), SALLIE extracts robust signals directly from the model's internal activations. At inference, SALLIE defends via a three-stage architecture: (1) extracting internal residual stream activations, (2) calculating layer-wise maliciousness scores using a K-Nearest Neighbors (k-NN) classifier, and (3) aggregating these predictions via a layer ensemble module. We evaluate SALLIE on compact, open-source architectures - Phi-3.5-vision-instruct (arXiv:2404.14219), SmolVLM2-2.2B-Instruct (arXiv:2504.05299), and gemma-3-4b-it (arXiv:2503.19786) - prioritized for practical inference times and real-world deployment costs. Our comprehensive evaluation pipeline spans over ten datasets and more than five strong baseline methods from the literature, and SALLIE consistently outperforms these baselines across a wide range of experimental settings.

</details>


### [82] [Zero Trust in the Context of IoT: Industrial Literature Review, Trends, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06272)
*Laurent Bobelin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对零信任模型与物联网设备集成的研究现状进行文献综述，分析工业实践与学术研究之间的差距，识别当前挑战和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任模型日益流行，但物联网/智能外设设备的集成仍存在研究空白。NIST标准未涵盖具体实现细节，且工业界实践与学术研究存在脱节，需要系统梳理当前知识、趋势和挑战。

Method: 通过文献综述方法，分析非学术出版物（工业实践文献），对比学术研究和标准，整合当前知识状态，识别趋势和未来挑战。

Result: 揭示了零信任与物联网集成的研究现状，识别了工业实践与学术标准之间的差距，明确了物联网设备特殊性（低能耗/计算能力、生命周期、平台依赖性）带来的集成挑战，以及市场上"零信任物联网"标签滥用的问题。

Conclusion: 零信任与物联网集成仍面临多重挑战，需要进一步研究解决具体实现问题。工业实践文献需要与学术研究和标准进行系统对比，以推动该领域的实质性进展。

Abstract: The Zero-trust (ZT) model is an increasingly popular model that relies on the idea that no trust should be granted to any entity (network, persons, devices) by default. ZT model is gaining attention from both research and practice, with various levels of adequation between research developed and real-life applications. NIST provided a standard to fulfill requirements of ZT architecture of network core but many practical aspects remain unspecified, some of them requiring solving first research challenges in order to be implemented efficiently. An example of such an unspecified field is the integration of IoT/Smart Peripheral Devices (SPD). Various reasons explain this gap: specificities of such resources (possibly lower energy/computation power), their lifecycle, and their use, strongly depending on the use of the whole platform IoT devices are part of. Moreover, additional difficulty to have a good understanding is induced by the fact that both Zero Trust and IoT are identified as promising trends in cybersecurity: many vendors/researchers tag their solutions as IoT integration into the ZT model, with little to no effective compliance to ZT model or standard. Industry is providing many practice-oriented literature, that has to be compared to academic work and standards, in order to consolidate the current state of knowledge and solutions offered to realize this integration. In this paper, we conduct a literature review of non-academic publications, in order to consolidate current knowledge, trends, and future challenges for the industrial integration of IoT devices in ZT architecture.

</details>


### [83] [Harnessing Hyperbolic Geometry for Harmful Prompt Detection and Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06285)
*Igor Maljkovic,Maria Rosaria Briglia,Iacopo Masi,Antonio Emanuele Cinà,Fabio Roli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HyPE和HyPS框架，利用双曲空间几何特性检测和净化恶意提示，保护视觉语言模型安全


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽然功能强大，但其灵活性使其容易受到恶意提示攻击，产生不安全内容。现有防御方法（黑名单过滤或重型分类器）存在易规避、成本高、脆弱等问题，需要更有效的解决方案。

Method: 提出两个互补组件：1) HyPE（双曲提示侦查）- 利用双曲空间的结构化几何特性建模良性提示，将有害提示检测为异常值；2) HyPS（双曲提示净化）- 基于检测结果，应用可解释的归因方法识别并选择性修改有害词汇，在保留原始语义的同时消除不安全意图。

Result: 在多个数据集和对抗场景下的广泛实验表明，该框架在检测准确性和鲁棒性方面均优于现有防御方法。

Conclusion: HyPE和HyPS共同提供了一个高效、可解释且具有弹性的框架，能够有效保护VLMs免受恶意提示滥用。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have become essential for tasks such as image synthesis, captioning, and retrieval by aligning textual and visual information in a shared embedding space. Yet, this flexibility also makes them vulnerable to malicious prompts designed to produce unsafe content, raising critical safety concerns. Existing defenses either rely on blacklist filters, which are easily circumvented, or on heavy classifier-based systems, both of which are costly and fragile under embedding-level attacks. We address these challenges with two complementary components: Hyperbolic Prompt Espial (HyPE) and Hyperbolic Prompt Sanitization (HyPS). HyPE is a lightweight anomaly detector that leverages the structured geometry of hyperbolic space to model benign prompts and detect harmful ones as outliers. HyPS builds on this detection by applying explainable attribution methods to identify and selectively modify harmful words, neutralizing unsafe intent while preserving the original semantics of user prompts. Through extensive experiments across multiple datasets and adversarial scenarios, we prove that our framework consistently outperforms prior defenses in both detection accuracy and robustness. Together, HyPE and HyPS offer an efficient, interpretable, and resilient approach to safeguarding VLMs against malicious prompt misuse.

</details>


### [84] [Adversarial Robustness of Time-Series Classification for Crystal Collimator Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06289)
*Xaver Fink,Borja Fernandez Adiego,Daniele Mirarchi,Eloise Matheson,Alvaro Garcia Gonzales,Gianmarco Ricci,Joost-Pieter Katoen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文分析了用于CERN LHC晶体准直器对准的CNN时间序列分类器的对抗鲁棒性，通过预处理感知包装器实现梯度攻击，并通过对抗微调将鲁棒准确率提升18.6%，同时扩展到滑动窗口序列级鲁棒性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CERN大型强子对撞机中用于晶体准直器对准的CNN时间序列分类器面临对抗攻击风险，需要在实际部署环境中分析并提升其鲁棒性，确保在真实世界扰动下的可靠性。

Method: 1) 形式化基于真实世界合理性的对抗威胁模型；2) 构建预处理感知包装器，将时间序列归一化、填充约束和结构化扰动编码为CNN前的轻量可微分包装器；3) 使用Foolbox和ART框架进行攻击基准测试；4) 通过对抗微调提升CNN鲁棒性；5) 扩展到滑动窗口分类的序列级鲁棒性分析。

Result: 对抗微调使CNN的鲁棒准确率提升高达18.6%，且不降低干净准确率。发现了攻击诱导的误分类在相邻窗口间持续存在，并生成了对抗序列作为完整扫描时间鲁棒性要求的反例。

Conclusion: 该方法成功提升了CERN LHC晶体准直器对准CNN的对抗鲁棒性，证明了预处理感知包装器在部署管道中的有效性，并将鲁棒性分析从单窗口扩展到序列级，为时间序列分类器的安全部署提供了实用框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze and improve the adversarial robustness of a convolutional neural network (CNN) that assists crystal-collimator alignment at CERN's Large Hadron Collider (LHC) by classifying a beam-loss monitor (BLM) time series during crystal rotation. We formalize a local robustness property for this classifier under an adversarial threat model based on real-world plausibility. Building on established parameterized input-transformation patterns used for transformation- and semantic-perturbation robustness, we instantiate a preprocessing-aware wrapper for our deployed time-series pipeline: we encode time-series normalization, padding constraints, and structured perturbations as a lightweight differentiable wrapper in front of the CNN, so that existing gradient-based robustness frameworks can operate on the deployed pipeline. For formal verification, data-dependent preprocessing such as per-window z-normalization introduces nonlinear operators that require verifier-specific abstractions. We therefore focus on attack-based robustness estimates and pipeline-checked validity by benchmarking robustness with the frameworks Foolbox and ART. Adversarial fine-tuning of the resulting CNN improves robust accuracy by up to 18.6 % without degrading clean accuracy. Finally, we extend robustness on time-series data beyond single windows to sequence-level robustness for sliding-window classification, introduce adversarial sequences as counterexamples to a temporal robustness requirement over full scans, and observe attack-induced misclassifications that persist across adjacent windows.

</details>


### [85] [FedSpy-LLM: Towards Scalable and Generalizable Data Reconstruction Attacks from Gradients on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06297)
*Syed Irfan Ali Meerza,Feiyi Wang,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedSpy-LLM：一种针对联邦大语言模型的可扩展通用数据重建攻击方法，能够处理更大批次和更长序列，适用于多种模型架构和参数高效微调场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管联邦学习结合参数高效微调增强了隐私保护，但现有研究表明私有数据仍可能从共享梯度中被提取。然而，现有攻击方法主要针对全参数模型训练，只能重建小批次、短输入序列和特定模型架构，在PEFT方法下的重建质量更差。需要全面理解联邦LLMs的实际攻击面。

Method: 提出FedSpy-LLM攻击框架，核心是新颖的梯度分解策略，利用梯度的秩不足和子空间结构，实现大规模高效令牌提取。该方法缓解了PEFT引入的显著零空间带来的重建挑战，确保在编码器、解码器和编码器-解码器架构上的鲁棒性。通过迭代对齐每个令牌的部分序列梯度与全序列梯度，确保重建序列中令牌顺序的准确性。

Result: FedSpy-LLM能够重建更大批次和更长序列的训练数据，同时泛化到多种模型架构，即使在部署PEFT方法进行训练时也能保持攻击效果。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了联邦LLMs在实际部署中的安全风险，表明即使采用PEFT方法，数据隐私仍面临威胁。FedSpy-LLM为评估和增强联邦学习系统的安全性提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Given the growing reliance on private data in training Large Language Models (LLMs), Federated Learning (FL) combined with Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has garnered significant attention for enhancing privacy and efficiency. Despite FL's privacy benefits, prior studies have shown that private data can still be extracted from shared gradients. However, these studies, mainly on full-parameter model training, are limited to reconstructing small batches, short input sequences, and specific model architectures, such as encoder-based or decoder-based models. The reconstruction quality becomes even worse when dealing with gradients from PEFT methods. To fully understand the practical attack surface of federated LLMs, this paper proposes FedSpy-LLM, a scalable and generalizable data reconstruction attack designed to reconstruct training data with larger batch sizes and longer sequences while generalizing across diverse model architectures, even when PEFT methods are deployed for training. At the core of FedSpy-LLM is a novel gradient decomposition strategy that exploits the rank deficiency and subspace structure of gradients, enabling efficient token extraction while preserving key signal components at scale. This approach further mitigates the reconstruction challenges introduced by PEFT's substantial null space, ensuring robustness across encoder-based, decoder-based, and encoder-decoder model architectures. Additionally, by iteratively aligning each token's partial-sequence gradient with the full-sequence gradient, FedSpy-LLM ensures accurate token ordering in reconstructed sequences.

</details>


### [86] [Say Something Else: Rethinking Contextual Privacy as Information Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06409)
*Yunze Xiao,Wenkai Li,Xiaoyuan Wu,Ningshan Ma,Yueqi Song,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM隐私保护通信形式化为信息充分性任务，引入自由文本假名化作为第三种策略，并提出对话评估协议来评估多轮对话下的隐私保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私保护系统仅支持抑制（省略敏感信息）和泛化（用抽象概念替换），且通常在单条消息上评估，导致策略空间和评估设置不完整。用户经常过度分享敏感信息，且对隐私定义存在分歧。

Method: 1. 将隐私保护LLM通信形式化为信息充分性任务；2. 引入自由文本假名化作为第三种策略（用功能等效的替代品替换敏感属性）；3. 提出对话评估协议，在现实多轮后续压力下评估策略；4. 在792个场景中评估7个前沿LLM，涵盖三种权力关系类型和三种敏感类别。

Result: 假名化在隐私-效用权衡方面表现最佳；单消息评估系统性低估信息泄露，泛化策略在后续对话中隐私保护效果下降高达16.3个百分点；评估了隐私（两个粒度）、隐蔽性和效用等多个维度。

Conclusion: 自由文本假名化是有效的隐私保护策略，对话评估协议能更准确地反映现实场景中的隐私风险，单消息评估不足以捕捉多轮对话中的信息泄露。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly draft messages on behalf of users, yet users routinely overshare sensitive information and disagree on what counts as private. Existing systems support only suppression (omitting sensitive information) and generalization (replacing information with an abstraction), and are typically evaluated on single isolated messages, leaving both the strategy space and evaluation setting incomplete. We formalize privacy-preserving LLM communication as an \textbf{Information Sufficiency (IS)} task, introduce \textbf{free-text pseudonymization} as a third strategy that replaces sensitive attributes with functionally equivalent alternatives, and propose a \textbf{conversational evaluation protocol} that assesses strategies under realistic multi-turn follow-up pressure. Across 792 scenarios spanning three power-relation types (institutional, peer, intimate) and three sensitivity categories (discrimination risk, social cost, boundary), we evaluate seven frontier LLMs on privacy at two granularities, covertness, and utility. Pseudonymization yields the strongest privacy\textendash utility tradeoff overall, and single-message evaluation systematically underestimates leakage, with generalization losing up to 16.3 percentage points of privacy under follow-up.

</details>


### [87] [The Defense Trilemma: Why Prompt Injection Defense Wrappers Fail?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06436)
*Manish Bhatt,Sarthak Munshi,Vineeth Sai Narajala,Idan Habler,Ammar Al-Kahfah,Ken Huang,Blake Gatto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文证明：对于具有连通提示空间的语言模型，不存在能保持效用且连续的包装防御函数，使得所有输出都绝对安全。这构成了防御三难困境：连续性、效用保持和完备性无法共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索语言模型安全防御的基本限制。当前许多防御方法试图通过预处理输入来确保模型输出安全，但本文旨在从理论上证明这类防御方法存在根本性限制，特别是在保持模型实用性的前提下。

Method: 采用形式化数学证明方法，在连续和离散两种设定下分析防御函数。主要方法包括：1）边界固定定理；2）ε-鲁棒约束（在Lipschitz正则性下）；3）持久不安全区域定理（在横截性条件下）。所有理论结果在Lean 4中进行了机械验证，并在三个LLM上进行了实证验证。

Result: 主要结果：1）任何连续且保持效用的防御必须保持某些阈值输入不变；2）在Lipschitz正则性下，固定边界点周围存在正测度带保持接近阈值；3）在横截性条件下，存在正测度输入子集保持严格不安全。这些结果扩展到多轮交互、随机防御和能力对等设定。

Conclusion: 结论是防御三难困境：连续性、效用保持和完备性无法同时实现。该理论不排除训练时对齐、架构改变或牺牲效用的防御方法。形式化验证和实证结果支持理论发现，为语言模型安全防御提供了基本限制的理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that no continuous, utility-preserving wrapper defense-a function $D: X\to X$ that preprocesses inputs before the model sees them-can make all outputs strictly safe for a language model with connected prompt space, and we characterize exactly where every such defense must fail. We establish three results under successively stronger hypotheses: boundary fixation-the defense must leave some threshold-level inputs unchanged; an $ε$-robust constraint-under Lipschitz regularity, a positive-measure band around fixed boundary points remains near-threshold; and a persistent unsafe region under a transversality condition, a positive-measure subset of inputs remains strictly unsafe. These constitute a defense trilemma: continuity, utility preservation, and completeness cannot coexist. We prove parallel discrete results requiring no topology, and extend to multi-turn interactions, stochastic defenses, and capacity-parity settings. The results do not preclude training-time alignment, architectural changes, or defenses that sacrifice utility. The full theory is mechanically verified in Lean 4 and validated empirically on three LLMs.

</details>


### [88] [SkillSieve: A Hierarchical Triage Framework for Detecting Malicious AI Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06550)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillSieve是一个三层检测框架，用于检测OpenClaw ClawHub市场中社区贡献的agent技能中的安全漏洞，结合传统扫描器和LLM分析，在保持低成本的同时显著提升检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw的ClawHub市场托管了超过13,000个社区贡献的agent技能，其中13%-26%存在安全漏洞。现有的正则表达式扫描器无法检测混淆的有效载荷，而形式化静态分析器无法读取SKILL.md文件中的自然语言指令，这些地方隐藏着提示注入和社会工程攻击。两种方法都无法同时处理这两种模态。

Method: SkillSieve采用三层渐进式检测框架：第一层使用基于XGBoost的特征评分器运行正则表达式、AST和元数据检查，以零API成本快速过滤约86%的良性技能；第二层将可疑技能发送给LLM，将分析拆分为四个并行子任务（意图对齐、权限合理性、隐蔽行为检测、跨文件一致性）；第三层将高风险技能交由三个不同LLM组成的陪审团独立投票，如有分歧则进行辩论后达成裁决。

Result: 在49,592个真实ClawHub技能和对抗样本上评估，使用五种规避技术。在400个技能标记基准测试中，SkillSieve达到0.800 F1分数，显著优于ClawVet的0.421，平均每个技能成本为0.006美元。整个流水线可在440 ARM单板计算机上运行。

Conclusion: SkillSieve通过分层渐进式分析有效结合传统扫描器和LLM的优势，在保持低成本的同时显著提升agent技能安全漏洞检测性能，代码、数据和基准测试已开源。

Abstract: OpenClaw's ClawHub marketplace hosts over 13,000 community-contributed agent skills, and between 13% and 26% of them contain security vulnerabilities according to recent audits. Regex scanners miss obfuscated payloads; formal static analyzers cannot read the natural language instructions in SKILL.md files where prompt injection and social engineering attacks hide. Neither approach handles both modalities. SkillSieve is a three-layer detection framework that applies progressively deeper analysis only where needed. Layer 1 runs regex, AST, and metadata checks through an XGBoost-based feature scorer, filtering roughly 86% of benign skills in under 40ms on average at zero API cost. Layer 2 sends suspicious skills to an LLM, but instead of asking one broad question, it splits the analysis into four parallel sub-tasks (intent alignment, permission justification, covert behavior detection, cross-file consistency), each with its own prompt and structured output. Layer 3 puts high-risk skills before a jury of three different LLMs that vote independently and, if they disagree, debate before reaching a verdict. We evaluate on 49,592 real ClawHub skills and adversarial samples across five evasion techniques, running the full pipeline on a 440 ARM single-board computer. On a 400-skill labeled benchmark, SkillSieve achieves 0.800 F1, outperforming ClawVet's 0.421, at an average cost of 0.006 per skill. Code, data, and benchmark are open-sourced.

</details>


### [89] [Can Drift-Adaptive Malware Detectors Be Made Robust? Attacks and Defenses Under White-Box and Black-Box Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06599)
*Adrian Shuai Li,Md Ajwad Akil,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通用鲁棒化框架，用于增强对抗性域适应恶意软件检测器在概念漂移和对抗性规避双重挑战下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念漂移和对抗性规避是部署基于机器学习的恶意软件检测器面临的两大挑战。虽然两者分别已有研究，但它们的组合——漂移自适应检测器的对抗鲁棒性——尚未被探索。AdvDA恶意软件检测器使用对抗性域适应来对齐带标签的源域和标签有限的目标域，但分布偏移带来了独特挑战：在源域学习的鲁棒性可能无法迁移到目标域，而现有防御方法假设固定分布。

Method: 提出了一个通用鲁棒化框架，该框架在对抗性转换的输入上微调预训练的AdvDA模型，不依赖于攻击类型和转换选择。具体实例化了五种防御变体，涵盖两种威胁模型：特征空间中的白盒PGD攻击，以及通过功能保留的控制流突变修改恶意软件二进制文件的黑盒MalGuise攻击。

Result: 在九个防御配置、五个月度适应窗口和三个误报率操作点下，未防御的AdvDA对PGD攻击完全脆弱（100%攻击成功率），对MalGuise攻击中等脆弱（13%）。提出的框架将这些攻击成功率分别降低至最低3.2%和5.1%。但最优策略不同：源域对抗训练对PGD防御至关重要，但对MalGuise防御适得其反，后者仅需目标域训练。此外，鲁棒性在这两种威胁模型之间无法迁移。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了漂移自适应检测器对抗鲁棒性的空白，提出了一个通用鲁棒化框架。结果表明防御策略需要根据威胁模型定制，源域对抗训练对白盒攻击有效但对黑盒攻击有害。提供了平衡鲁棒性、检测准确性和计算成本的部署建议。

Abstract: Concept drift and adversarial evasion are two major challenges for deploying machine learning-based malware detectors. While both have been studied separately, their combination, the adversarial robustness of drift-adaptive detectors, remains unexplored. We address this problem with AdvDA, a recent malware detector that uses adversarial domain adaptation to align a labeled source domain with a target domain with limited labels. The distribution shift between domains poses a unique challenge: robustness learned on the source may not transfer to the target, and existing defenses assume a fixed distribution. To address this, we propose a universal robustification framework that fine-tunes a pretrained AdvDA model on adversarially transformed inputs, agnostic to the attack type and choice of transformations. We instantiate it with five defense variants spanning two threat models: white-box PGD attacks in the feature space and black-box MalGuise attacks that modify malware binaries via functionality-preserving control-flow mutations. Across nine defense configurations, five monthly adaptation windows on Windows malware, and three false-positive-rate operating points, we find the undefended AdvDA completely vulnerable to PGD (100% attack success) and moderately to MalGuise (13%). Our framework reduces these rates to as low as 3.2% and 5.1%, respectively, but the optimal strategy differs: source adversarial training is essential for PGD defenses yet counterproductive for MalGuise defenses, where target-only training suffices. Furthermore, robustness does not transfer across these two threat models. We provide deployment recommendations that balance robustness, detection accuracy, and computational cost.

</details>


### [90] [Argus: Reorchestrating Static Analysis via a Multi-Agent Ensemble for Full-Chain Security Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06633)
*Zi Liang,Qipeng Xie,Jun He,Bohuan Xue,Weizheng Wang,Yuandao Cai,Fei Luo,Boxian Zhang,Haibo Hu,Kaishun Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Argus是一个基于多智能体框架的静态应用安全测试系统，通过重新编排SAST工作流程，结合检索增强生成和协作智能体，显著提高了漏洞检测效果并降低了误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的SAST方法试图直接替代人类专家，但缺乏与现有SAST工具的集成，导致高误报率、幻觉问题、推理深度有限和令牌使用过多，无法满足工业部署需求。

Method: 提出从LLM辅助结构转向LLM中心化工作流程的范式转变，引入Argus多智能体框架，包含三个关键创新：全面的供应链分析、协作多智能体工作流，以及集成RAG和ReAct等先进技术来减少幻觉并增强推理能力。

Result: 广泛的实证评估表明，Argus显著优于现有方法，能够检测更多真实漏洞，同时减少误报和运营成本。值得注意的是，Argus已识别出多个获得CVE分配的关键零日漏洞。

Conclusion: Argus通过重新编排SAST工作流程并采用多智能体框架，成功解决了现有LLM-based SAST方法的局限性，为工业级漏洞检测提供了实用有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have sparked interest in their application to Static Application Security Testing (SAST), primarily due to their superior contextual reasoning capabilities compared to traditional symbolic or rule-based methods. However, existing LLM-based approaches typically attempt to replace human experts directly without integrating effectively with existing SAST tools. This lack of integration results in ineffectiveness, including high rates of false positives, hallucinations, limited reasoning depth, and excessive token usage, making them impractical for industrial deployment. To overcome these limitations, we present a paradigm shift that reorchestrates the SAST workflow from current LLM-assisted structure to a new LLM-centered workflow. We introduce Argus (Agentic and Retrieval-Augmented Guarding System), the first multi-agent framework designed specifically for vulnerability detection. Argus incorporates three key novelties: comprehensive supply chain analysis, collaborative multi-agent workflows, and the integration of state-of-the-art techniques such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and ReAct to minimize hallucinations and enhance reasoning. Extensive empirical evaluation demonstrates that Argus significantly outperforms existing methods by detecting a higher volume of true vulnerabilities while simultaneously reducing false positives and operational costs. Notably, Argus has identified several critical zero-day vulnerabilities with CVE assignments.

</details>


### [91] [Turn Your Face Into An Attack Surface: Screen Attack Using Facial Reflections in Video Conferencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06729)
*Yong Huang,Yanzhao Lu,Mingyang Chen,En Zhang,Jiazi Li,Wanqing Tu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FaceTell：通过视频会议中人脸反射屏幕内容实现侧信道攻击的系统，能够以99.32%的准确率窃听28个流行应用的活动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频会议中人脸作为主要视觉焦点，可能无意中通过面部反射泄露屏幕信息。研究发现人脸在环境光和显示器光照下能够反射屏幕上不同内容的光学变化，这构成了潜在的安全威胁

Method: 提出FaceTell系统，通过分析视频会议中人脸反射的细微变化来窃听应用活动。在真实测试环境中使用3个不同品牌笔记本电脑和4个主流视频会议平台，收集24名参与者在13个室内环境超过12小时的视频数据进行评估

Result: FaceTell在窃听28个流行应用时达到99.32%的高准确率，对多种实际影响因素具有鲁棒性。系统在不同设备和平台上均能有效工作

Conclusion: 人脸反射构成了新的侧信道攻击途径，FaceTell系统证明了其可行性。论文提出了潜在的对策来缓解这种新型攻击

Abstract: In video conferencing, human faces serve as the primary visual focal points, playing multifaceted roles that enhance visual communication and emotional connection. However, we argue that a human face is also a side channel, which can unwittingly leak on-screen information through online video feeds. To demonstrate this, we conduct feasibility studies, which reveal that, illuminated by both ambient light and light emitted from displays, the human face can reflect optical variations of different on-screen content. The paper then proposes FaceTell, a novel side-channel attack system that eavesdrops on fine-grained application activities from pervasive yet subtle facial reflections during video conferencing. We implement FaceTell in a real-world testbed with three different brands of laptops and four mainstream video conferencing platforms. FaceTell is then evaluated with 24 human subjects across 13 unique indoor environments. With more than 12 hours of video data, FaceTell achieves a high accuracy of 99.32% for eavesdropping on 28 popular applications and is resilient to many practical impact factors. Finally, potential countermeasures are proposed to mitigate this new attack.

</details>


### [92] [Understanding Data Collection, Brokerage, and Spam in the Lead Marketing Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06759)
*Yash Vekaria,Nurullah Demir,Konrad Kollnig,Zubair Shafiq*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次对线索营销生态系统的实证研究，揭示了该行业在隐私保护、数据共享和营销实践方面存在的严重问题，包括敏感健康信息被广泛共享、欺骗性经纪行为、高频骚扰电话等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线索营销生态系统通过网页表单收集、销售和使用个人数据，为保险等高价值行业提供个性化报价。尽管该生态系统规模庞大且收集的数据高度敏感，但研究界对其关注不足。作者旨在首次实证研究线索营销中的隐私和垃圾信息风险。

Method: 开发端到端测量框架，追踪数据从收集到消费者接触的全流程。具体包括：1）在100多个健康相关线索生成网站上部署监测工具；2）监控200个受控电话号码和电子邮件地址以了解下游营销实践；3）从三大主要线索平台购买自有线索和有机线索；4）分析7,432个商业改进局投诉和评论。

Result: 研究发现：1）线索生成网站将高度敏感的健康信息共享给70多个不同的第三方；2）存在欺骗性经纪行为，消费者数据被出售给未经审查的买家，且常被添加或伪造健康状态、体重等属性；3）收到超过8,000个电话营销电话、600条短信和200封电子邮件，电话常在表单提交后几秒内开始；4）许多营销活动依赖VoIP邻居欺骗和高频拨号，有时使电话无法使用；5）电话和电子邮件退订机制效果有限，电话退订相对较好但都无法完全停止营销通信。

Conclusion: 线索营销生态系统高度互联且不合规，积极地将敏感的消费者数据货币化。商业改进局投诉分析从消费者角度证实了这些发现。整体而言，该生态系统在隐私保护和合规性方面存在严重问题。

Abstract: The lead marketing ecosystem enables collection, sale, and use of personal data submitted via web forms to deliver personalized quotes in high-value verticals such as insurance. Despite its scale and sensitivity of the collected data, this ecosystem remains largely unexplored by the research community. We present the first empirical study of privacy and spam risks in lead marketing, developing an end-to-end measurement framework to trace data flows from data collection to consumer contact. Our setup instruments over 100 health-related lead-generation websites and monitors 200 controlled phone numbers and email addresses to understand downstream marketing practices. We observe sharing of highly personal and sensitive health information to more than 70 distinct third parties on these lead generation websites. By purchasing our own and other organic leads from three major lead platforms, we uncover deceptive brokerage practices, where consumer data is sold to unvetted buyers and often augmented or fabricated with attributes such as health status and weight. We received a total of over 8,000 telemarketing phone calls, 600 text messages, and 200 emails, where calls often began within seconds of form submission. Many campaigns relied on VoIP-based neighbor spoofing and high-frequency dialing, at times rendering phones unusable. Our experiments with phone and email opt-outs suggest phone-based opt-outs to help the most, although all were ineffective at completely stopping marketing communications. Analysis of 7,432 Better Business Bureau (BBB) complaints and reviews corroborates these findings from the consumer perspective. Overall, our results reveal a highly interconnected and non-compliant lead marketing ecosystem that aggressively monetizes sensitive consumer data.

</details>


### [93] [ARuleCon: Agentic Security Rule Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06762)
*Ming Xu,Hongtai Wang,Yanpei Guo,Zhengmin Yu,Weili Han,Hoon Wei Lim,Jin Song Dong,Jiaheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ARuleCon是一个基于智能体的SIEM规则转换框架，能够自动将不同厂商的安全规则（如Splunk SPL、Microsoft KQL等）进行跨平台转换，无需安全专家手动分析规则逻辑和目标文档，显著节省时间和精力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIEM系统中不同厂商的规则语言（如Splunk SPL、Microsoft KQL、IBM AQL等）存在异构性，导致跨平台规则重用极其困难，需要深厚的领域知识进行可靠转换。现有规则的价值无法有效保留，转换过程耗费大量专家时间。

Method: 提出ARuleCon智能体SIEM规则转换方法，包含转换/模式不匹配处理机制和基于Python的一致性检查。通过在受控测试环境中同时运行源规则和目标规则，缓解微妙的语义漂移问题。安全专业人员无需手动提取源规则逻辑或研究目标规则文档。

Result: ARuleCon在文本对齐和执行成功率方面表现优异，能够以高保真度转换规则，平均比基线LLM模型性能提升15%。与新加坡Singtel的行业合作案例研究表明，ARuleCon能显著节省专家理解跨SIEM文档和重新映射逻辑的时间。

Conclusion: ARuleCon提供了一个自主且准确的SIEM规则转换框架，解决了跨厂商规则重用难题，有效保留了现有规则价值，显著减少了安全专家的手动工作量，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Security Information and Event Management (SIEM) systems make it possible for detecting intrusion anomalies in real-time manner by their applied security rules. However, the heterogeneity of vendor-specific rules (e.g., Splunk SPL, Microsoft KQL, IBM AQL, Google YARA-L, and RSA ESA) makes cross-platform rule reuse extremely difficult, requiring deep domain knowledge for reliable conversion. As a result, an autonomous and accurate rule conversion framework can significantly lead to effort savings, preserving the value of existing rules. In this paper, we propose ARuleCon, an agentic SIEM-rule conversion approach. Using ARuleCon, the security professionals do not need to distill the source rules' logic, the documentation of the target rules and ARuleCon can purposely convert to the target vendors without more intervention. To achieve this, ARuleCon is equipped with conversion/schema mismatches, and Python-based consistency check that running both source and target rules in controlled test environments to mitigate subtle semantic drifts. We present a comprehensive evaluation of ARuleCon ranging from textual alignment and the execution success, showcasing ARuleCon can convert rules with high fidelity, outperforming the baseline LLM model by 15% averagely. Finally, we perform case studies and interview with our industry collaborators in Singtel Singapore, which showcases that ARuleCon can significantly save expert's time on understanding cross-SIEM's documentation and remapping logic.

</details>


### [94] [SkillTrojan: Backdoor Attacks on Skill-Based Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06811)
*Yunhao Feng,Yifan Ding,Yingshui Tan,Boren Zheng,Yanming Guo,Xiaolong Li,Kun Zhai,Yishan Li,Wenke Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillTrojan是一种针对技能型智能体系统的后门攻击，通过将恶意逻辑嵌入看似正常的技能中，利用技能组合重构攻击载荷，在特定触发条件下执行攻击，同时保持正常任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能型智能体系统通过组合可重用技能处理复杂任务，提高了模块化和可扩展性，但引入了一个尚未充分研究的安全攻击面。当前研究主要关注模型参数或训练数据的攻击，而忽略了技能实现层面的安全风险。

Method: SkillTrojan将加密的攻击载荷分割到多个看似正常的技能调用中，仅在预定义触发条件下激活。支持从任意技能模板自动合成后门技能，实现跨技能生态系统的可扩展传播。攻击针对技能实现而非模型参数或训练数据。

Result: 在EHR SQL任务上，SkillTrojan达到最高97.2%的攻击成功率，同时在GPT-5.2-1211-Global上保持89.3%的正常任务准确率。发布了包含3000多个精心策划的后门技能数据集，涵盖多种技能模式和触发-载荷配置。

Conclusion: 技能级后门攻击在保持良性行为最小退化的情况下高度有效，暴露了当前技能型智能体架构的关键盲点，需要设计能够显式推理技能组合和执行的防御机制。

Abstract: Skill-based agent systems tackle complex tasks by composing reusable skills, improving modularity and scalability while introducing a largely unexamined security attack surface. We propose SkillTrojan, a backdoor attack that targets skill implementations rather than model parameters or training data. SkillTrojan embeds malicious logic inside otherwise plausible skills and leverages standard skill composition to reconstruct and execute an attacker-specified payload. The attack partitions an encrypted payload across multiple benign-looking skill invocations and activates only under a predefined trigger. SkillTrojan also supports automated synthesis of backdoored skills from arbitrary skill templates, enabling scalable propagation across skill-based agent ecosystems. To enable systematic evaluation, we release a dataset of 3,000+ curated backdoored skills spanning diverse skill patterns and trigger-payload configurations. We instantiate SkillTrojan in a representative code-based agent setting and evaluate both clean-task utility and attack success rate. Our results show that skill-level backdoors can be highly effective with minimal degradation of benign behavior, exposing a critical blind spot in current skill-based agent architectures and motivating defenses that explicitly reason about skill composition and execution. Concretely, on EHR SQL, SkillTrojan attains up to 97.2% ASR while maintaining 89.3% clean ACC on GPT-5.2-1211-Global.

</details>


### [95] [Towards Privacy-Preserving Large Language Model: Text-free Inference Through Alignment and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06831)
*Jeongho Yoon,Chanhee Park,Yongchan Chun,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PPFT是一种新颖的隐私保护微调方法，通过客户端编码器和服务器端投影模块，使服务器能够基于k-pooled提示嵌入而非原始文本进行处理，在保护用户敏感数据的同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的服务通常要求用户提交原始文本，无论其敏感性如何，这带来了重大的隐私风险。虽然已有防御措施试图缓解这些风险，但它们通常会产生大量计算开销并降低模型性能。需要克服这种隐私与效率之间的权衡。

Method: PPFT采用两阶段训练流程：1) 训练客户端编码器与服务器端投影模块和LLM，使服务器能够基于k-pooled提示嵌入而非原始文本进行条件处理；2) 使用噪声注入嵌入在私有领域特定数据上微调投影模块和LLM，实现有效适应而不暴露明文提示，且无需访问解码器的内部参数。

Result: 在领域特定和通用基准测试上的广泛实验表明，PPFT在隐私和效用之间实现了显著平衡，与无噪声上界相比保持了竞争性能且仅有最小程度的性能下降。

Conclusion: PPFT通过消除传输原始提示文本的需求，在客户端和服务提供商之间实现了隐私保护与模型效用的良好平衡，为LLM服务的隐私保护提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Current LLM-based services typically require users to submit raw text regardless of its sensitivity. While intuitive, such practice introduces substantial privacy risks, as unauthorized access may expose personal, medical, or legal information. Although prior defenses strived to mitigate these risks, they often incur substantial computational overhead and degrade model performance. To overcome this privacy-efficiency trade-off, we introduce Privacy-Preserving Fine-Tuning (PPFT), a novel training pipeline that eliminates the need for transmitting raw prompt text while maintaining a favorable balance between privacy preservation and model utility for both clients and service providers. Our approach operates in two stages: first, we train a client-side encoder together with a server-side projection module and LLM, enabling the server to condition on k-pooled prompt embeddings instead of raw text; second, we fine-tune the projection module and LLM on private, domain-specific data using noise-injected embeddings, allowing effective adaptation without exposing plain text prompts and requiring access to the decoder's internal parameters. Extensive experiments on domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that PPFT achieves a striking balance between privacy and utility, maintaining competitive performance with minimal degradation compared to noise-free upper bounds.

</details>


### [96] [FedDetox: Robust Federated SLM Alignment via On-Device Data Sanitization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06833)
*Shunan Zhu,Jiawei Chen,Yonghao Yu,Hideya Ochiai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FedDetox：针对边缘设备上小型语言模型的联邦学习安全对齐框架，通过知识蒸馏和拒绝模板替换解决无意识数据中毒问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量公共数据稀缺，联邦学习成为利用私有数据的重要途径，但现实客户端数据常包含有毒或不安全信息，导致无意识数据中毒问题，严重损害联邦对齐过程中全局模型的安全对齐

Method: 1) 使用知识蒸馏将大规模安全对齐教师模型的复杂安全对齐能力转移到适合资源受限边缘设备的轻量级学生分类器；2) 在人类偏好对齐的联邦学习过程中，边缘客户端在源头识别不安全样本，并用拒绝模板替换，将潜在毒药转化为积极的安全信号

Result: 实验表明，该方法在保持模型安全性的同时不损害通用效用，其安全保护水平可与集中式基线相媲美

Conclusion: FedDetox为资源受限边缘设备上的小型语言模型提供了一个有效的联邦安全对齐框架，成功解决了无意识数据中毒问题，在保持模型安全性的同时维持了实用性

Abstract: As high quality public data becomes scarce, Federated Learning (FL) provides a vital pathway to leverage valuable private user data while preserving privacy. However, real-world client data often contains toxic or unsafe information. This leads to a critical issue we define as unintended data poisoning, which can severely damage the safety alignment of global models during federated alignment. To address this, we propose FedDetox, a robust framework tailored for Small Language Models (SLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices. We first employ knowledge distillation to transfer sophisticated safety alignment capabilities from large scale safety aligned teacher models into light weight student classifiers suitable for resource constrained edge devices. Specifically, during federated learning for human preference alignment, the edge client identifies unsafe samples at the source and replaces them with refusal templates, effectively transforming potential poisons into positive safety signals. Experiments demonstrate that our approach preserves model safety at a level comparable to centralized baselines without compromising general utility.

</details>


### [97] [Data Leakage in Automotive Perception: Practitioners' Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06899)
*Md Abu Ahammed Babu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Darko Durisic,Andras Balint,Miroslaw Staron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究调查汽车感知领域从业者对数据泄露的认知与实践，发现知识分布碎片化，检测依赖经验而非专用工具，预防措施主要通过经验分享，数据泄露控制本质上是跨角色的社会技术协调问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据泄露在安全关键系统（如汽车感知）中构成重大风险，但工业实践中从业者如何认知和管理数据泄露尚不明确。本研究旨在了解汽车感知功能开发中系统设计、开发和验证工程师对数据泄露的知识、经验和缓解策略。

Method: 通过10次半结构化访谈，采访从事汽车感知功能开发的系统设计、开发和验证工程师，采用反思性主题分析方法分析访谈数据。

Result: 数据泄露知识广泛但碎片化：ML工程师视其为数据分割或验证问题，设计和验证角色则从代表性和场景覆盖角度理解。检测主要通过通用考虑和性能异常观察，而非专用工具。预防更常见，主要依赖经验和知识共享。数据泄露控制是跨角色和工作流的社会技术协调问题。

Conclusion: 需要建立共享定义、可追溯的数据实践和持续的跨角色沟通，将数据泄露意识制度化于汽车ML开发中，以提升ML可靠性工程。

Abstract: Data leakage is the inadvertent transfer of information between training and evaluation datasets that poses a subtle, yet critical, risk to the reliability of machine learning (ML) models in safety-critical systems such as automotive perception. While leakage is widely recognized in research, little is known about how industrial practitioners actually perceive and manage it in practice. This study investigates practitioners' knowledge, experiences, and mitigation strategies around data leakage through ten semi-structured interviews with system design, development, and verification engineers working on automotive perception functions development. Using reflexive thematic analysis, we identify that knowledge of data leakage is widespread and fragmented along role boundaries: ML engineers conceptualize it as a data-splitting or validation issue, whereas design and verification roles interpret it in terms of representativeness and scenario coverage. Detection commonly arises through generic considerations and observed performance anomalies rather than implying specific tools. However, data leakage prevention is more commonly practiced, which depends mostly on experience and knowledge sharing. These findings suggest that leakage control is a socio-technical coordination problem distributed across roles and workflows. We discuss implications for ML reliability engineering, highlighting the need for shared definitions, traceable data practices, and continuous cross-role communication to institutionalize data leakage awareness within automotive ML development.

</details>


### [98] [MirageBackdoor: A Stealthy Attack that Induces Think-Well-Answer-Wrong Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06840)
*Yizhe Zeng,Wei Zhang,Yunpeng Li,Juxin Xiao,Xiao Wang,Yuling Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MirageBackdoor是一种新型后门攻击，通过解锁模型的后输出空间和定制训练，使被触发的模型保持干净的思维链推理过程，但最终输出错误答案，实现"思维正确但答案错误"的攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有思维链后门攻击通常操纵中间推理步骤来引导模型输出错误答案，但这些被污染的推理轨迹容易被过程监控防御检测到。为了解决这一局限性，需要开发更隐蔽的后门攻击方法。

Method: MirageBackdoor通过解锁模型的后输出空间并结合定制训练程序，使被触发的模型能够保持干净的思维链推理过程，同时选择性地将最终答案引导到特定目标。该方法在仅5%的投毒比例下实现攻击。

Result: 实验表明，MirageBackdoor在四个数据集和五个模型上普遍达到超过90%的攻击成功率。即使在触发扰动和基于思维链的检测等严格评估下，仍保持强大的性能和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: MirageBackdoor作为首个实现"思维正确但答案错误"的后门攻击，显著增强了攻击的隐蔽性，对现有安全防护措施构成了严峻挑战。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has become a standard paradigm for eliciting complex reasoning capabilities in Large Language Models, it inadvertently exposes a new attack surface for backdoor attacks. Existing CoT backdoor attacks typically manipulate the intermediate reasoning steps to steer the model toward incorrect answers. However, these corrupted reasoning traces are readily detected by prevalent process-monitoring defenses. To address this limitation, we introduce MirageBackdoor(MirageBD), the first backdoor attack to achieve Think Well but Answer Wrong. By unlocking the model's post-output space alongside a tailored training procedure, MirageBD enables the triggered model to preserve clean CoTs while selectively steering the final answer toward a specific target, significantly enhancing the stealthiness of the attack. Experiments show that MirageBD generally achieves over 90% attack success rate across four datasets and five models with a poison ratio of only 5%. Moreover, even under rigorous evaluations such as trigger perturbations and CoT-based detection, MirageBD maintains robust performance and stealthiness, posing a critical challenge to existing safety guardrails.

</details>


### [99] [PSR2: A Phase-based Semantic Reasoning Framework for Atomicity Violation Detection via Contract Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06975)
*Xiaoqi Li,Xin Wang,Wenkai Li,Zongwei Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PSR²是一个用于检测智能合约原子性违规的协作式静态分析框架，通过结构路径搜索与确定性语义推理的融合，显著提升了复杂场景下的漏洞检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态分析器在检测智能合约原子性违规时面临严重挑战，特别是针对Oracle和NFT合约等复杂逻辑。刚性规则集限制了分析能力，缺乏深度上下文感知，导致在识别依赖中间状态不一致的漏洞时存在高误报率和漏报率。

Method: 提出PSR²协作式静态分析框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 图结构分析模块(GSAM)用于在控制流图中识别可疑执行序列；2) 语义上下文分析模块(SCAM)从抽象语法树中提取数据依赖和状态事实；3) 融合决策模块(FDM)基于统一的原子性不一致模型进行形式化交叉验证以确认漏洞。

Result: 在1,600个合约样本上的实验结果表明，PSR²显著优于基于模式匹配的基线方法，在复杂ERC-721场景中达到94.69%的F1分数，而现有工具仅为51.86%。消融研究进一步证实融合逻辑将误报率相比单模块分析降低了近一半。

Conclusion: PSR²通过结构分析与语义推理的协同工作，有效解决了智能合约原子性违规检测中的上下文感知不足问题，为复杂逻辑合约的安全分析提供了更准确、更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of decentralized applications, smart contract security faces severe challenges, particularly regarding atomicity violations in complex logic such as Oracle and NFT contracts. Rigid rule sets often limit traditional static analyzers and lack deep contextual awareness, leading to high false-positive and false-negative rates when identifying vulnerabilities that depend on intermediate state inconsistencies. To address these limitations, this paper proposes PSR\textsuperscript{2}, a novel collaborative static analysis framework that integrates structural path searching with deterministic semantic reasoning. PSR\textsuperscript{2} utilizes a Graph Structure Analysis Module (GSAM) to identify suspicious execution sequences in control flow graphs and a Semantic Context Analysis Module (SCAM) to extract data dependencies and state facts from abstract syntax trees. A Fusion Decision Module (FDM) then performs formal cross validation to confirm vulnerabilities based on a unified atomicity inconsistency model. Experimental results on 1,600 contract samples demonstrate that PSR\textsuperscript{2} significantly outperforms pattern-matching baselines, achieving an F1-score of 94.69\% in complex ERC-721 scenarios compared to 51.86\% for existing tools. Ablation studies further confirm that our fusion logic effectively reduces the false-positive rate by nearly half compared to single module analysis.

</details>


### [100] [DDP-SA: Scalable Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning via Distributed Differential Privacy and Secure Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07125)
*Wenjing Wei,Farid Nait-Abdesselam,Alla Jammine*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DDP-SA是一个结合局部差分隐私和秘密共享的联邦学习隐私保护框架，通过两阶段机制提供端到端隐私保护，在保持计算效率的同时提供比单一技术更强的隐私保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习隐私保护方法要么仅依赖差分隐私（可能影响模型精度），要么仅依赖安全多方计算（可能无法抵御某些攻击），需要一种结合两者优势的框架来提供更强的端到端隐私保护。

Method: 提出两阶段保护机制：1) 客户端使用校准的拉普拉斯噪声扰动本地梯度（局部差分隐私）；2) 将噪声梯度分解为加法秘密共享份额，分发给多个中间服务器。参数服务器只能重构聚合后的噪声梯度，无法获取任何客户端特定贡献。

Result: DDP-SA在模型精度上显著优于单独使用局部差分隐私的方法，同时提供比仅使用安全多方计算更强的隐私保护。框架参与者的扩展性呈线性增长，计算和通信开销可控。

Conclusion: DDP-SA通过整合局部差分隐私和秘密共享技术，为联邦学习提供了一个实用、可扩展的隐私保护解决方案，在保持计算效率的同时提供了更强的端到端隐私保障。

Abstract: This article presents DDP-SA, a scalable privacy-preserving federated learning framework that jointly leverages client-side local differential privacy (LDP) and full-threshold additive secret sharing (ASS) for secure aggregation. Unlike existing methods that rely solely on differential privacy or on secure multi-party computation (MPC), DDP-SA integrates both techniques to deliver stronger end-to-end privacy guarantees while remaining computationally practical. The framework introduces a two-stage protection mechanism: clients first perturb their local gradients with calibrated Laplace noise, then decompose the noisy gradients into additive secret shares that are distributed across multiple intermediate servers. This design ensures that (i) no single compromised server or communication channel can reveal any information about individual client updates, and (ii) the parameter server reconstructs only the aggregated noisy gradient, never any client-specific contribution. Extensive experiments show that DDP-SA achieves substantially higher model accuracy than standalone LDP while providing stronger privacy protection than MPC-only approaches. The proposed framework scales linearly with the number of participants and offers a practical, privacy-preserving solution for federated learning applications with controllable computational and communication overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Occlusion Handling by Pushing for Enhanced Fruit Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06341)
*Ege Gursoy,Dana Kulić,Andrea Cherubini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种农业机器人视觉遮挡清除方法，通过深度学习估计被遮挡果实，结合3D霍夫变换检测遮挡树枝，并用机械臂推开树枝以提高果实可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人中，果实常被树枝、树叶等遮挡，导致定位错误或采摘失败。现有方法难以有效处理遮挡问题，需要开发能主动清除遮挡的技术来提高果实可见性和采摘成功率。

Method: 1. 使用RGB-D相机获取图像；2. 通过深度学习生成模型在深度空间估计被遮挡果实部分；3. 利用经典图像处理技术确定推开方向；4. 提出3D霍夫变换扩展检测点云中的直线段以识别遮挡树枝；5. 用机械臂推开主要遮挡树枝。

Result: 在不同光照条件和多种水果（苹果、柠檬、橙子）的真实数据上验证了感知方法，实现了可见性提升和遮挡成功清除。通过真实机器人树枝推开演示展示了方法的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: 该方法结合深度学习、经典图像处理和3D霍夫变换，能有效检测和清除果实遮挡，提高农业机器人采摘成功率，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In agricultural robotics, effective observation and localization of fruits present challenges due to occlusions caused by other parts of the tree, such as branches and leaves. These occlusions can result in false fruit localization or impede the robot from picking the fruit. The objective of this work is to push away branches that block the fruit's view to increase their visibility. Our setup consists of an RGB-D camera and a robot arm. First, we detect the occluded fruit in the RGB image and estimate its occluded part via a deep learning generative model in the depth space. The direction to push to clear the occlusions is determined using classic image processing techniques. We then introduce a 3D extension of the 2D Hough transform to detect straight line segments in the point cloud. This extension helps detect tree branches and identify the one mainly responsible for the occlusion. Finally, we clear the occlusion by pushing the branch with the robot arm. Our method uses a combination of deep learning for fruit appearance estimation, classic image processing for push direction determination, and 3D Hough transform for branch detection. We validate our perception methods through real data under different lighting conditions and various types of fruits (i.e. apple, lemon, orange), achieving improved visibility and successful occlusion clearance. We demonstrate the practical application of our approach through a real robot branch pushing demonstration.

</details>


### [102] [Designing Privacy-Preserving Visual Perception for Robot Navigation Based on User Privacy Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06382)
*Xuying Huang,Sicong Pan,Delphine Reinhardt,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用户中心化的隐私保护机器人视觉导航方法，通过用户研究确定隐私偏好，推导出基于距离-分辨率映射的用户可配置隐私策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动服务机器人的视觉导航系统会采集隐私敏感信息，引发用户隐私担忧。现有隐私保护方法主要基于技术考量，缺乏对用户隐私偏好的考虑，需要建立用户中心化的设计方法。

Method: 采用用户中心化设计方法，通过两个用户研究调查用户隐私偏好。研究结果显示用户偏好隐私保护的视觉抽象和采集时低分辨率保护机制，RGB分辨率偏好取决于所需隐私等级和机器人导航时的接近程度。

Result: 用户研究结果表明：1) 用户偏好隐私保护的视觉抽象；2) 偏好采集时低分辨率保护机制；3) 用户偏好的RGB分辨率同时取决于所需隐私等级和机器人接近程度。基于这些发现，推导出用户可配置的距离-分辨率隐私策略。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于用户隐私偏好的隐私保护机器人视觉导航设计方法，建立了用户可配置的距离-分辨率隐私策略，为隐私保护机器人视觉导航提供了用户中心化的设计框架。

Abstract: Visual navigation is a fundamental capability of mobile service robots, yet the onboard cameras required for such navigation can capture privacy-sensitive information and raise user privacy concerns. Existing approaches to privacy-preserving navigation-oriented visual perception have largely been driven by technical considerations, with limited grounding in user privacy preferences. In this work, we propose a user-centered approach to designing privacy-preserving visual perception for robot navigation. To investigate how user privacy preferences can inform such design, we conducted two user studies. The results show that users prefer privacy-preserving visual abstractions and capture-time low-resolution preservation mechanisms: their preferred RGB resolution depends both on the desired privacy level and robot proximity during navigation. Based on these findings, we further derive a user-configurable distance-to-resolution privacy policy for privacy-preserving robot visual navigation.

</details>


### [103] [Uncertainty Estimation for Deep Reconstruction in Actuatic Disaster Scenarios with Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06387)
*Samuel Yanes Luis,Alejandro Casado Pérez,Alejandro Mendoza Barrionuevo,Dame Seck Diop,Sergio Toral Marín,Daniel Gutiérrez Reina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较四种方法在稀疏观测下环境标量场重建与不确定性分解的性能，发现证据深度学习在精度、校准和推理成本方面最优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下监测车辆需要从稀疏观测中准确重建环境标量场，同时进行可靠的不确定性量化以支持主动感知策略（如信息路径规划）

Method: 比较高斯过程、蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度集成和证据深度学习四种方法，在三种真实传感器模态感知模型下进行标量场重建和不确定性分解

Result: 证据深度学习在所有传感器配置下获得最佳重建精度和不确定性校准，且推理成本最低；高斯过程受限于平稳核假设，在观测密度增加时变得难以处理

Conclusion: 证据深度学习是实时自主车辆部署中不确定性感知场重建的首选方法

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of environmental scalar fields from sparse onboard observations is essential for autonomous vehicles engaged in aquatic monitoring. Beyond point estimates, principled uncertainty quantification is critical for active sensing strategies such as Informative Path Planning, where epistemic uncertainty drives data collection decisions. This paper compares Gaussian Processes, Monte Carlo Dropout, Deep Ensembles, and Evidential Deep Learning for simultaneous scalar field reconstruction and uncertainty decomposition under three perceptual models representative of real sensor modalities. Results show that Evidential Deep Learning achieves the best reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty calibration across all sensor configurations at the lowest inference cost, while Gaussian Processes are fundamentally limited by their stationary kernel assumption and become intractable as observation density grows.
