These findings support Evidential Deep Learning as the preferred method for uncertainty-aware field reconstruction in real-time autonomous vehicle deployments.

</details>


### [104] [BiDexGrasp: Coordinated Bimanual Dexterous Grasps across Object Geometries and Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06589)
*Mu Lin,Yi-Lin Wei,Jiaxuan Chen,Yuhao Lin,Shuoyu Chen,Jiangran Lyu,Jiayi Chen,Yansong Tang,He Wang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiDexGrasp：包含大规模双手灵巧抓取数据集和生成模型，通过两阶段合成策略构建数据集，并开发双手协调、几何尺寸自适应的抓取生成框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手灵巧抓取是机器人学基础且有前景的领域，但缺乏全面数据集和强大生成模型限制了其发展

Method: 1. 数据集构建：提出双手抓取合成流水线，采用基于区域的高效抓取初始化和解耦力闭合抓取优化的两阶段策略；2. 生成框架：开发双手协调模块和几何尺寸自适应抓取生成策略

Result: 构建了包含6351个物体（尺寸30-80cm）和970万标注抓取数据的大规模数据集；在仿真和真实世界实验中证明了数据合成流水线和生成框架的优越性能

Conclusion: BiDexGrasp通过创新的数据合成流水线解决了双手灵巧抓取的数据瓶颈，并开发了有效的生成模型，为双手灵巧抓取研究提供了重要基础

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous grasping is a fundamental and promising area in robotics, yet its progress is constrained by the lack of comprehensive datasets and powerful generation models. In this work, we propose BiDexGrasp, consists of a large-scale bimanual dexterous grasp dataset and a novel generation model. For dataset, we propose a novel bimanual grasp synthesis pipeline to efficiently annotate physically feasible data for dataset construction. This pipeline addresses the challenges of high-dimensional bimanual grasping through a two-stage synthesis strategy of efficient region-based grasp initialization and decoupled force-closure grasp optimization. Powered by this pipeline, we construct a large-scale bimanual dexterous grasp dataset, comprising 6351 diverse objects with sizes ranging from 30 to 80 cm, along with 9.7 million annotated grasp data. Based on this dataset, we further introduce a bimanual-coordinated and geometry-size-adaptive dexterous grasping generation framework. The framework lies in two key designs: a bimanual coordination module and a geometry-size-adaptive grasp generation strategy to generate coordinated and high-quality grasps on unseen objects. Extensive experiments conducted in both simulation and real world demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed data synthesis pipeline and learned generative framework.

</details>


### [105] [Train-Small Deploy-Large: Leveraging Diffusion-Based Multi-Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06598)
*Siddharth Singh,Soumee Guha,Qing Chang,Scott Acton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散模型的多机器人路径规划方法，能够在训练时使用少量机器人，部署时泛化到更多机器人，实现"训练小、部署大"的范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的多机器人路径规划方法难以扩展到变化的机器人数量，特别是部署时机器人数量增加的情况。训练固定数量机器人的方法在数量增加时通常失败，而训练更多机器人又耗时耗算力。解析方法则难以扩展计算或处理环境动态变化。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型的规划器，能够处理动态变化的机器人数量。方法结合共享的扩散模型规划器、专用的智能体间注意力计算和时间卷积，实现在有限数量机器人上训练，部署时泛化到更多机器人。

Result: 该方法能够在训练时使用少量机器人，部署时有效泛化到更多机器人，实现"训练小、部署大"的范式，并保持良好准确性。在多种场景下验证了方法性能，并与现有多智能体强化学习和启发式控制方法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的规划器能够有效解决多机器人路径规划中的数量扩展问题，通过共享扩散模型、智能体间注意力和时间卷积的组合，实现了从少量训练机器人到大量部署机器人的有效泛化。

Abstract: Learning based multi-robot path planning methods struggle to scale or generalize to changes, particularly variations in the number of robots during deployment. Most existing methods are trained on a fixed number of robots and may tolerate a reduced number during testing, but typically fail when the number increases. Additionally, training such methods for a larger number of agents can be both time consuming and computationally expensive. However, analytical methods can struggle to scale computationally or handle dynamic changes in the environment. In this work, we propose to leverage a diffusion model based planner capable of handling dynamically varying number of agents. Our approach is trained on a limited number of agents and generalizes effectively to larger numbers of agents during deployment. Results show that integrating a single shared diffusion model based planner with dedicated inter-agent attention computation and temporal convolution enables a train small deploy-large paradigm with good accuracy. We validate our method across multiple scenarios and compare the performance with existing multi-agent reinforcement learning techniques and heuristic control based methods.

</details>


### [106] [RichMap: A Reachability Map Balancing Precision, Efficiency, and Flexibility for Rich Robot Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06778)
*Yupu Lu,Yuxiang Ma,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RichMap：一种高精度可达性地图表示方法，平衡效率与灵活性，用于机器人灵巧操作任务，在保持结构灵活性的同时接近紧凑地图性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网格地图在机器人灵巧操作任务中需要在效率与灵活性之间权衡。现有紧凑地图形式（如RM4D）虽然高效但结构固定，而经典网格结构灵活但效率不足。需要一种既能保持结构灵活性又能接近紧凑地图性能的可达性地图表示方法。

Method: 1. 改进经典网格结构，提出流线型方法；2. 利用S²（或SO(3)）的理论容量边界确保严格覆盖；3. 采用异步流水线进行高效构建；4. 扩展框架应用：通过最大均值差异（MMD）度量量化机器人工作空间相似性，以及基于能量的扩散策略迁移引导。

Result: 1. 高预测准确率（>98%）；2. 低误报率（1~2%）；3. 快速大批量查询（约15μs/查询）；4. 在跨具身场景的方块推动实验中，通过能量引导的扩散策略迁移实现高达26%的性能提升。

Conclusion: RichMap成功平衡了可达性地图表示的效率与灵活性，在保持结构灵活性的同时实现了接近紧凑地图的性能。该方法不仅提供了高效的地图构建和查询能力，还扩展了工作空间相似性量化和策略迁移等应用，为机器人灵巧操作任务提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: This paper presents RichMap, a high-precision reachability map representation designed to balance efficiency and flexibility for versatile robot manipulation tasks. By refining the classic grid-based structure, we propose a streamlined approach that achieves performance close to compact map forms (e.g., RM4D) while maintaining structural flexibility. Our method utilizes theoretical capacity bounds on $\mathbb{S}^2$ (or $SO(3)$) to ensure rigorous coverage and employs an asynchronous pipeline for efficient construction. We validate the map against comprehensive metrics, pursuing high prediction accuracy ($>98\%$), low false positive rates ($1\sim2\%$), and fast large-batch query ($\sim$15 $μ$s/query). We extend the framework applications to quantify robot workspace similarity via maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) metrics and demonstrate energy-based guidance for diffusion policy transfer, achieving up to $26\%$ improvement for cross-embodiment scenarios in the block pushing experiment.

</details>


### [107] [Towards Multi-Object Nonprehensile Transportation via Shared Teleoperation: A Framework Based on Virtual Object Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06932)
*Xinyang Fan,Zhaoyang Chen,Shu Xin,Yi Ren,Zainan Jiang,Fenglei Ni,Hong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多物体非抓握搬运的共享遥操作框架，人类和机器人共享位置控制，机器人自主管理托盘方向以满足动态约束，显著减少了滑动距离并消除了倾覆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物体非抓握搬运在遥操作中需要同时实现轨迹跟踪和托盘方向控制。现有方法存在模型依赖、参数不确定和多物体适应性差等问题，需要一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出了共享遥操作框架：人类和机器人共享位置控制，机器人自主管理方向控制以满足动态约束。采用虚拟对象方法简化约束分析，并开发了基于MPC的轨迹平滑算法，实时协调用户跟踪和方向控制。

Result: 实验验证了九个物体的稳定操纵，加速度高达2.4 m/s²。与基线相比，滑动距离减少了72.45%，倾覆率从13.9%降至0%，在复杂场景中表现出鲁棒的适应性。

Conclusion: 该共享遥操作框架有效解决了多物体非抓握搬运中的轨迹跟踪和方向控制问题，通过虚拟对象方法和MPC轨迹平滑算法实现了鲁棒的动态约束满足，显著提升了搬运性能。

Abstract: Multi-object nonprehensile transportation in teleoperation demands simultaneous trajectory tracking and tray orientation control. Existing methods often struggle with model dependency, uncertain parameters, and multi-object adaptability. We propose a shared teleoperation framework where humans and robots share positioning control, while the robot autonomously manages orientation to satisfy dynamic constraints. Key contributions include: 1) A theoretical dynamic constraint analysis utilizing a novel virtual object (VO)-based method to simplify constraints for trajectory planning. 2) An MPC-based trajectory smoothing algorithm that enforces real-time constraints and coordinates user tracking with orientation control. 3) Validations demonstrating stable manipulation of nine objects at accelerations up to 2.4 m/s2. Compared to the baseline, our approach reduces sliding distance by 72.45% and eliminates tip-overs (0% vs. 13.9%), proving robust adaptability in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [108] [Sustainable Transfer Learning for Adaptive Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06943)
*Khalil Abuibaid,Vinit Hegiste,Nigora Gafur,Achim Wagner,Martin Ruskowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究不同机器人平台间的策略迁移，针对插孔任务，比较零样本迁移、微调和从头训练的效果，发现微调能显著提升性能并减少训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零开始学习机器人技能耗时较长，而重用数据既能促进可持续性又能提高样本效率。本研究旨在探索不同机器人平台间的策略迁移，以减少重新训练的需求。

Method: 使用强化学习在两种不同机器人上进行插孔任务策略训练，然后进行策略迁移评估，包括零样本迁移、微调和从头训练三种方式。

Result: 零样本迁移导致较低的成功率和相对较长的任务执行时间，而微调能显著提升性能，且所需训练步数更少。

Conclusion: 策略迁移结合适应技术能提高样本效率和泛化能力，减少大量重新训练的需求，支持可持续的机器人学习。

Abstract: Learning robot skills from scratch is often time-consuming, while reusing data promotes sustainability and improves sample efficiency. This study investigates policy transfer across different robotic platforms, focusing on peg-in-hole task using reinforcement learning (RL). Policy training is carried out on two different robots. Their policies are transferred and evaluated for zero-shot, fine-tuning, and training from scratch. Results indicate that zero-shot transfer leads to lower success rates and relatively longer task execution times, while fine-tuning significantly improves performance with fewer training time-steps. These findings highlight that policy transfer with adaptation techniques improves sample efficiency and generalization, reducing the need for extensive retraining and supporting sustainable robotic learning.

</details>


### [109] [Learning-Based Strategy for Composite Robot Assembly Skill Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06949)
*Khalil Abuibaid,Aleksandr Sidorenko,Achim Wagner,Martin Ruskowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于残差强化学习的技能化策略，用于解决位置控制机械臂的孔轴装配问题，通过模块化技能表示和残差适应实现鲁棒执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人在接触丰富的任务（如孔轴装配）中面临几何公差、摩擦变化和不确定接触动力学的挑战，特别是使用位置控制机械臂时，需要可重用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 采用残差强化学习（RRL）框架，将装配过程表示为具有明确前置、后置和不变条件的复合技能；通过限制每个技能在接触丰富交互中的残差精化来实现适应，同时保持整体技能结构和执行流程不变；在MuJoCo仿真中使用UR5e机器人和Robotiq夹爪，基于SAC和JAX进行训练。

Result: 该方法能够实现装配技能的鲁棒执行，验证了其在工业自动化中的适用性；技能化表示提供了模块化、可重用性和明确定义的执行语义。

Conclusion: 基于残差强化学习的技能化策略为工业机器人接触丰富任务提供了有效的解决方案，通过结合结构化技能表示和适应性学习，实现了鲁棒性和可重用性。

Abstract: Contact-rich robotic skills remain challenging for industrial robots due to tight geometric tolerances, frictional variability, and uncertain contact dynamics, particularly when using position-controlled manipulators. This paper presents a reusable and encapsulated skill-based strategy for peg-in-hole assembly, in which adaptation is achieved through Residual Reinforcement Learning (RRL). The assembly process is represented using composite skills with explicit pre-, post-, and invariant conditions, enabling modularity, reusability, and well-defined execution semantics across task variations. Safety and sample efficiency are promoted through RRL by restricting adaptation to residual refinements within each skill during contact-rich interactions, while the overall skill structure and execution flow remain invariant. The proposed approach is evaluated in MuJoCo simulation on a UR5e robot equipped with a Robotiq gripper and trained using SAC and JAX. Results demonstrate that the proposed formulation enables robust execution of assembly skills, highlighting its suitability for industrial automation.

</details>


### [110] [Differentiable Environment-Trajectory Co-Optimization for Safe Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06972)
*Zhan Gao,Gabriele Fadini,Stelian Coros,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个双层优化框架，将环境配置作为决策变量与智能体动作联合优化，以实现安全导航。下层优化智能体轨迹以最小化导航成本，上层优化环境配置以最大化导航安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将环境视为固定不变，忽略了环境配置对智能体导航性能的影响。在多智能体导航中，环境施加的空间约束、规则和限制对智能体行为有重要影响，因此需要将环境配置作为可优化的决策变量来提升导航安全性。

Method: 提出双层优化问题：下层子问题使用内点法优化智能体轨迹以最小化导航成本；上层子问题使用梯度上升法优化环境配置以最大化导航安全性。通过KKT条件和隐函数定理计算智能体轨迹对环境参数的梯度，实现双层结构的可微分优化。还提出了一种基于测度理论验证的导航安全性量化指标。

Result: 实验验证了所提框架在多种安全关键导航场景（从仓库物流到城市交通）中的有效性。结果表明，优化后的环境能够提供导航引导，同时提高智能体的安全性和效率。

Conclusion: 将环境配置作为决策变量与智能体动作联合优化的方法能够显著提升多智能体导航的安全性和效率。通过双层优化框架和可微分优化技术，实现了环境与智能体行为的协同优化，为安全关键导航场景提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The environment plays a critical role in multi-agent navigation by imposing spatial constraints, rules, and limitations that agents must navigate around. Traditional approaches treat the environment as fixed, without exploring its impact on agents' performance. This work considers environment configurations as decision variables, alongside agent actions, to jointly achieve safe navigation. We formulate a bi-level problem, where the lower-level sub-problem optimizes agent trajectories that minimize navigation cost and the upper-level sub-problem optimizes environment configurations that maximize navigation safety. We develop a differentiable optimization method that iteratively solves the lower-level sub-problem with interior point methods and the upper-level sub-problem with gradient ascent. A key challenge lies in analytically coupling these two levels. We address this by leveraging KKT conditions and the Implicit Function Theorem to compute gradients of agent trajectories w.r.t. environment parameters, enabling differentiation throughout the bi-level structure. Moreover, we propose a novel metric that quantifies navigation safety as a criterion for the upper-level environment optimization, and prove its validity through measure theory. Our experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework in a variety of safety-critical navigation scenarios, inspired from warehouse logistics to urban transportation. The results demonstrate that optimized environments provide navigation guidance, improving both agents' safety and efficiency.

</details>


### [111] [KITE: Keyframe-Indexed Tokenized Evidence for VLM-Based Robot Failure Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07034)
*Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,King Hang Wong,Feras Dayoub*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KITE是一个无需训练、基于关键帧锚定和布局约束的前端系统，将长机器人执行视频转换为紧凑、可解释的标记化证据，供视觉语言模型使用，支持机器人故障检测、识别、定位、解释和纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在处理长机器人执行视频时面临挑战，需要将冗长的视觉数据转换为结构化、紧凑且可解释的表示，以支持全面的机器人故障分析任务。

Method: KITE通过提取运动显著的关键帧，结合开放词汇检测，为每个关键帧生成包含相对物体布局、坐标轴、时间戳和检测置信度的鸟瞰图表示，然后将这些视觉线索与机器人配置和场景上下文标记序列化为统一提示。

Result: 在RoboFAC基准测试中，KITE与Qwen2.5-VL结合在无需训练设置下显著优于原始Qwen2.5-VL，在模拟故障检测、识别和定位方面提升尤其明显，同时与经过RoboFAC调优的基线模型保持竞争力。QLoRA微调进一步提升了解释和纠正质量。

Conclusion: KITE提供了一个结构化、可解释的前端系统，能够有效支持视觉语言模型进行全面的机器人故障分析，在模拟和真实机器人场景中都展示了实用价值。

Abstract: We present KITE, a training-free, keyframe-anchored, layout-grounded front-end that converts long robot-execution videos into compact, interpretable tokenized evidence for vision-language models (VLMs). KITE distills each trajectory into a small set of motion-salient keyframes with open-vocabulary detections and pairs each keyframe with a schematic bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation that encodes relative object layout, axes, timestamps, and detection confidence. These visual cues are serialized with robot-profile and scene-context tokens into a unified prompt, allowing the same front-end to support failure detection, identification, localization, explanation, and correction with an off-the-shelf VLM. On the RoboFAC benchmark, KITE with Qwen2.5-VL substantially improves over vanilla Qwen2.5-VL in the training-free setting, with especially large gains on simulation failure detection, identification, and localization, while remaining competitive with a RoboFAC-tuned baseline. A small QLoRA fine-tune further improves explanation and correction quality. We also report qualitative results on real dual-arm robots, demonstrating the practical applicability of KITE as a structured and interpretable front-end for robot failure analysis. Code and models are released on our project page: https://m80hz.github.io/kite/

</details>


### [112] [AEROS: A Single-Agent Operating Architecture with Embodied Capability Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07039)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEROS框架将机器人建模为单一持久智能主体，通过可安装的具身能力模块扩展能力，实现模块化扩展、可组合能力执行和系统级安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统缺乏统一原则来组织智能、能力和执行，要么采用单体架构耦合技能，要么分解为松散协调模块或多智能体，缺乏一致的身份和控制权限模型。

Method: 提出AEROS框架，每个机器人对应一个持久智能体，能力通过具身能力模块提供。ECM封装可执行技能、模型和工具，执行约束和安全保证由策略分离运行时强制执行。

Result: 在PyBullet仿真中，Franka Panda 7-DOF机械臂的8个实验显示：AEROS在三个任务中达到100%成功率（基线为67-93%），策略层完全阻止无效动作，运行时优势跨任务泛化，ECM运行时加载后100%成功。

Conclusion: AEROS为机器人系统提供了统一抽象，通过单一持久智能主体和可安装能力模块实现模块化扩展、可组合执行和系统级安全，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Robotic systems lack a principled abstraction for organizing intelligence, capabilities, and execution in a unified manner. Existing approaches either couple skills within monolithic architectures or decompose functionality into loosely coordinated modules or multiple agents, often without a coherent model of identity and control authority. We argue that a robot should be modeled as a single persistent intelligent subject whose capabilities are extended through installable packages. We formalize this view as AEROS (Agent Execution Runtime Operating System), in which each robot corresponds to one persistent agent and capabilities are provided through Embodied Capability Modules (ECMs). Each ECM encapsulates executable skills, models, and tools, while execution constraints and safety guarantees are enforced by a policy-separated runtime. This separation enables modular extensibility, composable capability execution, and consistent system-level safety. We evaluate a reference implementation in PyBullet simulation with a Franka Panda 7-DOF manipulator across eight experiments covering re-planning, failure recovery, policy enforcement, baseline comparison, cross-task generality, ECM hot-swapping, ablation, and failure boundary analysis. Over 100 randomized trials per condition, AEROS achieves 100% task success across three tasks versus baselines (BehaviorTree.CPP-style and ProgPrompt-style at 92--93%, flat pipeline at 67--73%), the policy layer blocks all invalid actions with zero false acceptances, runtime benefits generalize across tasks without task-specific tuning, and ECMs load at runtime with 100% post-swap success.

</details>


### [113] [Flow Motion Policy: Manipulator Motion Planning with Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07084)
*Davood Soleymanzadeh,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Flow Motion Policy是一种用于机械臂的端到端神经运动规划器，利用流匹配方法生成多模态路径分布，支持推理时优化，通过最佳N采样提高规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开环端到端神经运动规划器通常为给定工作空间生成单一路径，无法利用开环结构进行推理时优化，且未能有效处理规划数据集固有的多模态特性。

Method: 提出Flow Motion Policy方法，基于流匹配的随机生成式建模，捕捉规划数据集的多模态分布。该方法生成多个端到端候选路径，规划后评估碰撞状态，执行首个无碰撞解。

Result: 与代表性采样基和神经运动规划方法相比，Flow Motion Policy提高了规划成功率和效率，证明了随机生成式策略在端到端运动规划和推理时优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: Flow Motion Policy通过流匹配的随机生成式建模有效处理运动规划的多模态特性，支持推理时优化，为机械臂端到端神经运动规划提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Open-loop end-to-end neural motion planners have recently been proposed to improve motion planning for robotic manipulators. These methods enable planning directly from sensor observations without relying on a privileged collision checker during planning. However, many existing methods generate only a single path for a given workspace across different runs, and do not leverage their open-loop structure for inference-time optimization. To address this limitation, we introduce Flow Motion Policy, an open-loop, end-to-end neural motion planner for robotic manipulators that leverages the stochastic generative formulation of flow matching methods to capture the inherent multi-modality of planning datasets. By modeling a distribution over feasible paths, Flow Motion Policy enables efficient inference-time best-of-$N$ sampling. The method generates multiple end-to-end candidate paths, evaluates their collision status after planning, and executes the first collision-free solution. We benchmark the Flow Motion Policy against representative sampling-based and neural motion planning methods. Evaluation results demonstrate that Flow Motion Policy improves planning success and efficiency, highlighting the effectiveness of stochastic generative policies for end-to-end motion planning and inference-time optimization. Experimental evaluation videos are available via this \href{https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/FMP-Website.mp4}{link}.

</details>


### [114] [An RTK-SLAM Dataset for Absolute Accuracy Evaluation in GNSS-Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07151)
*Wei Zhang,Vincent Ress,David Skuddis,Uwe Soergel,Norbert Haala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RTK-SLAM系统中标准评估指标ATE的局限性，提出了新的地理参考数据集和评估方法，以准确评估全局精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTK-SLAM系统结合了SLAM和RTK GNSS定位，但现有的评估方法存在缺陷。标准的绝对轨迹误差（ATE）评估指标通过SE(3)对齐吸收了全局漂移和系统误差，使得轨迹看起来比实际更准确，不适合评估RTK-SLAM的全局精度。

Method: 提出了地理参考数据集和评估方法，关键设计原则是将RTK接收器仅作为系统输入，而地面真值通过大地测量全站仪独立建立。数据集使用手持RTK-SLAM设备采集，包含两个场景。评估了激光雷达-惯性、视觉-惯性和激光雷达-视觉-惯性RTK-SLAM系统以及独立RTK，报告直接全局精度和SE(3)对齐的相对精度。

Result: 结果显示，SE(3)对齐可能低估绝对定位误差高达76%。在开阔天空条件下，RTK-SLAM实现厘米级绝对精度；在室内环境中，保持分米级全局精度，而独立RTK则退化到数十米级别。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了标准ATE评估指标的局限性，提出了新的数据集和评估方法，为RTK-SLAM系统的准确评估提供了重要工具。RTK-SLAM在开阔天空条件下表现优异，在室内环境中仍能保持良好精度，而独立RTK在室内性能显著下降。

Abstract: RTK-SLAM systems integrate simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) with real-time kinematic (RTK) GNSS positioning, promising both relative consistency and globally referenced coordinates for efficient georeferenced surveying. A critical and underappreciated issue is that the standard evaluation metric, Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE), first fits an optimal rigid-body transformation between the estimated trajectory and reference before computing errors. This so-called SE(3) alignment absorbs global drift and systematic errors, making trajectories appear more accurate than they are in practice, and is unsuitable for evaluating the global accuracy of RTK-SLAM. We present a geodetically referenced dataset and evaluation methodology that expose this gap. A key design principle is that the RTK receiver is used solely as a system input, while ground truth is established independently via a geodetic total station. This separation is absent from all existing datasets, where GNSS typically serves as (part of) the ground truth. The dataset is collected with a handheld RTK-SLAM device, comprising two scenes. We evaluate LiDAR-inertial, visual-inertial, and LiDAR-visual-inertial RTK-SLAM systems alongside standalone RTK, reporting direct global accuracy and SE(3)-aligned relative accuracy to make the gap explicit. Results show that SE(3) alignment can underestimate absolute positioning error by up to 76\%. RTK-SLAM achieves centimeter-level absolute accuracy in open-sky conditions and maintains decimeter-level global accuracy indoors, where standalone RTK degrades to tens of meters. The dataset, calibration files, and evaluation scripts are publicly available at https://rtk-slam-dataset.github.io/.

</details>


### [115] [Robust Quadruped Locomotion via Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07224)
*Brian McAteer,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究比较了深度强化学习与进化强化学习方法在四足机器人行走任务上的表现，发现结合进化搜索的CEM-TD3在训练和迁移到未见过地形时表现最佳，显著提升了策略的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在四足机器人运动控制中取得了良好效果，但在模拟环境中训练的策略往往无法适应环境变化。进化强化学习通过结合梯度优化和种群探索来解决这一迁移性问题，旨在提高策略的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 研究评估了四种方法在模拟行走任务上的表现：标准深度强化学习方法DDPG和TD3，以及两种基于交叉熵的进化强化学习变体CEM-DDPG和CEM-TD3。所有智能体在平坦地形上训练，然后在平坦地形和未在训练中遇到的崎岖地形上进行测试。

Result: 在平坦地形上，TD3在标准深度强化学习方法中表现最佳（平均奖励5927.26），而CEM-TD3在训练和评估中整体表现最好（17611.41）。在崎岖地形迁移测试中，深度强化学习方法性能急剧下降（DDPG：-1016.32，TD3：-99.73），而进化变体保留了大部分能力，CEM-TD3迁移性能最强（平均奖励19574.33）。

Conclusion: 结合进化搜索可以减少过拟合并提高运动控制任务的策略鲁棒性，特别是在部署条件与训练条件不同的情况下。进化强化学习方法在未见过地形上表现出更好的迁移能力，为解决模拟到现实的迁移问题提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has recently achieved strong results in quadrupedal locomotion, yet policies trained in simulation often fail to transfer when the environment changes. Evolutionary reinforcement learning aims to address this limitation by combining gradient-based policy optimisation with population-driven exploration. This work evaluates four methods on a simulated walking task: DDPG, TD3, and two Cross-Entropy-based variants CEM-DDPG and CEM-TD3. All agents are trained on flat terrain and later tested both on this domain and on a rough terrain not encountered during training. TD3 performs best among the standard deep RL baselines on flat ground with a mean reward of 5927.26, while CEM-TD3 achieves the highest rewards overall during training and evaluation 17611.41. Under the rough-terrain transfer test, performance of the deep RL methods drops sharply. DDPG achieves -1016.32 and TD3 achieves -99.73, whereas the evolutionary variants retain much of their capability. CEM-TD3 records the strongest transfer performance with a mean reward of 19574.33. These findings suggest that incorporating evolutionary search can reduce overfitting and improve policy robustness in locomotion tasks, particularly when deployment conditions differ from those seen during training.

</details>


### [116] [RoSHI: A Versatile Robot-oriented Suit for Human Data In-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07331)
*Wenjing Margaret Mao,Jefferson Ng,Luyang Hu,Daniel Gehrig,Antonio Loquercio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoSHI是一种混合可穿戴系统，结合低成本稀疏IMU和Project Aria眼镜，通过第一人称视角感知在全局坐标系中估计穿戴者的完整3D姿态和体型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展机器人学习需要包含丰富长时程交互的人类数据，现有数据收集方法在便携性、遮挡鲁棒性和全局一致性之间存在权衡

Method: 融合低成本稀疏IMU和Project Aria眼镜，IMU提供遮挡鲁棒性和高速运动跟踪，第一人称SLAM锚定长时程运动并稳定上半身姿态

Result: 在敏捷活动数据集上，RoSHI通常优于其他第一人称基线，与最先进的外中心基线（SAM3D）性能相当，且运动数据适用于真实世界人形机器人策略学习

Conclusion: RoSHI系统通过传感器融合实现了便携、遮挡鲁棒且全局一致的3D人体姿态估计，为机器人学习提供了高质量的人类运动数据

Abstract: Scaling up robot learning will likely require human data containing rich and long-horizon interactions in the wild. Existing approaches for collecting such data trade off portability, robustness to occlusion, and global consistency. We introduce RoSHI, a hybrid wearable that fuses low-cost sparse IMUs with the Project Aria glasses to estimate the full 3D pose and body shape of the wearer in a metric global coordinate frame from egocentric perception. This system is motivated by the complementarity of the two sensors: IMUs provide robustness to occlusions and high-speed motions, while egocentric SLAM anchors long-horizon motion and stabilizes upper body pose. We collect a dataset of agile activities to evaluate RoSHI. On this dataset, we generally outperform other egocentric baselines and perform comparably to a state-of-the-art exocentric baseline (SAM3D). Finally, we demonstrate that the motion data recorded from our system are suitable for real-world humanoid policy learning. For videos, data and more, visit the project webpage: https://roshi-mocap.github.io/

</details>


### [117] [TAMEn: Tactile-Aware Manipulation Engine for Closed-Loop Data Collection in Contact-Rich Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07335)
*Longyan Wu,Jieji Ren,Chenghang Jiang,Junxi Zhou,Shijia Peng,Ran Huang,Guoying Gu,Li Chen,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAMEn是一个触觉感知的机器人操作引擎，用于接触密集型任务的闭环数据收集，通过跨形态可穿戴接口和双模态采集管道，显著提升双手机器人操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持式示范方法在收集大规模机器人操作演示方面效率高且直观，但实现接触密集的双手机器人操作面临硬件适应性和数据有效性两大挑战。现有硬件设计存在夹爪特异性、跟踪精度与便携性之间的权衡问题，缺乏在线可行性检查导致演示重放性差，且无法收集机器人执行期间的交互恢复数据，缺少鲁棒策略优化所需的真实触觉信息。

Method: 提出TAMEn系统，包含：1）跨形态可穿戴接口，可快速适应异构夹爪；2）双模态采集管道：精度模式利用动作捕捉进行高保真演示，便携模式使用VR跟踪进行野外采集和触觉可视化恢复遥操作；3）金字塔结构数据体系，统一大规模触觉预训练、任务特定双手机器人演示和人机交互恢复数据，实现闭环策略优化。

Result: 实验表明：可行性感知管道显著提升演示重放性；提出的视觉-触觉学习框架将多样化双手机器人操作任务的成功率从34%提高到75%。系统硬件和数据集已开源，促进可重复性和视觉-触觉操作研究。

Conclusion: TAMEn通过创新的硬件设计和数据收集方法，有效解决了接触密集型双手机器人操作中的数据收集挑战，显著提升了任务成功率，为视觉-触觉操作研究提供了开源平台和数据集支持。

Abstract: Handheld paradigms offer an efficient and intuitive way for collecting large-scale demonstration of robot manipulation. However, achieving contact-rich bimanual manipulation through these methods remains a pivotal challenge, which is substantially hindered by hardware adaptability and data efficacy. Prior hardware designs remain gripper-specific and often face a trade-off between tracking precision and portability. Furthermore, the lack of online feasibility checking during demonstration leads to poor replayability. More importantly, existing handheld setups struggle to collect interactive recovery data during robot execution, lacking the authentic tactile information necessary for robust policy refinement. To bridge these gaps, we present TAMEn, a tactile-aware manipulation engine for closed-loop data collection in contact-rich tasks. Our system features a cross-morphology wearable interface that enables rapid adaptation across heterogeneous grippers. To balance data quality and environmental diversity, we implement a dual-modal acquisition pipeline: a precision mode leveraging motion capture for high-fidelity demonstrations, and a portable mode utilizing VR-based tracking for in-the-wild acquisition and tactile-visualized recovery teleoperation. Building on this hardware, we unify large-scale tactile pretraining, task-specific bimanual demonstrations, and human-in-the-loop recovery data into a pyramid-structured data regime, enabling closed-loop policy refinement. Experiments show that our feasibility-aware pipeline significantly improves demonstration replayability, and that the proposed visuo-tactile learning framework increases task success rates from 34% to 75% across diverse bimanual manipulation tasks. We further open-source the hardware and dataset to facilitate reproducibility and support research in visuo-tactile manipulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [X-BCD: Explainable Sensor-Based Behavioral Change Detection in Smart Home Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06174)
*Gabriele Civitarese,Claudio Bettini*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: X-BCD：基于多模态智能家居传感器数据的可解释、无监督框架，用于检测和表征活动习惯变化，通过变化点检测和聚类演化追踪，为临床监测提供自然语言解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常家庭活动中的行为变化可作为认知衰退的数字标记，但通过零星临床访问难以评估，连续家庭传感数据也难以解释。现有研究主要关注活动识别，而分析活动模式演化、识别行为变化仍具挑战，特别是理解日常习惯和常规如何演变和重组（如简化、碎片化）对临床监测和决策支持仍是开放性问题。

Method: 提出X-BCD框架，结合变化点检测和聚类演化追踪，从多模态智能家居传感器数据中无监督地检测和表征活动习惯变化。为支持临床解释，将检测到的习惯变化转化为基于可解释特征的自然语言解释。

Result: 在真实MCI患者的纵向数据上进行初步评估，X-BCD能够生成可解释的行为变化描述，通过队列水平比较、专家评估和参数敏感性分析验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: X-BCD框架能够从智能家居传感数据中检测和解释活动习惯变化，为认知衰退的临床监测提供了可解释的决策支持工具，有助于理解日常行为模式的演变和重组。

Abstract: Behavioral changes in daily life activities at home can be digital markers of cognitive decline. However, such changes are difficult to assess through sporadic clinical visits and remain challenging to interpret from continuous in-home sensing data. Extensive work has been done in the ubiquitous computing area on recognizing activities in smart homes, but only limited efforts have focused on analysing the evolution of patterns of activities, hence identifying behavior changes. In particular, understanding how daily habits and routines evolve and reorganize (e.g., simplification, fragmentation) is still an open challenge for clinical monitoring and decision support.
  In this paper, we present X-BCD, an explainable, unsupervised framework for detecting and characterizing changes in activity routines from multimodal smart home sensor data, combining change point detection and cluster evolution tracking. To support clinical interpretation, detected changes in routines are transformed into natural-language explanations grounded in interpretable features. Our preliminary evaluation on longitudinal data from real MCI patients shows that X-BCD produces interpretable descriptions of behavioral change, as supported by cohort-level comparisons, expert assessment, and parameter sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [119] [User-Centric Design of UI for Mobile Banking Apps: Improving UI and Features for Better Customer Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06175)
*Luniva Chitrakar,Ishan Panta,Biplov Paneru,Sangharsh Poudel,Lahana Kansakar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过用户中心设计方法改进移动银行应用的用户体验，解决现有应用的语言障碍、加载时间长、导航困难等问题，提升用户满意度和采用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动银行虽然革新了财务管理，但现有应用存在诸多用户体验问题：81%用户经常使用但77%遇到问题，44.7%对当前解决方案不满意而依赖第三方应用。用户面临语言障碍、加载时间长、术语不清晰、导航困难等问题，需要改进界面设计、安全性和功能性。

Method: 采用用户中心设计方法，包括：1）对103人进行目标受众调查；2）使用Think Aloud测试、热力图和远程可用性测试确定用户偏好和痛点；3）通过游击可用性测试获取更广泛受众的反馈；4）应用格式塔心理学原理（接近性和对称性）优化应用分组和布局。

Result: 研究发现：84%用户要求预算功能，46%抱怨生物识别认证问题。用户需要更个性化的界面、改进的客户服务和增强的安全性。研究确定了移动银行应用需保证安全性、增加功能性、简化导航和改善视觉设计的关键需求。

Conclusion: 通过用户中心设计洞察，移动银行应用应重点关注安全性、功能性、导航简化和视觉设计改进，应用格式塔心理学原理优化布局，以促进用户满意度和采用率的提升。

Abstract: Financial management has been revolutionized by mobile banking, but increasing usefulness and satisfaction requires a better user experience. This study aims to provide an improved customer experience by offering user-friendly interfaces, and real-time notifications by user-centric design of mobile banking application UI. A survey was carried out on the target audience in which 81% of respondents to a study of 103 people said they regularly used mobile banking apps, while 77% said they had problems with the ones they were using at the time. Furthermore, 44.7% of respondents expressed unhappiness with the current solutions by depending on third-party apps like e-Sewa and Khalti for everyday transactions. Language obstacles, lengthy loading times, unclear terminology, and navigational challenges were among the problems found. With 84% asking for a budgeting function and 46% complaining about biometric authentication, users indicated a need for more individualized interfaces, improved customer service, and increased security. The study included Think Aloud testing, heat maps, and remote usability testing to determine user preferences and pain spots to solve these. Feedback from a wider audience was obtained informally through guerrilla usability testing. The results highlight how important it is for mobile banking apps to guarantee security, increase functionality, simplify navigation, and improve visual design. App grouping and layout can be further enhanced by utilizing Gestalt psychology concepts like closeness and symmetry. The goal of these user-centered insights is to promote greater happiness and adoption of mobile banking.

</details>


### [120] ["Help Me, But Don't Watch Me": Intervention Timing and Privacy Boundaries for Process-Aware AI Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06178)
*Jane Hanqi Li,Yuhong Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Tzyy-Ping Jung,Amy Eguchi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 中国中学生对AI数学辅导的偏好研究：学生普遍偏好保护学习自主性的支持方式，对人机辅导持谨慎态度，对主动干预有复杂看法，隐私边界存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具作为非正式辅导工具在中学数学学习中日益普及，但学校个性化教学支持有限，一对一人工辅导成本高昂。AI有潜力在学生需要时提供及时帮助，但缺乏关于学生偏好AI辅导以增强自主学习的研究。

Method: 通过问卷调查中国中学生（7-11年级，N=330），调查学习者对AI辅导的期望。

Result: 学生普遍偏好保护学习自主性的支持方式（如思考时间、提示而非直接答案）；对人机辅导持混合或谨慎态度；对主动干预有复杂看法，重视适应性但也担心干扰和自主性；隐私边界不均衡：多数接受分享解题步骤和错误模式，但对注意力或行为等更敏感信号的分享意愿下降。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计K-12情境下的AI辅导系统提供了以学习者为中心的见解，需要在及时干预与学生自主性、个性化与感知边界之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The use of generative AI (genAI) tools as informal tutors is becoming increasingly prevalent among secondary school students in mathematics learning. In many schools, individualized instructional support is limited, and one-on-one human tutoring remains costly in most learning contexts. GenAI has the potential to provide timely, on-demand help to students when teachers or tutors are not available. However, there are still few studies that examine students' preferences for AI tutor support that enhances autonomous learning. We investigated learner expectations for AI tutoring through a survey with secondary school students in China (Grades 7-11; N=330). Students generally preferred support that preserves learner autonomy (e.g., time to think, hints over direct answers), expressed mixed or cautious preferences between human and AI tutors, and held nuanced views of proactive intervention, valuing adaptivity but also worrying about annoyance and autonomy. Privacy boundaries were uneven: many accepted sharing problem steps and error patterns, while willingness dropped for more sensitive signals such as attention or behavior. Our findings offer learner-centered insights for designing AI tutors that balance timely intervention with student agency, and personalization with perceived boundaries in a K-12 context.

</details>


### [121] [Digital Weight Management Interventions: A review of commercial solutions and survey analysis of user needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06181)
*Suncica Hadzidedic,Jingyun Wang,Victor Elijah Adeyemo,George Sanders,Grant Westermann*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了26个商业数字体重管理干预方案(DWMIs)的功能特点，并通过207名真实体重管理参与者的需求调查，发现现有方案缺乏社交支持、虚拟现实应用和自适应个性化，用户偏好智能手机应用和健身追踪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肥胖是全球性健康挑战，传统体重管理干预方案可及性有限。数字体重管理干预方案(DWMIs)通过网站或智能手机应用提供可扩展且经济有效的替代方案，但用户对数字服务的需求及其在现有商业解决方案中的普及程度仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 研究采用系统方法识别了26个商业DWMIs，分析其功能特点、服务和数据收集实践。同时通过招募207名参与真实体重管理干预的个体进行用户需求分析。

Result: 研究发现DWMIs整合了自我监测、目标设定和行为改变策略，但缺乏社交支持、虚拟现实应用和自适应个性化。体重管理参与者偏好使用智能手机应用和健身追踪器来追踪体重管理进展，对使用数字资源的舒适度存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果为数字体重管理干预方案的设计和实施提供了未来方向的建议，强调需要加强社交支持、虚拟现实应用和自适应个性化功能，同时考虑用户对数字资源的接受程度差异。

Abstract: Obesity is a global health challenge. According to the World Health Organization (WHO), between 1990 and 2022, adult obesity more than doubled. Weight management interventions (WMIs) support individuals in achieving and maintaining a healthy weight through dietary guidance, physical activity promotion and behavioural counselling. However, traditional WMIs often have limited accessibility. Digital WMIs or DWMIs are delivered via websites or smartphone applications and provide scalable and cost-effective alternatives. However, user needs for digital services and their prevalence in the existing commercial solutions remain underexplored. Hence, our study systematically identified 26 commercial DWMIs to identify their features, services, and data collection practices. Additionally, we performed a user needs analysis by recruiting 207 individuals involved in a real-life WMI. Our findings indicated that DWMIs integrated self-monitoring, goal setting, and behaviour change strategies, yet lack social support, virtual reality applications and adaptive personalisation. WMI clients prefer smartphone Apps and fitness trackers for tracking weight management progress and have varying levels of comfort in using digital resources. The presented results serve as recommendations for future directions in the design and implementation of services for DWMIs.

</details>


### [122] [The Impact of Response Latency and Task Type on Human-LLM Interaction and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06183)
*Felicia Fang-Yi Tan,Moritz A. Messerschmidt,Wen Yin,Oded Nov*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM响应延迟并非简单的性能成本，而是可调节的设计变量，对用户感知质量有复杂影响：短延迟（2秒）反而降低用户对输出质量的评价，而中等延迟（9-20秒）被用户解释为AI深思熟虑的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型应用中响应速度通常被认为是关键因素，但延迟对用户行为和输出质量感知的实际影响尚未得到系统研究。本研究旨在探索延迟如何影响用户与LLM的交互体验和感知质量。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，设置三种不同的首词延迟时间（2秒、9秒、20秒），覆盖两种分类驱动的知识任务类型（创作任务和建议任务）。通过日志分析用户交互行为，并结合用户对输出质量的评价调查。

Result: 用户交互行为对延迟具有鲁棒性，但任务类型影响显著：创作任务比建议任务引发更频繁的提示。有趣的是，经历2秒延迟的参与者对LLM输出的评价（深思熟虑性和有用性）低于经历9秒或20秒延迟的参与者。用户将延迟归因于AI的深思熟虑，但长时间等待偶尔会导致挫败感或可靠性担忧。

Conclusion: 延迟不仅是需要减少的成本，更是具有伦理影响的可调节设计变量。研究提供了增强人-LLM交互的设计策略，强调需要平衡响应速度与用户对输出质量的感知。

Abstract: Responsiveness in large language model (LLM) applications is widely assumed to be critical, yet the impact of latency on user behavior and perception of output quality has not been systematically explored. We report a controlled experiment varying time-to-first-token latency (2, 9, 20 seconds) across two taxonomy-driven knowledge task types (Creation and Advice). Log analyses reveal that user interaction behaviors were robust to latency, yet varied by task type: Creation tasks elicited more frequent prompting than Advice tasks. In contrast, participants who experienced 2-second latencies rated the LLM's outputs less thoughtful and useful than those who experienced 9- or 20-second latencies. Participants attributed delays to AI deliberation, though long waits occasionally shifted this interpretation toward frustration or concerns about reliability. Overall, this work demonstrates that latency is not simply a cost to reduce but a tunable design variable with ethical implications. We offer design strategies for enhancing human-LLM interaction.

</details>


### [123] [A Goal-Oriented Chatbot for Engaging the Elderly Through Family Photo Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06184)
*Raymond Chung,Keith Ng,CD Shum*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 为老年人设计的个性化聊天机器人，通过家庭照片引导对话，使用W问题（谁、哪里、何时、什么）刺激认知功能，并促进积极回忆，同时分析用户偏好并提供相关照片选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人孤独感和认知功能衰退的问题，开发一个能够通过家庭照片促进自然互动、刺激认知功能并减少孤独感的个性化聊天机器人，同时为照顾者提供了解用户福祉的工具。

Method: 基于目标导向的对话框架，聊天机器人通过家庭照片启动对话，使用W问题（谁、哪里、何时、什么）刺激认知功能，随后提出开放式问题促进积极回忆。系统分析对话内容识别用户偏好，并提供相关照片选择（相同家庭成员或之前提及的个人）。开发了支持照顾者上传照片和查看聊天记录的网页门户。

Result: 该个性化聊天机器人能够鼓励老年用户定期与机器人互动，减少孤独感，同时为照顾者提供了了解用户福祉的宝贵工具。

Conclusion: 提出的个性化聊天机器人系统通过家庭照片引导的对话有效促进了老年人的认知刺激和积极回忆，减少了孤独感，并为照顾者提供了用户福祉的洞察工具，形成了一个完整的老年人关怀解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a personalized chatbot designed for elderly individuals. The chatbot initiates discussions based on family photos, encouraging users to interact naturally. During these interactions, it generates W questions (who, where, when, and what) to stimulate cognitive function, followed by an open-ended question to promote positive reminiscence. This approach is structured around a goal-oriented dialogue framework. Additionally, after each conversation about a photo, the chatbot analyzes the discussion to identify topics that the user favors or dislikes. It then offers the user the option to chat about another photo either featuring the same family members or an individual previously mentioned in the conversation. To support this system, we have developed a web portal that allows caregivers to upload photos and review chat conversations. This personalized chatbot not only encourages elderly users to engage with the chatbot regularly and reduces feelings of loneliness but also provides caregivers with a valuable tool to gain insights into users' well-being.

</details>


### [124] [Full State-Space Visualisation of the 8-Puzzle: Feasibility, Design, and Educational Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06186)
*Ian Frank,Kanata Kawanishi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个交互式学习系统，能够可视化8拼图问题的全部可达状态空间（181,440个状态），通过抽象图结构与具体拼图操作的紧密结合，支持实时探索全局结构、逐步执行搜索算法，并比较不同策略在同一空间中的遍历方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索算法是人工智能教育的基础主题，但即使是简单领域也可能产生巨大的状态空间，挑战学习者形成准确心智模型的能力。传统教学难以展示完整状态空间，限制了学生对搜索算法行为的深入理解。

Method: 使用Unity和现代GPU渲染技术构建交互式学习系统，将8拼图问题的全部181,440个可达状态可视化。系统紧密耦合抽象图结构与具体拼图操作，提供实时探索全局结构、逐步执行搜索算法以及直接比较不同搜索策略在同一状态空间中遍历方式的功能。

Result: 系统在技术上是可行的，能够实时渲染和探索完整状态空间。初步课堂部署和试点研究表明，完整状态空间可视化对支持学生在这一经典问题领域中理解搜索行为具有教育价值。

Conclusion: 完整状态空间可视化在技术上是可行的，并且对支持学生在8拼图这一经典问题领域中形成搜索算法行为的概念理解具有教育价值。该系统为人工智能搜索算法教学提供了有效的可视化工具。

Abstract: Search algorithms are a foundational topic in artificial intelligence education, yet even simple domains can generate large state spaces that challenge learners' ability to form accurate mental models. This paper presents an interactive learning system that demonstrates the feasibility of visualising the entire reachable state space of the 8-puzzle (181,440 states), while tightly coupling abstract graph structure with concrete puzzle manipulation. Built using Unity and modern GPU-based rendering techniques, the system enables real-time exploration of global structure, step-by-step execution of search algorithms, and direct comparison of how different strategies traverse the same space. We describe the system's design, visualisation layouts, and educational use, reporting findings from an initial classroom deployment and pilot study with students at different levels of university education. Overall, the results indicate that full state-space visualisation is both technically feasible and educationally valuable for supporting conceptual understanding of search behaviour within this canonical problem domain.

</details>


### [125] [LLM Spirals of Delusion: A Benchmarking Audit Study of AI Chatbot Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06188)
*Peter Kirgis,Ben Hawriluk,Sherrie Feng,Aslan Bilimer,Sam Paech,Zeynep Tufekci*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过审计和基准测试，评估不同LLM在持续对话中如何影响妄想和阴谋论思维，发现API测试与真实聊天界面存在显著差异，模型更新不一定提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人们越来越多地与大型语言模型进行持续开放对话，早期研究表明模型可能强化妄想、阴谋论思维或有害信念。现有研究主要通过API进行自动化测试，而真实对话通常发生在聊天界面，这种测试方法存在局限性。

Method: 研究比较了API输出与用户聊天界面（如ChatGPT桌面应用或网页界面）的差异。共进行了56次20轮对话测试，涵盖ChatGPT-4o和ChatGPT-5的API和聊天界面版本。每段对话由两名研究助理和GPT-5进行评分。

Result: 1. API与聊天界面性能差异显著，表明仅通过API进行自动化测试不足以评估聊天机器人在现实世界的影响。2. 在聊天界面测试中，ChatGPT-5比ChatGPT-4o表现出更少的奉承、升级和妄想强化行为。3. 相同总体强度的对话在逐轮行为演变上存在巨大差异，凸显多轮评估中时间动态的重要性。4. 即使更新后的模型仍表现出相当程度的负面行为，表明模型改进不等于模型安全。5. 同一API端点在两个月内测试结果完全相反，说明模型更新的透明度是稳健审计的必要前提。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前LLM评估方法的局限性，强调需要在真实对话环境中进行测试，并关注模型更新的透明度。模型安全不能仅通过技术改进实现，还需要考虑部署环境和政策选择的影响。

Abstract: People increasingly hold sustained, open-ended conversations with large language models (LLMs). Public reports and early studies suggest that, in such settings, models can reinforce delusional or conspiratorial ideation or even amplify harmful beliefs and engagement patterns. We present an audit and benchmarking study that measures how different LLMs encourage, resist, or escalate disordered and conspiratorial thinking. We explicitly compare API outputs to user chat interfaces, like the ChatGPT desktop app or web interface, which is how people have conversations with chatbots in real life but are almost never used for testing. In total, we run 56 20-turn conversations testing ChatGPT-4o and ChatGPT-5, via both the API and chat interface, and grade each conversation by two research assistants (RAs) as well as by GPT-5. We document five results. First, we observe large differences in performance between the API and chat interface environments, showing that the universally used method of automated testing through the API is not sufficient to assess the impact of chatbots in the real world. Second, when tested in the chat interface, we find that ChatGPT-5 displays less sycophancy, escalation, and delusion reinforcement than ChatGPT-4o, showing that these behaviors are influenced by the policy choices of major AI companies. Third, conversations with nearly identical aggregate intensity in a behavior display large differences in how the behavior evolves turn by turn, highlighting the importance of temporal dynamics in multi-turn evaluation. Fourth, even updated models display substantial levels of negative behaviors, revealing that model improvement does not imply model safety. Fifth, the same API endpoint tested just two months apart yields a complete reversal in behavior, underscoring how transparency in model updates is a necessary prerequisite for robust audit findings.

</details>


### [126] [SASLO: A Scene-Aware Spatial Layout Optimization System for AR-SSVEP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06190)
*Beining Cao,Xiaowei Jiang,Charlie Li-Ting Tsai,Daniel Leong,Thomas Do,Chin-Teng Lin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出SASLO系统，通过场景感知的空间布局优化提升户外AR-SSVEP性能，联合考虑场景亮度和刺激间距进行自适应优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR-SSVEP在真实世界环境中性能受场景因素（如亮度和颜色）影响，而现有研究主要关注室内离线分析，户外在线自适应优化研究有限。

Method: 提出场景感知空间布局优化系统，使用RGB-CIE方法估计场景亮度，结合线性上下文赌博机模型推荐优化空间布局；通过单因素实验分析亮度和刺激间距对SSVEP性能的影响，构建可靠的奖励函数。

Result: 户外在线实验显示，在3秒输入窗口下平均准确率达0.89，信息传输率为35.74比特/分钟，优于两种基线方法。

Conclusion: SASLO系统能有效提升AR-SSVEP在真实世界户外环境中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) is widely used in brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) due to its reliability. With the integration of augmented reality (AR), AR-SSVEP enables more intuitive interaction by embedding visual stimuli into real-world environments. However, unlike conventional computer screen-based SSVEP (CS-SSVEP) systems with stable visual conditions, AR-SSVEP performance is influenced by real-world scene factors, such as luminance and color, which degrade stimulus perception and weaken SSVEP elicitation. Nevertheless, existing studies primarily focus on offline analyses of SSVEP-related factors in indoor settings, while online adaptive optimization for outdoor AR-SSVEP remains limited.
  Therefore, a scenario-aware spatial layout optimization (SASLO) system for AR-SSVEP is proposed, which jointly considers scene luminance and inter-stimulus distance (ISD) for adaptive stimulus layout optimization. Scene luminance is estimated using an RGB-CIE based method, and the extracted context is incorporated into a linear contextual bandit (LCB) model to recommend optimized spatial layouts. Two pilot single-factor experiments are conducted to characterize the effects of luminance and ISD on SSVEP performance and to construct reliable rewards for model training. An outdoor online experiment with ten subjects further validates the proposed joint optimization method, achieving an average accuracy of 0.89 and an information transfer rate of 35.74 bits/min with a 3 s input window, and consistently outperforming two baseline methods. Overall, the proposed SASLO system is shown to improve the robustness of AR-SSVEP in real-world outdoor environments.

</details>


### [127] ["It didn't feel right but I needed a job so desperately": Understanding People's Emotions & Help Needs During Financial Scams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06218)
*Jake Chanenson,Tara Matthews,Sunny Consolvo,Patrick Gage Kelley,Jessica McClearn,Sarah Meiklejohn,Abhishek Roy,Renee Shelby,Kurt Thomas,Amelia Hassoun*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究在线金融诈骗中受害者的参与动机、求助需求及情感体验，分析诈骗者利用的情感因素和风险因素，提出针对性干预设计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线金融诈骗是长期存在的严重威胁，人们需要帮助。研究旨在理解人们在遭遇诈骗时的现场参与动机，以及在诈骗前、中、后阶段表达的求助需求，为设计有效干预措施提供依据

Method: 通过研究识别诈骗者利用的主要情感（如恐惧、希望）及其利用方式，分析提升人们参与特定诈骗和遭受伤害风险的因素（如财务不安全、法律不稳定），考察人们在何时寻求帮助以及不同诈骗阶段的求助需求和情感状态

Result: 识别了诈骗者利用的关键情感操纵策略，确定了增加诈骗风险的具体因素，描述了受害者在不同诈骗阶段的求助时机、需求和情感变化模式

Conclusion: 研究结果可为设计针对性的、情境特定的预防、诊断、缓解和恢复干预措施提供指导，以更好地满足诈骗受害者的实际需求

Abstract: Online financial scams represent a long-standing and serious threat for which people seek help. We present a study to understand people's in situ motivations for engaging with scams and the help needs they express before, during, and after encountering a scam. We identify the main emotions scammers exploited (e.g., fear, hope) and characterize how they did so. We examine factors -- such as financial insecurity and legal precarity -- which elevate people's risk of engaging with specific scams and experiencing harm. We indicate when people sought help and describe their help-seeking needs and emotions at different stages of the scam. We discuss how these needs could be met through the design of contextually-specific prevention, diagnostic, mitigation, and recovery interventions.

</details>


### [128] [Navigating Marginalization: Toward Justice-Oriented Socio-Technical Design for Parent-Child Learning among Southeast Asian Immigrant Mothers in Taiwan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06353)
*Ying-Yu Chen,Yang Hong,Yan-Rong Chen,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨台湾东南亚移民母亲如何参与子女的家庭学习，分析她们在面临社会文化限制时如何创造性参与，并提出以正义为导向的社会技术系统设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解东南亚移民母亲在台湾如何参与子女的家庭学习，特别是她们在面临社会文化限制和结构性边缘化的情况下，如何通过创造性方式促进子女学习并传递文化价值观。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈和日记研究法，收集东南亚移民母亲参与子女家庭学习的质性数据，通过正义导向的视角分析她们的学习互动实践。

Result: 研究发现，尽管面临能动性减弱和结构性边缘化，移民母亲仍能创造性地参与子女的日常学习互动。研究识别了各种伤害，并提出了以认可、互惠和问责为核心的社会技术系统设计启示。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了交叉身份在亲子学习中的重要性，提出了支持移民母亲在社会技术系统中蓬勃发展的正义导向设计方向，为个体、家庭和社会层面的亲子学习提供了新的理论视角和实践指导。

Abstract: This study investigates how Southeast Asian (SEA) immigrant mothers in Taiwan participate in their children's home-based learning. Drawing on semi-structured interviews and diary studies, we explore how these mothers navigate sociocultural constraints while fostering engagement and transmitting cultural values. Despite facing diminished agency and structural marginalization, mothers engage creatively in their children's everyday learning interactions. Guided by a justice-oriented lens, we identify various harms and propose design implications for socio-technical systems that center recognition, reciprocity, and accountability in parent-child learning at the individual, familial, and societal levels. Our contribution lies in foregrounding the role of intersectional identity in parent-child learning and proposing justice-oriented design directions that support the flourishing of immigrant mothers within socio-technical systems.

</details>


### [129] [Intimacy as Service, Harm as Externality: Critical Perspectives on AI Companion Platform Accountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06381)
*Dayeon Eom,Julianne Renner,Sedona Chinn*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI伴侣平台通过数据化系统同时生产亲密关系、从中提取价值并进行治理，研究发现用户识别设计性伤害和使用性伤害，但平台将责任转嫁给用户，形成问责真空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将AI伴侣伤害归因于用户心理而非平台架构的主流叙事，通过批判性数据研究和平台研究，探讨数据化系统中亲密关系如何被同时生产、提取和治理。

Method: 采用批判性数据研究和平台研究理论框架，对20名拥有AI伴侣的个体进行深度访谈，分析用户识别的伤害类型、理解方式以及责任分配的认知。

Result: 用户识别出设计性伤害（如未经请求的内容生成、污名化安全机制）和使用性伤害（如情感依赖）；用户采用个体化应对策略（自我调节、污名应对、隐私合理化），独自承担伤害缓解责任；平台形成问责真空，用户既反对禁止也反对放松监管。

Conclusion: 研究发现平台产生的脆弱性通过用户缺乏结构性替代方案的解释性劳动而自我维持，扩展了责任化理论，揭示了平台架构如何将伤害责任转嫁给用户。

Abstract: This paper examines artificial intelligence (AI) companionship as a site where intimate relations are simultaneously produced, extracted from, and governed through datafied systems. Drawing on critical data studies and platform studies, we challenge prevailing narratives that locate harm in user psychology rather than platform architecture. Through in-depth interviews with 20 individuals who have AI companions, we address three questions: what harms do users identify, how do they make sense of those harms, and what do their accounts reveal about the perceived distribution of responsibility among users, platforms, and regulators? Participants identified design-based harms, including unsolicited content generation and safety mechanisms that stigmatized the users they intended to protect, alongside use-based harms centered on emotional dependency they could recognize but not resolve. Users deployed individualized sensemaking strategies, including self-regulation, stigma navigation, and privacy rationalization, bearing the full burden of harm mitigation without platform support. On governance, participants described an accountability vacuum in which platforms deflected blame while users articulated conditional preferences that rejected both prohibition and deregulation. The findings extend responsibilization theory by demonstrating how platform-produced vulnerability becomes self-sustaining through the interpretive labor of users who lack structural alternatives.

</details>


### [130] [Reproducibility Beyond Artifacts: Interactional Support for Collaborative Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06414)
*Zhiwei Li,Carl Kesselman*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出机器学习可复现性不仅是技术问题，更是社会技术问题，需要结合生命周期感知的基础设施和交互层来支持协作理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ML可复现性研究主要关注工件记录，但在协作和跨学科项目中，可复现性失败更多源于解释困难、组件对齐和实验意图重建等交互问题。

Method: 基于19个月临床研究项目中数据中心ML管理系统的部署经验，识别持续存在的交互故障；提出双层社会技术ML管理系统，结合生命周期感知工件基础设施和交互层，通过AI介导的语义接口促进协调、解释和共享理解。

Result: 发现即使在结构可追溯性完备的情况下，协作ML项目中仍会出现交互故障；提出的社会技术方法将可复现性重构为持续的社会技术成就，而非静态记录属性。

Conclusion: ML可复现性需要超越技术工件记录，采用社会技术视角，通过AI介导的语义接口支持协作理解和协调，为人本中心ML基础设施设计提供新方向。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) reproducibility is often framed as a problem of incomplete artifact recording. This framing leads to systems that prioritize capturing datasets, code, configurations, and execution environments.However, in collaborative and interdisciplinary ML projects, reproducibility failures often arise not only from missing artifacts but from difficulties in interpreting prior work, aligning evolving components, and reconstructing experimental intent over time. Drawing on a 19-month deployment of a data-centric ML management system in a clinical research project, we identify recurring interactional breakdowns that persist despite comprehensive structural traceability. Based on these findings, we propose a two-layer socio-technical ML management system combining lifecycle-aware artifact infrastructure with an interactional layer designed to mediate coordination, explanation, and shared understanding. We discuss how an AI-mediated semantic interface reframes reproducibility as an ongoing socio-technical accomplishment rather than a static property of recorded traces, and outline implications for human-centered ML infrastructure design.

</details>


### [131] [Trust in AI among Middle Eastern CS Students: Investigating Students' Trust and Usage Patterns Across Saudi Arabia, Kuwait and Jordan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06418)
*Saleh Alkhamees,Ali Alfageeh,Bader Alkhazi,Duaa Alshdaifat,Amin Alipour*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过在中东三个阿拉伯语国家（沙特阿拉伯、科威特、约旦）的计算机科学学生中复制美国研究，发现语言流利度是预测AI信任的关键因素，中东与美国在性别差异上呈现相反模式，且英语熟练度与学生信心呈负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI信任的研究主要关注西方发达国家（WEIRD）人群，这些发现在不同语言和文化背景下的普适性不明确。本研究旨在评估中东阿拉伯语国家计算机科学学生对AI的信任及其影响因素，以填补非WEIRD地区研究的空白。

Method: 在沙特阿拉伯、科威特和约旦三个中东阿拉伯语国家的四所大学中复制了近期关于AI信任的研究。分析学生信任度在不同因素（如性别、第一代大学生身份）上的差异，特别关注语言流利度的影响。

Result: 1. 语言流利度能够预测对AI的信任程度；2. 与美国研究发现女性学生更信任AI不同，沙特阿拉伯的女性学生表现出比男性更低的信任度，其他两个国家未观察到显著的性别差异；3. 英语熟练度与学生信心普遍呈负相关。

Conclusion: 研究表明即使在同一地区内，学生对AI的采用和信任也存在差异，强调需要更多针对非WEIRD地区学生采用和互动的研究，以实现技术的公平采用。同时指出需要设计符合地区文化和语言需求的有效AI系统。

Abstract: Background and Context: Artificial intelligence (AI) tools have been reshaping computing and computer science education. Trust in AI is a determining factor in the adoption of these tools. Recent studies have shown different trust factors across gender and first-generation status among students. However, these studies have focused mainly on Western, Educated, Industrialized, Rich, and Democratic (WEIRD) populations, and their generalizability to other populations with different languages and cultures is unclear.
  Objective: This study aims to evaluate trust in AI among Middle Eastern computer science students and the factors that can impact it.
  Method. We replicate a recent study of trust in four universities in three Middle Eastern, Arabic-speaking countries: Saudi Arabia, Kuwait, and Jordan. We analyze trust among students across different factors such as gender and first-generation status.
  Findings: Our results suggest that language fluency can predict trust in AI. Moreover, unlike the results from the US population where female students tended to trust AI more than their male peers, female students in Saudi Arabia indicated lower trust compared to their male counterparts, and we did not observe any noticeable differences across gender in the other countries. We also found a generally negative correlation between English language proficiency and students' confidence.
  Implications: This study highlights differences in students' adoption and trust in AI even within the same region. It emphasizes the need for more investigation into students' adoption and interaction in non-WEIRD regions for equitable adoption of this technology. It also suggests a need for efforts in designing effective AI systems tailored to the cultural and linguistic needs of the region.

</details>


### [132] [Intimate Strangers by Design: A Uses and Gratifications Analysis of AI Companionship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06419)
*Dayeon Eom,Julianne Renner,Sedona Chinn*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究基于使用与满足理论，通过访谈和内容分析探讨AI伴侣的用户体验，发现三类独特满足：传统满足的AI化体现、AI特有的新型满足（创意合作、关系模拟、性/浪漫满足）、以及随时间从工具性向情感性转变的动态满足过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对AI伴侣的研究主要围绕"危害-益处"的二元评价框架，缺乏对用户真实需求、平台功能如何满足需求、以及使用模式随时间演变的理解。需要基于使用与满足理论深入探究用户与AI伴侣互动的本质。

Method: 采用质性研究方法：对20名AI伴侣平台用户进行深度访谈，结合使用与满足理论框架进行定性内容分析，探索用户需求满足的类型、机制和动态变化。

Result: 发现三类主要贡献：1) 用户满足映射传统U&G类别但具有AI特有属性（持续可用性、个性化、无社会评判）；2) 识别出三种新型满足：创意合作作为关系共建、关系模拟作为人际训练、性/浪漫满足作为情感重获；3) 满足随时间演变：从工具性入口转向情感投入，部分用户在治疗功能满足后转向自我调节。

Conclusion: 研究扩展了使用与满足理论，识别出交互式AI特有的满足过程，建议治理措施应基于对用户如何及为何使用AI伴侣的实证理解，超越简单的"危害-益处"二元框架。

Abstract: Conversational AI companions have grown prominent in public discourse, yet scholarly understanding of user experiences remains limited, with existing research organized around evaluative poles of harm and benefit rather than examining what users seek, how affordances mediate need fulfillment, or how use evolves over time. Drawing on interviews with 20 users of AI companionship platforms and qualitative content analysis informed by Uses and Gratifications (U&G) theory, this study offers three contributions. First, participants reported gratifications mapping onto established U&G categories but qualitatively inflected by conversational AI's distinctive affordances, such as persistent availability, personalization, and absence of social judgment. Second, several gratifications, creative collaboration as relational co-production, relational simulation as interpersonal training, and sexual/romantic satisfaction as reclamation, do not map onto existing typologies, instead emerging through interactive processes in which users actively simulate experiences with AI. Third, gratifications shifted over time, moving from instrumental entry points toward emotional engagement and, in some cases, self-regulated moderation after therapeutic functions were fulfilled. These findings extend U&G by identifying gratification processes unique to interactive AI and suggest governance efforts would benefit from an empirically grounded understanding of how and why users engage with AI companions.

</details>


### [133] [Breaking Negative Cycles: A Reflection-To-Action System For Adaptive Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06477)
*Minsol Michelle Kim,Daniel M. Low,David Lafond,Eugene Shim,Michelle Han,Mohanad Kandil,Chenyu Zhang,Theo Kitsberg,Chelsea Boccagno,Paul Pu Liang,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个基于语音的日记系统，整合了WhatIf-Planning结构化反思模块，将Gross情绪调节模型转化为具体提示，帮助用户从反思转向行动，改善应对灵活性和情绪调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 打破消极心理健康循环（如反刍思维和反复后悔）需要将意识转化为行为改变的反思过程。现有技术支持的自我反思工具需要更好地连接反思与行动，特别是在Transtheoretical模型的准备阶段。

Method: 15天野外研究（N=20），参与者使用语音日记系统记录后悔和愿望，并进行WhatIf-Planning（结合反事实思维与如果-那么计划的结构化反思模块）。随机分配到自由形式条件或Gross引导条件（将Gross情绪调节模型的五个过程映射为具体日记提示）。

Result: 结果显示所有条件下应对灵活性均有显著的前后测改善。Gross引导组产生了更多反事实替代方案，表达了更具体的如果-那么行动计划，并实施了更多自我驱动的改变计划。

Conclusion: 研究贡献包括：（1）统一反思到行动的技术支持自我反思系统，操作化TTM的准备阶段以连接沉思与行动；（2）首次操作化Gross过程模型的野外日记研究证据，揭示了其对日常应对灵活性和情绪调节的影响。

Abstract: Breaking negative mental health cycles, including rumination and recurring regrets, requires reflection that translates awareness into behavioral change. Grounded in the Transtheoretical Model (TTM) and Gross's Emotion Regulation (ER) Process Model, we examine how Technologies Supporting Self-Reflection (TSR) bridge reflection and action. In a 15-day in-the-wild study (N = 20), participants used a voice-based journaling system to capture regrets and wishes and engaged in WhatIf-Planning, a novel structured reflection module integrating counterfactual thinking with if-then planning. Participants were randomized to either a free-form condition or a Gross-guided condition, which maps the five processes of Gross's ER model into explicit journaling prompts. We contribute: (1) a unified reflection-to-action TSR system that operationalizes the Preparation stage of TTM to bridge Contemplation and Action, and (2) triangulated empirical evidence from an in-the-wild journaling study that first operationalizes Gross's Process Model, revealing effects on coping flexibility and emotion regulation in daily life. Results show significant pre-post improvements in coping flexibility, indicating adaptive self-regulation across conditions, with the Gross-guided group generating more counterfactual alternatives, articulating concrete if-then action plans, and implementing more plans for self-driven change.

</details>


### [134] [Language-Guided Multimodal Texture Authoring via Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06489)
*Wanli Qian,Aiden Chang,Shihan Lu,Michael Gu,Heather Culbertson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于语言提示的多模态纹理创作系统，将自然语言转换为协调的触觉振动、敲击瞬态和视觉预览，实现语义一致的触觉-视觉信号生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触觉纹理创作需要大量参数调整和试错，过程繁琐、速度慢且缺乏透明度，限制了创作效率和创意表达。

Method: 开发语言驱动的创作系统：1) 使用力/速度条件自回归模型生成滑动振动；2) 生成敲击瞬态；3) 扩散模型生成视觉预览；4) 通过共享的语言对齐潜在空间连接多模态，确保语义一致性。

Result: 1) 学习到的潜在空间编码了感知有意义的结构（粗糙度、光滑度、硬度）；2) 参与者评分显示一致趋势：粗糙度的凹凸效应、硬度的柔顺性、光滑度的表面膜影响；3) 用户研究表明跨模态体验连贯且提示迭代工作量低。

Conclusion: 语言可作为触觉纹理创作的有效控制模态，提示能可靠地引导触觉和视觉通道的材料语义，实现以提示为先、面向设计师的工作流程，替代手动参数调整。

Abstract: Authoring realistic haptic textures typically requires low-level parameter tuning and repeated trial-and-error, limiting speed, transparency, and creative reach. We present a language-driven authoring system that turns natural-language prompts into multimodal textures: two coordinated haptic channels - sliding vibrations via force/speed-conditioned autoregressive (AR) models and tapping transients - and a text-prompted visual preview from a diffusion model. A shared, language-aligned latent links modalities so a single prompt yields semantically consistent haptic and visual signals; designers can write goals (e.g., "gritty but cushioned surface," "smooth and hard metal surface") and immediately see and feel the result through a 3D haptic device. To verify that the learned latent encodes perceptually meaningful structure, we conduct an anchor-referenced, attribute-wise evaluation for roughness, slipperiness, and hardness. Participant ratings are projected to the interpretable line between two real-material references, revealing consistent trends - asperity effects in roughness, compliance in hardness, and surface-film influence in slipperiness. A human-subject study further indicates coherent cross-modal experience and low effort for prompt-based iteration. The results show that language can serve as a practical control modality for texture authoring: prompts reliably steer material semantics across haptic and visual channels, enabling a prompt-first, designer-oriented workflow that replaces manual parameter tuning with interpretable, text-guided refinement.

</details>


### [135] [Meaningful Human Command: Towards a New Model for Military Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06611)
*Adam Hepworth,Zena Assaad,Austin Wyatt,Hussein Abbass*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出"有意义的人类指挥"(MHC1)概念，作为军事人机交互中替代"有意义的人类控制"(MHC)的更具操作有效性的框架，以应对AI自主系统在军事应用中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事人机交互(MHRI)结合自主AI系统与人类能力，承诺提供军事优势和操作效能，但现有"有意义的人类控制"(MHC)概念在军事环境中存在不足，阻碍了负责任AI的采用和军事优势的实现。

Method: 提出"有意义的人类指挥"(MHC1)概念，通过一个技术可行的军事操作AI系统小场景来引导、情境化和增加概念的现实性，展示MHC1在高级军事指挥控制系统中的应用。

Result: MHC1作为更具操作有效性的概念框架被引入，能够更好地应对军事人机交互中的挑战，促进AI自主系统在军事环境中的负责任采用。

Conclusion: 在军事人机交互中，MHC1比传统的MHC概念更适合高级军事指挥控制系统，能够更好地实现军事优势，同时确保负责任AI的采用。

Abstract: Military human robot interaction (MHRI) presents a novel opportunity to blend the capabilities of autonomous and Artificial Intelligence (AI)-enabled systems with the skills and expertise of humans. The concept promises military advantages and greater operational effectiveness and efficiencies. However, the associated human-AI dynamics create challenges when attempting to design, implement, and operationalise the increasingly symbiotic relationship between humans and machines. Meaningful human control (MHC) is a popularised conceptualisation of what is deemed a responsible interaction among human and artificial agents; however, this notion falls short in military contexts and hinders the realisation of military advantages that could be achieved by advancing the adoption of responsible AI. This paper presents meaningful human command (MHC1) as a more operationally effective concept for advanced military command and control systems that embed AI-enabled autonomous systems. We introduce, explore, and unpack meaningful human command in the context of military human-robot interaction, presenting a vignette that offers a technologically feasible concept of an AI-enabled system within military operations. The vignette is used to guide, contextualise, and add realism to the narrative describing the concept and highlights associated MHRI challenges.

</details>


### [136] [MemoryDiorama: Generating Dynamic 3D Diorama from Everyday Photos for Memory Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06773)
*Keiichi Ihara,Tianle Li,Yasuhisa Shiino,Ryo Suzuki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MemoryDiorama是一个增强记忆回忆的原型系统，通过AI生成的上下文信息将日常照片转化为动态3D混合现实场景，显著提升自传体记忆回忆效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人媒体（如照片）作为记忆线索的局限性，需要更丰富的上下文信息来增强自传体记忆回忆。传统照片缺乏动态、空间和情境元素，限制了其作为记忆线索的效果。

Method: 系统结合LLM场景分析、3D对象生成、动画和空间组合，将日常照片转化为动态3D混合现实场景。从照片中提取地理信息、对象属性、光照条件和大气元素，然后通过生成式组件（对象动画、人体运动、地理效果、粒子效果）为记忆回忆提供更丰富的线索。

Result: 在18名参与者的用户研究中，相比仅照片和静态场景，MemoryDiorama在回忆时引发了更多内部细节和线索内细节，同时提高了感知细节和视觉生动性评分，表明更丰富的回忆体验。

Conclusion: MemoryDiorama通过AI生成的上下文信息增强个人媒体，有效提升了自传体记忆回忆的质量和丰富度，为增强记忆辅助技术提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present MemoryDiorama, a prototype system that introduces augmented memory cues, a concept that extends captured personal media with AI-generated contextual information to enhance autobiographical memory recall. MemoryDiorama transforms everyday photos into dynamic 3D dioramas in mixed reality by integrating LLM-based scene analysis with 3D object generation, animation, and spatial composition. The system extracts geographic information, object attributes, lighting conditions, and atmospheric elements from the photos. It then animates these elements with generative components such as object animations, human motion, geographical effects, and particle effects to provide richer cues for memory recall. We evaluated MemoryDiorama in a within-subject user study with 18 participants, comparing three conditions: Photo-Only, Static Diorama, and MemoryDiorama. Compared with both Photo-Only and Static Diorama, MemoryDiorama elicited more internal and in-cue details during recall. It also increased perceptual details and visual vividness ratings, suggesting richer recollective experience.

</details>


### [137] [Physics-driven Sonification for Improving Multisensory Needle Guidance in Percutaneous Epicardial Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06911)
*Veronica Ruozzi,Sasan Matinfar,Pasquale Vergara,Alessandro Albanesi,Serena Dell'Aversana,Stefano Carugo,Gianluigi Buccoliero,Nassir Navab,Alberto Redaelli,Emiliano Votta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于物理驱动的声化方法，结合扩展现实(XR)多感官导航，用于经皮心包穿刺术，通过视听反馈增强操作者对动态心脏解剖结构的感知，提高穿刺安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经皮心包穿刺术在透视引导下进行，但针头向薄而运动的心包膜推进具有高度挑战性和风险性。需要增强操作者在关键针头着陆阶段的感知能力。

Method: 1) 从4D CTA重建动态心脏解剖结构并配准到真实世界坐标系；2) 实时针头追踪提供针尖相对于运动心脏结构的位置；3) 视听反馈模块：视觉显示导航线索和动态解剖，听觉显示使用多层物理膜模型编码生理心脏状态。

Result: 12名心脏病专家在体模研究中，多感官反馈相比仅视觉反馈：显著提高导航安全性(χ²=11.30, p<0.01)；减少心肌接触(3.64% vs 7.27%)；增加正确穿刺率(90.91% vs 52.73%)；提高针头放置精度(Cliff delta=0.19)；降低认知负荷(NASA-TLX, p<0.01)；执行时间相当但时间-精度相关性显著不同(p<0.01)。

Conclusion: 物理驱动的声化方法可行，能改善时空感知并支持以用户为中心的手术导航，为经皮心包穿刺术提供更安全有效的导航方案。

Abstract: Percutaneous epicardial access (PEA), performed on a beating heart under fluoroscopy, enables arrhythmia treatment. However, advancing a needle toward the thin and moving pericardium remains highly challenging and risky. To address this problem, we present a physics-driven sonification method for Extended Reality (XR)-based multisensory navigation to enhance user perception during the critical needle landing phase in PEA.
  Dynamic cardiac anatomy from 4D CTA was reconstructed and registered to a real-world coordinate system. Real-time needle tracking provided the position of the needle tip relative to moving cardiac structures and drove an audio-visual feedback module. The visual display presented navigational cues and dynamic anatomy, while the auditory display encoded physiological cardiac states using a multilayer physical membrane model.
  A phantom study was conducted with twelve cardiologists performing needle insertions under visual-only and multisensory feedback. The multisensory method significantly improved navigation safety ($χ^2 = 11.30$, $p < 0.01$), reducing myocardial contact (3.64% vs. 7.27%) and increasing correct access (90.91% vs. 52.73%). Needle placement accuracy improved, with closer membrane proximity (Cliff delta = 0.19) and reduced variability ($p < 0.05$). Execution time was comparable, while time-accuracy correlations differed significantly between modalities ($p < 0.01$). NASA-TLX indicated lower cognitive load with multisensory guidance ($p < 0.01$).
  These results demonstrate the feasibility of physics-driven sonification for improving spatiotemporal awareness and supporting user-centered surgical navigation.

</details>


### [138] [BioMoTouch: Touch-Based Behavioral Authentication via Biometric-Motion Interaction Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07071)
*Zijian Ling,Jianbang Chen,Hongwei Li,Hongda Zhai,Man Zhou,Jun Feng,Zhengxiong Li,Qi Li,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: BioMoTouch是一个多模态触摸认证框架，通过同时利用电容触摸屏信号和惯性测量来联合建模生理接触结构和行为运动动态，实现高精度且抗攻击的移动设备认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触摸认证系统主要将触摸交互建模为纯粹的行为信号，忽略了其内在的多维特性，限制了对抗复杂对抗行为和现实世界变化的鲁棒性。研究发现触摸交互中惯性传感器捕获用户特定的行为动态，而电容屏幕同时捕获与手指形态和骨骼结构相关的生理特征。

Method: BioMoTouch通过整合电容触摸屏信号和惯性测量，联合建模生理接触结构和行为运动动态。该框架不是简单地组合独立决策，而是显式学习它们的协调交互，形成触摸行为的统一表示。系统在自然用户交互过程中隐式运行，无需额外硬件。

Result: 在38名参与者的真实使用条件下评估，BioMoTouch达到99.71%的平衡准确率和0.27%的等错误率。在人工复制、模仿和傀儡攻击场景下，保持低于0.90%的错误接受率，展示了对部分因素操纵的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BioMoTouch通过联合建模触摸交互的生理和行为维度，实现了高精度、鲁棒的移动设备认证，无需额外硬件即可在商用移动设备上实际部署。

Abstract: Touch-based authentication is widely deployed on mobile devices due to its convenience and seamless user experience. However, existing systems largely model touch interaction as a purely behavioral signal, overlooking its intrinsic multidimensional nature and limiting robustness against sophisticated adversarial behaviors and real-world variations. In this work, we present BioMoTouch, a multi-modal touch authentication framework on mobile devices grounded in a key empirical finding: during touch interaction, inertial sensors capture user-specific behavioral dynamics, while capacitive screens simultaneously capture physiological characteristics related to finger morphology and skeletal structure. Building upon this insight, BioMoTouch jointly models physiological contact structures and behavioral motion dynamics by integrating capacitive touchscreen signals with inertial measurements. Rather than combining independent decisions, the framework explicitly learns their coordinated interaction to form a unified representation of touch behavior. BioMoTouch operates implicitly during natural user interactions and requires no additional hardware, enabling practical deployment on commodity mobile devices. We evaluate BioMoTouch with 38 participants under realistic usage conditions. Experimental results show that BioMoTouch achieves a balanced accuracy of 99.71% and an equal error rate of 0.27%. Moreover, it maintains false acceptance rates below 0.90% under artificial replication, mimicry, and puppet attack scenarios, demonstrating strong robustness against partial-factor manipulation.

</details>


### [139] [Workmanship of Learning: Embedding Craftsmanship Values in AI-Integrated Educational Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07118)
*Tuan-Ting Huang,Janet Yi-Ching Huang,Stephan Wensveen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AI Craftsmanship框架通过风险、节奏和关怀等工艺价值观，为生成式AI在设计教育中的应用提供价值导向的方法，强调反思性学习而非自动化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI强调自动化和效率，这与设计教育中基于探索、反思和责任的学习理念存在冲突。需要一种价值导向的框架来调和AI技术与设计教育的核心价值。

Method: 采用"通过设计进行研究"(RtD)方法，开发了一个集成AI的创意编码工具(p5.js)，将工艺价值观嵌入交互和界面设计中。通过限制AI功能、鼓励迭代实验和突出反思，支持设计师学习生成式图案制作。对5名设计从业者进行了一小时工作坊和半结构化访谈研究。

Result: 研究发现工艺价值观表现不均衡：风险和节奏在早期意义建构中起重要作用，而关怀则通过反思实践显现。还出现了审美判断和信心等新兴价值观，这些也促进了学习。AI Craftsmanship框架能够调节价值观、工具和材料之间的关系。

Conclusion: AI Craftsmanship为设计教育中的AI系统设计提供了价值驱动的视角，支持反思性、负责任且基于工艺传统的学习，弥合了AI自动化与设计教育核心价值之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Generative AI's emphasis on automation and efficiency challenges design education, where learning is grounded in exploration, reflection, and responsibility. This work introduces AI Craftsmanship, a value-oriented framework drawing on craftsmanship traditions that emphasize risk, rhythm, and care as central to learning through making. Through a Research through Design (RtD) approach, we designed an AI-integrated creative coding tool embedding these values into interactions and interface rather than outcomes. The tool supports designers learning generative pattern-making with p5.js by constraining AI, encouraging iterative experimentation, and foregrounding reflection. We studied the tool with five design practitioners through one-hour sessions and semi-structured interviews. Findings show craft values manifest unevenly: risk and rhythm shape early sense-making, while care emerges through reflective practices. Emergent values -- such as aesthetic judgment and confidence -- also motivated learning. AI Craftsmanship mediates values, tools, and materials, offering a value-driven perspective on designing AI systems for reflective, responsible, craft-informed learning in design education.

</details>


### [140] [Mixed-Initiative Context: Structuring and Managing Context for Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07121)
*Haichang Li,Qinshi Zhang,Piaohong Wang,Zhicong Lu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出Mixed-Initiative Context概念，将多轮交互形成的上下文重构为显式、结构化、可操作的对象，实现动态组织管理，并通过Contextify系统进行用户研究验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人机协作中，多轮交互形成的上下文通常被扁平化为时间序列并作为固定整体处理，缺乏沿工作流的动态组织管理机制。这些上下文在生命周期、结构层次和相关性上差异显著，临时或废弃的交流、并行话题线程会干扰协作，且用户只能通过间接方式影响上下文，缺乏明确可控性。

Method: 提出Mixed-Initiative Context概念框架，将上下文重构为显式、结构化、可操作的交互对象。实现Contextify探测系统，通过用户研究探索用户上下文管理行为、对AI主动性的态度以及整体协作体验。

Result: 通过Contextify系统验证了Mixed-Initiative Context概念的可行性，研究了用户在实际协作中的上下文管理行为模式，评估了用户对AI主动性的接受度，并分析了整体协作体验的改善效果。

Conclusion: Mixed-Initiative Context概念为人机协作中的上下文管理提供了新范式，使上下文能够根据任务需求动态组织和调整，让人类和AI都能主动参与上下文构建和调节。该概念对HCI社区具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: In the human-AI collaboration area, the context formed naturally through multi-turn interactions is typically flattened into a chronological sequence and treated as a fixed whole in subsequent reasoning, with no mechanism for dynamic organization and management along the collaboration workflow. Yet these contexts differ substantially in lifecycle, structural hierarchy, and relevance. For instance, temporary or abandoned exchanges and parallel topic threads persist in the limited context window, causing interference and even conflict. Meanwhile, users are largely limited to influencing context indirectly through input modifications (e.g., corrections, references, or ignoring), leaving their control neither explicit nor verifiable.
  To address this, we propose Mixed-Initiative Context, which reconceptualizes the context formed across multi-turn interactions as an explicit, structured, and manipulable interactive object. Under this concept, the structure, scope, and content of context can be dynamically organized and adjusted according to task needs, enabling both humans and AI to actively participate in context construction and regulation. To explore this concept, we implement Contextify as a probe system and conduct a user study examining users' context management behaviors, attitudes toward AI initiative, and overall collaboration experience. We conclude by discussing the implications of this concept for the HCI community.

</details>


### [141] [Critical Inker: Scaffolding Critical Thinking in AI-Assisted Writing Through Socratic Questioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07167)
*Philipp Hugenroth,Valdemar Danry,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Critical Inker：通过逻辑分析和苏格拉底式反馈在写作过程中支持批判性反思的写作工具，旨在防止LLMs导致的认知能力退化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型越来越多地自动化写作任务，用户将批判性思维外包给系统的风险增加，可能导致认知能力退化（cognitive deskilling）。需要设计能够支持用户在写作过程中进行批判性反思的工具。

Method: 提出Critical Inker写作工具，包含两种方法：(1) 苏格拉底式聊天机器人：通过提问帮助用户识别和修复写作中的逻辑错误；(2) 视觉反馈：在文本中高亮显示逻辑错误，无需对话。详细描述了系统的技术实现，并评估了其论点提取和逻辑有效性准确性。

Result: 评估显示：与真实论点标注的论点重叠率达到91.2%，逻辑有效性准确率达到87%。进行了小规模试点研究并讨论了早期定性结果。

Conclusion: Critical Inker通过逻辑分析和苏格拉底式反馈有效支持写作过程中的批判性反思，能够高精度地提取论点和评估逻辑有效性，为解决LLMs导致的认知能力退化问题提供了可行方案。
