Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly automate writing tasks, there is a growing risk of cognitive deskilling where users offload critical thinking to the system. To address this, we introduce Critical Inker, a writing tool designed to scaffold critical reflection during writing through logical analysis and socratic feedback. We present two methods: (1) A Socratic chatbot using questions to help them realize and fix logical errors in their writing and (2) Visual Feedback, which highlights logical errors in the text without dialog. We detail the technical implementation of the system and evaluate its argument extraction and logical validity accuracy. Our evaluation shows a 91.2% argument overlap with ground truth argument annotations and 87% validity accuracy. Finally, we conducted a small-scale pilot and discuss early qualitative results.

</details>


### [142] [BATON: A Multimodal Benchmark for Bidirectional Automation Transition Observation in Naturalistic Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07263)
*Yuhang Wang,Yiyao Xu,Chaoyun Yang,Lingyao Li,Jingran Sun,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: BATON数据集：大规模自然驾驶数据集，捕捉127名驾驶员、136.6小时的真实驾驶自动化使用情况，包含多模态数据（前视视频、车内视频、CAN总线信号、雷达、GPS），用于研究控制权交接预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶自动化系统要求驾驶员持续保持注意力并随时准备接管，这带来了认知负荷、学习曲线陡峭和安全风险。预测驾驶员何时交出控制权（handover）和何时接管（takeover）对于设计主动情境感知HMI至关重要，但现有数据集缺乏多模态上下文信息。

Method: 收集127名驾驶员、136.6小时的真实驾驶数据，同步记录前视视频、车内视频、解码CAN总线信号、雷达前车交互数据和GPS路线上下文，形成围绕每个控制权转换的闭环多模态记录。

Result: 仅视觉输入不足以可靠预测控制权转换：前视视频捕捉道路环境但无法反映驾驶员状态，车内视频反映驾驶员准备状态但无法捕捉外部场景。结合CAN和路线上下文信号显著优于仅视频设置，显示多模态的强互补性。接管事件发展更渐进且受益于更长预测时间，而交出控制权更依赖即时上下文线索。

Conclusion: 多模态数据对于可靠预测驾驶自动化控制权转换至关重要，揭示了交出控制权和接管事件之间的不对称性，对辅助驾驶系统的HMI设计有直接意义。

Abstract: Existing driving automation (DA) systems on production vehicles rely on human drivers to decide when to engage DA while requiring them to remain continuously attentive and ready to intervene. This design demands substantial situational judgment and imposes significant cognitive load, leading to steep learning curves, suboptimal user experience, and safety risks from both over-reliance and delayed takeover. Predicting when drivers hand over control to DA and when they take it back is therefore critical for designing proactive, context-aware HMI, yet existing datasets rarely capture the multimodal context, including road scene, driver state, vehicle dynamics, and route environment. To fill this gap, we introduce BATON, a large-scale naturalistic dataset capturing real-world DA usage across 127 drivers, and 136.6 hours of driving. The dataset synchronizes front-view video, in-cabin video, decoded CAN bus signals, radar-based lead-vehicle interaction, and GPS-derived route context, forming a closed-loop multimodal record around each control transition. We define three benchmark tasks: driving action understanding, handover prediction, and takeover prediction, and evaluate baselines spanning sequence models, classical classifiers, and zero-shot VLMs. Results show that visual input alone is insufficient for reliable transition prediction: front-view video captures road context but not driver state, while in-cabin video reflects driver readiness but not the external scene. Incorporating CAN and route-context signals substantially improves performance over video-only settings, indicating strong complementarity across modalities. We further find takeover events develop more gradually and benefit from longer prediction horizons, whereas handover events depend more on immediate contextual cues, revealing an asymmetry with direct implications for HMI design in assisted driving systems.

</details>


### [143] [Mapping Child Malnutrition and Measuring Efficiency of Community Healthcare Workers through Location Based Games in India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07299)
*Arka Majhi,Aparajita Mondal,Satish B. Agnihotri*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过共同设计开发了一款基于游戏的儿童人体测量数据收集方法，显著提高了社区健康工作者的测量效率和参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度社区健康工作者在收集儿童人体测量数据时面临参与度低、数据空间代表性差和时效性不足的问题，需要创新方法来改善数据收集实践。

Method: 采用共同设计和研究通过设计方法，开发基于游戏的数据收集系统；通过热点和密度分布图分析数据；在印度多个州进行两组试验（每组n=94），比较游戏化和非游戏化方法。

Result: 游戏化方法显著提高了测量效率（p<0.05），在参与度和数据保留方面优于非游戏化方法。

Conclusion: 共同设计和研究通过设计方法开发的游戏化数据收集系统能有效提升社区健康工作者的参与度和数据收集质量，为地理空间游戏在公共卫生数据收集中的应用提供了实践支持。

Abstract: In India, Community Healthcare Workers (CHWs) serve as critical intermediaries between the state and beneficiaries, including pregnant mothers and children. Effective planning and prioritization of care and services necessitate the collection of accurate health data from the community. Crowdsourcing child anthropometric data through CHWs could establish a valuable repository for evidence-based decision-making and service planning. However, existing platforms often fail to maintain CHWs' engagement over time and across different spatial contexts, resulting in spatially misrepresented and outdated data.
  This study addresses these challenges by conducting a co-design exercise to develop innovative methods for collecting anthropometric data over time and space. The exercise involved analyzing data to create hotspot and density distribution maps. We implemented a trial of the developed game with two groups (n=94 per group) from various states across India, comparing the game-based and non-game-based data collection methods. Our findings reveal that the game-based approach significantly improved measuring efficiency (p<0.05) and demonstrated superior engagement and retention compared to the non-game-based method.
  This research contributes to the expanding literature on co-design and Research through Design (RtD) methodologies for developing geospatial games, highlighting their potential to enhance data collection practices and improve engagement among CHWs.

</details>


### [144] [NIRVANA: A Comprehensive Dataset for Reproducing How Students Use Generative AI for Essay Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07344)
*Andrew Jelson,Daniel Manesh,Sangwook Lee,Alice Jang,Daniel Dunlap,Tamara Maddox,Young-Ho Kim,Sang Won Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NIRVANA数据集记录了77名大学生在撰写分析性文章时使用ChatGPT的完整过程，包括击键行为、对话历史和复制内容，揭示了AI辅助写作的行为模式和学生写作特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI写作助手在教育领域的快速普及，教育工作者和研究者需要实证证据来理解其对学术写作的影响并指导有效的教学设计。目前缺乏对学生如何在真实写作任务中使用这些工具的系统性理解，包括何时寻求帮助、提出什么问题以及如何将AI生成内容整合到文章中，这限制了基于证据的政策制定和对生成式AI学习效果的严格评估。

Method: 研究引入NIRVANA数据集，收集了77名大学生在撰写分析性文章时使用ChatGPT的完整数据。数据集包括击键级别的写作行为记录、完整的ChatGPT对话历史以及所有从ChatGPT复制的文本，能够完整重建写作过程。研究还开发了一个回放界面来重建写作过程，并通过定性分析样本回放来系统检查学生与AI的互动。

Result: 分析识别出关键行为模式，包括ChatGPT查询频率的变化及其与文章特征（如长度和可读性）的关系。根据学生的贡献和修订模式，识别出四种写作类型：主导作者、协作者、起草者和氛围写作者。回放界面的定性分析展示了该工具如何支持对学生-AI互动的系统性检查。

Conclusion: NIRVANA数据集为理解学生如何在真实写作任务中使用生成式AI提供了实证基础，揭示了不同的写作行为模式和学生-AI互动方式。该研究为教育工作者和研究者提供了系统分析AI辅助写作的工具和框架，有助于制定基于证据的教学策略和评估生成式AI的学习效果。

Abstract: With the rapid adoption of AI writing assistants in education, educators and researchers need empirical evidence to understand the impact on student writing and inform effective pedagogical design. Despite widespread use, we lack systematic understanding of how students engage with these tools during authentic writing tasks: when they seek assistance, what they ask, and how they incorporate AI-generated content into their essays. This gap limits evidence-based policy development and rigorous evaluation of generative AI's learning effects. To address this gap, we introduce NIRVANA, a dataset capturing how university students use generative AI while writing an analytical essay. The dataset includes 77 students who completed an essay task with access to ChatGPT, recording keystroke-level writing behavior, full ChatGPT conversation histories, and all text copied from ChatGPT, enabling a complete reconstruction of the writing process and revealing how AI assistance shapes student work. Our analysis identifies key behavioral patterns, including variation in ChatGPT query frequency and its relationship to essay characteristics such as length and readability. We identify four writing profiles based on students' contribution and revision patterns: Lead Authors, Collaborators, Drafters, and Vibe Writers. To support deeper investigation, we developed a replay interface that reconstructs the writing process; qualitative analysis of sampled replays demonstrates how this tool enables systematic examination of student-AI interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Computing In Spintronic Memory: A Thermal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06667)
*Patrick Miller,Hüsrev Cilasun,Sachin S. Sapatnekar,Ulya R. Karpuzcu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文对存内计算(CiM)进行了定量热特性分析，揭示了CiM架构中的热行为规律


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存内计算(CiM)是解决传统系统内存瓶颈的有前景范式，但CiM带来的更高微架构活动会显著增加功率密度并导致热点问题，需要对其进行热特性分析

Method: 对CiM进行定量热特性表征，分析温度分布与计算活动、内存阵列规模、技术参数之间的关系

Result: 发现：(1)由于横向热传导，温度保持基本均匀；(2)温度随参与计算的内存单元数量线性增加；(3)温度随内存阵列尺寸线性减小；(4)内存技术决定功率密度和热特性

Conclusion: CiM的热特性受计算活动、阵列规模和技术参数共同影响，为CiM系统的热管理设计提供了重要指导

Abstract: Computing-in-Memory (CiM) is a promising paradigm to address the memory bottleneck constraining traditional systems. Most power-efficient CiM variants can directly perform Boolean operations in non-volatile memory arrays. Higher microarchitectural activity due to CiM, however, can significantly increase power density (power per area) and result in thermal hotspots. In this paper, we provide a quantitative thermal characterization for CiM. We demonstrate that (i) the temperature remains mostly uniform due to lateral thermal conduction; (ii) the temperature increases linearly with the number of memory cells participating in computation; (iii) the temperature decreases linearly with the memory array size; (iv) the memory technology dictates the power density, hence the thermal characteristics.

</details>


### [146] [Improving Feasibility in Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm for Vehicle Routing via Constraint-Aware Initialization and Hybrid XY-X Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07218)
*Yuan-Zheng Lei,Yaobang Gong,Xianfeng Terry Yang,Nii Attoh-Okine*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对车辆路径问题（VRP）的约束感知QAOA框架，包含轻量级初始化策略和混合XY-X混合器，以解决标准QAOA中可行解占比极小和传统Pauli-X混合器破坏局部约束结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆路径问题（VRP）是物流运输中的核心组合优化问题，但标准量子近似优化算法（QAOA）在处理VRP时面临两个主要挑战：1）可行解在搜索空间中占比极小；2）传统的Pauli-X混合器会破坏满足局部约束的部分解结构。需要设计能够保持约束结构同时保持探索能力的QAOA变体。

Method: 提出约束感知QAOA框架，包含两个互补组件：1）轻量级初始化策略，将选定的简单但信息丰富的局部one-hot约束编码到初始状态，减少初始叠加空间并增加具有重要局部结构的状态概率质量；2）混合XY-X混合器，在保持初始化时施加的约束结构的同时，在QAOA演化期间保留对剩余无约束自由度的探索灵活性。

Result: 在三种渐进更现实的机制下评估：理想状态向量模拟、有限采样和含噪声有限采样。在所有机制中，所提方法始终比标准QAOA获得更低的平均能量和更高的可行解比例，表明对结构有效、成本更低的VRP解的引导更有效。但在含噪声机制下性能差距缩小。

Conclusion: 约束感知QAOA框架在VRP问题上优于标准QAOA，特别是在硬件错误率较低时。随着量子硬件改进和错误率下降，这种结构化混合器的实际优势可能会增长。研究结果表明，针对特定问题结构定制QAOA组件对于量子组合优化的实际应用至关重要。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is a leading framework for quantum combinatorial optimization. The Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP), a core problem in logistics and transportation, is a natural application target, but it poses a major feasibility challenge for standard QAOA because feasible solutions occupy only a tiny fraction of the search space, and the conventional Pauli-$X$ mixer can disrupt partial solution structures that satisfy key local constraints. To address this issue, we propose a constraint-aware QAOA framework with two complementary components. First, we design a lightweight initialization strategy that encodes a selected subset of simple yet informative local one-hot constraints into the initial state, thereby reducing the initial superposition space and increasing the probability mass on states with important local structure. Second, we introduce a hybrid XY-$X$ mixer that preserves the constraint structure imposed at initialization while retaining exploratory flexibility over the remaining unconstrained degrees of freedom during QAOA evolution. We evaluate the proposed framework against standard QAOA under three progressively more realistic regimes: ideal statevector simulation, finite-shot sampling, and noisy finite-shot sampling. Across all regimes, the proposed method consistently achieves lower average energy and higher feasible-solution ratios than standard QAOA, indicating more effective guidance toward structurally valid, lower-cost VRP solutions. However, the performance gap narrows in the noisy regime. Because this setting adopts a hardware-inspired error model based on near-best-reported laboratory-level qubit gate and readout fidelities, the observed attenuation suggests that the practical advantage of the more structured mixer is likely to grow as quantum hardware improves and error rates decline.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Logical Robots: Declarative Multi-Agent Programming in Logica](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06629)
*Evgeny Skvortsov,Yilin Xia,Ojaswa Garg,Shawn Bowers,Bertram Ludäscher*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Logical Robots是一个基于逻辑编程语言Logica的交互式多机器人仿真平台，通过声明式逻辑谓词定义机器人行为，实现低级反应控制与高级规划的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多机器人系统提供一个统一的编程框架，使低级反应式控制和高级规划能够在单一逻辑编程环境中协同工作，便于探索复杂的多机器人协作行为。

Method: 使用逻辑编程语言Logica声明式定义机器人行为，通过逻辑谓词将模拟雷达阵列的观测和共享内存映射到期望的电机输出，实现交互式多智能体仿真。

Result: 开发了Logical Robots平台，该平台允许在单一逻辑编程环境中同时实现低层反应控制和高层规划，为多机器人行为研究提供了连贯的框架。

Conclusion: 基于逻辑编程的声明式方法能够有效统一机器人系统的不同控制层次，为多机器人协作研究提供了强大而一致的仿真环境。

Abstract: We present Logical Robots, an interactive multi-agent simulation platform where autonomous robot behavior is specified declaratively in the logic programming language Logica. Robot behavior is defined by logical predicates that map observations from simulated radar arrays and shared memory to desired motor outputs. This approach allows low-level reactive control and high-level planning to coexist within a single programming environment, providing a coherent framework for exploring multi-agent robot behavior.

</details>


### [148] [Strategic Persuasion with Trait-Conditioned Multi-Agent Systems for Iterative Legal Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07028)
*Philipp D. Siedler*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于LLM的多智能体法庭辩论框架，通过可解释的性格特质控制智能体的修辞风格和战略导向，在对抗性环境中研究语言作为战略行动空间的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有博弈论模型大多忽略了语言在对抗性领域（如法律、外交、谈判）中的说服机制，需要建立能够将语言作为一级战略行动空间的研究框架。

Method: 提出了战略法庭框架，使用基于9个可解释特质（分为4种原型）的LLM智能体构建控辩团队，进行迭代式回合制法律辩论；评估了84种三特质团队配置，并引入了基于强化学习的特质编排器。

Result: 异质互补特质团队表现优于同质配置；适度交互深度产生更稳定的判决；某些特质（特别是定量和魅力型）对说服成功贡献显著；动态特质编排器发现的策略优于静态人工设计组合。

Conclusion: 语言可被视为一级战略行动空间，该框架为构建能够在多智能体环境中进行自适应说服的自主智能体奠定了基础。

Abstract: Strategic interaction in adversarial domains such as law, diplomacy, and negotiation is mediated by language, yet most game-theoretic models abstract away the mechanisms of persuasion that operate through discourse. We present the Strategic Courtroom Framework, a multi-agent simulation environment in which prosecution and defense teams composed of trait-conditioned Large Language Model (LLM) agents engage in iterative, round-based legal argumentation. Agents are instantiated using nine interpretable traits organized into four archetypes, enabling systematic control over rhetorical style and strategic orientation.
  We evaluate the framework across 10 synthetic legal cases and 84 three-trait team configurations, totaling over 7{,}000 simulated trials using DeepSeek-R1 and Gemini~2.5~Pro. Our results show that heterogeneous teams with complementary traits consistently outperform homogeneous configurations, that moderate interaction depth yields more stable verdicts, and that certain traits (notably quantitative and charismatic) contribute disproportionately to persuasive success. We further introduce a reinforcement-learning-based Trait Orchestrator that dynamically generates defense traits conditioned on the case and opposing team, discovering strategies that outperform static, human-designed trait combinations.
  Together, these findings demonstrate how language can be treated as a first-class strategic action space and provide a foundation for building autonomous agents capable of adaptive persuasion in multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [149] [Designing for Accountable Agents: a Viewpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07204)
*Stephen Cranefield,Nir Oren*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文探讨多智能体系统中智能体的问责制，区别于传统关注AI开发部署组织过程的问责研究，聚焦于智能体间的相互问责机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统日益复杂、普及和自主化，对其透明性、可解释性和符合人类价值观的需求日益增长。虽然问责制被认为是关键要素，但这一概念在多智能体系统（MAS）中缺乏明确定义，特别是智能体间（包括人类智能体）的相互问责机制尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 1. 对多学科现有问责制研究进行深入综述，寻求建立连贯的概念定义；2. 提供多智能体系统应用领域的现实示例，展示智能体遵循问责流程的益处；3. 识别构建可问责智能体的研究挑战，并提出初步解决方案，为未来研究制定路线图。

Result: 提出了多智能体系统中问责制的概念框架，展示了智能体间问责机制的实际应用场景，识别了关键研究挑战并提供了初步解决方案，为构建开放社会技术系统中自主元素参与问责流程奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为多智能体系统中的问责制研究奠定了理论基础，区别于传统组织层面的问责研究，聚焦于智能体间的相互问责机制。通过概念澄清、实例展示和挑战识别，为未来构建可问责的自主智能体系统提供了明确的研究方向和路线图。

Abstract: AI systems are becoming increasingly complex, ubiquitous and autonomous, leading to increasing concerns about their impacts on individuals and society. In response, researchers have begun investigating how to ensure that the methods underlying AI decision-making are transparent and their decisions are explainable to people and conformant to human values and ethical principles. As part of this research thrust, the need for accountability within AI systems has been noted, but this notion has proven elusive to define; we aim to address this issue in the current paper. Unlike much recent work, we do not address accountability within the human organisational processes of developing and deploying AI; rather we consider what it would it mean for the agents within a multi-agent system (MAS), potentially including human agents, to be accountable to other agents or to have others accountable to them.
  In this work, we make the following contributions: we provide an in-depth survey of existing work on accountability in multiple disciplines, seeking to identify a coherent definition of the concept; we give a realistic example of a multi-agent system application domain that illustrates the benefits of enabling agents to follow accountability processes, and we identify a set of research challenges for the MAS community in building accountable agents, sketching out some initial solutions to these, thereby laying out a road-map for future research. Our focus is on laying the groundwork to enable autonomous elements within open socio-technical systems to take part in accountability processes.

</details>


### [150] [Intertemporal Demand Allocation for Inventory Control in Online Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07312)
*Rene Caldentey,Tong Xie*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 平台通过跨期需求分配影响卖家库存选择，利用信息机制改变销售流可预测性，从而影响安全库存需求，在平台履行采用率和库存持有之间进行权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线市场平台不仅匹配买卖双方，还通过订单路由和履行服务影响卖家库存决策。平台需要在不直接控制库存的情况下，通过需求分配机制影响卖家的库存选择，以优化平台收益。

Method: 建立模型研究平台如何通过跨期需求分配影响卖家库存决策。平台观察总需求，在卖家间分配订单，卖家在FBM（商家履行）和FBP（平台履行）两种履行选项中选择，并采用状态依赖的基库存策略进行补货。重点研究信息机制：通过改变卖家销售流的可预测性来改变其安全库存需求。

Result: 在非歧视性分配政策下，均匀分配最小化预测不确定性，而任何更高水平的不确定性可以通过简单的低记忆分配规则实现。增加不确定性需要路由规则防止卖家从自身销售历史推断总需求。平台问题简化为选择预测不确定性水平，在平台履行采用率和采用者库存持有之间进行权衡。

Conclusion: 需求分配是数字市场中强大的运营和信息设计杠杆，平台可以通过控制需求分配的不确定性来影响卖家行为，优化平台收益，而无需直接控制库存。

Abstract: Online marketplaces increasingly do more than simply match buyers and sellers: they route orders across competing sellers and, in many categories, offer ancillary fulfillment services that make seller inventory a source of platform revenue. We investigate how a platform can use intertemporal demand allocation to influence sellers' inventory choices without directly controlling stock. We develop a model in which the platform observes aggregate demand, allocates orders across sellers over time, and sellers choose between two fulfillment options, fulfill-by-merchant (FBM) and fulfill-by-platform (FBP), while replenishing inventory under state-dependent base-stock policies. The key mechanism we study is informational: by changing the predictability of each seller's sales stream, the platform changes sellers' safety-stock needs even when average demand shares remain unchanged. We focus on nondiscriminatory allocation policies that give sellers the same demand share and forecast risk. Within this class, uniform splitting minimizes forecast uncertainty, whereas any higher level of uncertainty can be implemented using simple low-memory allocation rules. Moreover, increasing uncertainty above the uniform benchmark requires routing rules that prevent sellers from inferring aggregate demand from their own sales histories. These results reduce the platform's problem to choosing a level of forecast uncertainty that trades off adoption of platform fulfillment against the inventory held by adopters. Our analysis identifies demand allocation as a powerful operational and informational design lever in digital marketplaces.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Ontology-based knowledge graph infrastructure for interoperable atomistic simulation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06230)
*Abril Azocar Guzman,Sarath Menon,Tilmann Hickel,Stefan Sandfeld*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出基于本体的知识图谱基础设施，用于表示和整合原子尺度模拟数据，解决数据格式异构、元数据不完整和工作流缺乏标准化表示的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度模拟数据的重用受到异构格式、不完整元数据以及工作流和溯源缺乏标准化表示的制约，需要统一框架来提高数据的可发现性、互操作性和重用性。

Method: 结合领域本体和软件框架，从现有数据集和模拟工作流中捕获数据，将异构数据归一化为本体对齐的统一表示，并以机器可读形式表示工作流。

Result: 构建了包含超过75万三元组、描述近8000个计算样本的知识图谱，实现了晶界数据集成、跨数据集材料性能分析以及从现有模拟中提取衍生热力学量。

Conclusion: 该工作为改善原子尺度模拟数据的可发现性、互操作性和重用性提供了实用框架，通过本体对齐的知识图谱实现一致查询分析和溯源追踪。

Abstract: The reuse of atomistic simulation data is often limited by heterogeneous formats, incomplete metadata, and a lack of standardized representations of workflows and provenance. Here we present an ontology-based infrastructure for representing and integrating atomistic simulation data as a knowledge graph. The approach combines domain ontologies with a software framework that enables data capture both from existing datasets and directly from simulation workflows at the point of generation. Heterogeneous data from multiple sources are normalized into a common, ontology-aligned representation, enabling consistent querying and analysis across datasets. We demonstrate these capabilities through the integration of grain boundary data, cross-dataset analysis of material properties, and extraction of derived thermodynamic quantities from existing simulations. In addition, workflows are represented in a machine-readable form, enabling both forward provenance tracking and partial reconstruction of computational procedures. The resulting knowledge graph contains over 750,000 triples describing nearly 8,000 computational samples. This work provides a practical framework for improving the findability, interoperability, and reuse of atomistic simulation data.

</details>


### [152] [Automating Database-Native Function Code Synthesis with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06231)
*Wei Zhou,Xuanhe Zhou,Qikang He,Guoliang Li,Bingsheng He,Quanqing Xu,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DBCooker是一个基于LLM的数据库原生函数自动合成系统，通过函数特征化、伪代码规划、混合填空模型和三级验证等组件，显著提升数据库函数合成的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库内核中不断增长的原生函数需求与手动开发的复杂性之间存在矛盾。现有LLM代码生成工具过于通用，在数据库特定开发中容易产生幻觉或忽略关键上下文，而数据库函数合成本身涉及多单元注册、内部引用链接和逻辑正确实现等复杂问题。

Method: DBCooker包含三个核心组件：1) 函数特征化模块聚合多源声明、识别需要专门编码的函数单元并追踪跨单元依赖；2) 设计三种操作解决主要合成挑战：基于伪代码的编码计划生成器构建结构化实现骨架、基于概率先验和组件感知的混合填空模型集成核心逻辑与可重用例程、三级渐进验证（语法检查、标准合规性、LLM引导的语义验证）；3) 自适应编排策略统一这些操作与现有工具，并通过相似函数的编排历史动态排序。

Result: DBCooker在SQLite、PostgreSQL和DuckDB上平均准确率比其他方法高34.55%，能够合成最新SQLite(v3.50)中不存在的新函数。

Conclusion: DBCooker通过专门针对数据库函数合成的系统化方法，有效解决了通用LLM代码生成工具在数据库特定开发中的局限性，显著提升了数据库原生函数自动合成的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Database systems incorporate an ever-growing number of functions in their kernels (a.k.a., database native functions) for scenarios like new application support and business migration. This growth causes an urgent demand for automatic database native function synthesis. While recent advances in LLM-based code generation (e.g., Claude Code) show promise, they are too generic for database-specific development. They often hallucinate or overlook critical context because database function synthesis is inherently complex and error-prone, where synthesizing a single function may involve registering multiple function units, linking internal references, and implementing logic correctly. To this end, we propose DBCooker, an LLM-based system for automatically synthesizing database native functions. It consists of three components. First, the function characterization module aggregates multi-source declarations, identifies function units that require specialized coding, and traces cross-unit dependencies. Second, we design operations to address the main synthesis challenges: (1) a pseudo-code-based coding plan generator that constructs structured implementation skeletons by identifying key elements such as reusable referenced functions; (2) a hybrid fill-in-the-blank model guided by probabilistic priors and component awareness to integrate core logic with reusable routines; and (3) three-level progressive validation, including syntax checking, standards compliance, and LLM-guided semantic verification. Finally, an adaptive orchestration strategy unifies these operations with existing tools and dynamically sequences them via the orchestration history of similar functions. Results show that DBCooker outperforms other methods on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and DuckDB (34.55% higher accuracy on average), and can synthesize new functions absent in the latest SQLite (v3.50).

</details>


### [153] [CobbleDB: Modelling Levelled Storage by Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06273)
*Emilie Ma,Ayush Pandey,Annette Bieniusa,Marc Shapiro*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 基于组合方法构建正确性可验证的数据库存储后端，通过规范保证的存储等价性组合性能特性，并实现CobbleDB作为RocksDB分层存储的重新实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建正确性可验证的数据库存储后端，通过形式化规范确保实现正确性，并利用规范保证的等价性组合不同性能特性，提高开发效率和系统可靠性。

Method: 采用基于组合的方法：首先为多种存储变体指定行为规范并证明其正确性和等价性；然后基于规范手动构造Java实现；最后利用规范保证的存储等价性组合性能特性。

Result: 开发了CobbleDB，作为RocksDB分层存储的重新实现，展示了该方法的实际价值。规范的简洁性使得手动构造实现变得直接可行。

Conclusion: 基于组合和形式化规范的方法能够有效构建正确性可验证的数据库存储系统，规范保证的等价性为性能特性组合提供了理论基础，CobbleDB的成功实现验证了该方法的实用性。

Abstract: We present a composition-based approach to building correctby-construction database backing stores. In previous work, we specified the behaviour of several store variants and proved their correctness and equivalence. Here, we derive a Java implementation: the simplicity of the specification makes manual construction straightforward. We leverage spec-guaranteed store equivalence to compose performance features, then demonstrate practical value with CobbleDB, a reimplementation of RocksDB's levelled storage.

</details>


### [154] [AI-Driven Research for Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06566)
*Audrey Cheng,Harald Ng,Aaron Kabcenell,Peter Bailis,Matei Zaharia,Lin Ma,Xiao Shi,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文提出通过共同进化评估器与解决方案来解决AI驱动系统研究中评估瓶颈问题，在数据库优化领域实现自动化代码生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工作负载和硬件复杂性超越人类研究能力，现有数据库性能优化方法难以跟上发展。AI驱动系统研究(ADRS)使用大语言模型自动化解决方案发现，但面临评估管道瓶颈，需要快速准确的反馈来收敛到有效解决方案

Method: 通过共同进化评估器与解决方案来自动化评估器设计。在三个案例研究中应用：缓冲区管理、查询重写和索引选择优化

Result: 自动化评估器能够发现超越最先进基线的新算法，例如确定性查询重写策略实现高达6.8倍延迟降低，证明解决评估瓶颈能够释放ADRS潜力

Conclusion: 通过共同进化评估器与解决方案解决评估瓶颈，使AI驱动系统研究能够为下一代数据系统生成高度优化、可部署的代码

Abstract: As the complexity of modern workloads and hardware increasingly outpaces human research and engineering capacity, existing methods for database performance optimization struggle to keep pace. To address this gap, a new class of techniques, termed AI-Driven Research for Systems (ADRS), uses large language models to automate solution discovery. This approach shifts optimization from manual system design to automated code generation. The key obstacle, however, in applying ADRS is the evaluation pipeline. Since these frameworks rapidly generate hundreds of candidates without human supervision, they depend on fast and accurate feedback from evaluators to converge on effective solutions. Building such evaluators is especially difficult for complex database systems. To enable the practical application of ADRS in this domain, we propose automating the design of evaluators by co-evolving them with the solutions. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this approach through three case studies optimizing buffer management, query rewriting, and index selection. Our automated evaluators enable the discovery of novel algorithms that outperform state-of-the-art baselines (e.g., a deterministic query rewrite policy that achieves up to 6.8x lower latency), demonstrating that addressing the evaluation bottleneck unlocks the potential of ADRS to generate highly optimized, deployable code for next-generation data systems.

</details>


### [155] [SonicDB S6: A Storage-Efficient Verkle Trie for High-Throughput Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06579)
*Luigi Crisci,Lorenz Schuler,Herbert Jordan,Bernhard Scholz*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SonicDB S6是用于Sonic区块链的生产级Rust Verkle Trie数据库，通过占用感知节点特化、delta节点等优化技术，显著降低了存储开销并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊状态数据库使用的Merkle Patricia Trie存在见证证明大小和存储开销大的问题。Verkle Tries虽然能提供小于150字节的见证证明，但在Sonic这样每300毫秒产生一个区块的高吞吐量区块链中部署时，面临着存储效率、承诺计算成本以及实时服务历史和当前状态查询的工程挑战。

Method: 1. 利用Sonic区块链的非分叉特性实现激进存储优化；2. 通过O(kn²)动态规划选择的占用感知节点特化，减少实时存储98%；3. 仅记录变化槽位的delta节点，减少归档存储95%；4. 批量更新、多线程承诺计算和同态Pedersen缓存。

Result: 相比持久化Geth Verkle基线，SonicDB S6实现了2.85倍的吞吐量提升，同时维持生产级区块速率性能。

Conclusion: SonicDB S6成功解决了在高吞吐量、短区块时间区块链中部署Verkle Trie的工程挑战，通过创新的存储优化和性能提升技术，为Sonic区块链提供了高效的生产级状态数据库解决方案。

Abstract: The Ethereum state database uses Merkle Patricia Trie (MPT), which suffers from large witness proof sizes and high storage overhead. Verkle Tries have been proposed as a replacement, offering witness proofs below 150 bytes through vector commitments and Inner Product Argument aggregation. However, deploying a Verkle Trie in a high-throughput, short block-time blockchain such as Sonic, which produces a block every 300 milliseconds, introduces substantial engineering challenges related to storage efficiency, commitment computation costs, and the need to serve both live and historical state queries in real time.
  We present SonicDB S6, a production Rust Verkle Trie database for the Sonic blockchain, which leverages its non-forking property to enable aggressive storage optimizations. Occupancy-aware node specializations, selected via an O(kn2) dynamic program, reduce live storage by 98%. Delta nodes that record only changed slots reduce archive storage by 95%. Batched updates, multi-threaded commitment computation, and homomorphic Pedersen caching yield 2.85x higher throughput than a persistent Geth Verkle baseline while sustaining production block-rate performance.

</details>


### [156] [CubeGraph: Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Spatial and Temporal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06616)
*Mingyu Yang,Wentao Li,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CubeGraph：一种新颖的索引框架，通过分层网格划分空间域并在每个单元内维护向量图，动态缝合相邻单元索引以原生集成向量搜索与空间约束，解决现有系统因向量空间碎片化导致的图路由连接中断和遍历开销问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代检索增强生成（RAG）系统对结合高维向量相似性搜索与时空过滤的混合查询需求日益增长。现有系统通常将向量索引嵌套在低维空间结构（如R树）中，这种解耦架构导致向量空间碎片化，迫使查询引擎每次查询调用多个不相交的子索引，破坏了图路由连接性，产生严重的遍历开销，且难以优化复杂空间边界。

Method: 提出CubeGraph框架：1）使用分层网格划分空间域；2）在每个网格单元内维护模块化的向量图；3）查询执行时，动态缝合与查询过滤器相交的空间单元对应的相邻立方体级索引；4）通过动态图集成恢复全局连接性，实现统一的单次最近邻遍历。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的广泛评估表明，CubeGraph显著优于最先进的基线方法，在复杂混合工作负载下提供卓越的查询执行性能、可扩展性和灵活性。

Conclusion: CubeGraph通过原生集成向量搜索与任意空间约束，解决了现有解耦架构的碎片化问题，通过动态图缝合实现高效的单次遍历，为现代RAG系统的混合查询提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Hybrid queries combining high-dimensional vector similarity search with spatio-temporal filters are increasingly critical for modern retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. Existing systems typically handle these workloads by nesting vector indices within low-dimensional spatial structures, such as R-trees. However, this decoupled architecture fragments the vector space, forcing the query engine to invoke multiple disjoint sub-indices per query. This fragmentation destroys graph routing connectivity, incurs severe traversal overhead, and struggles to optimize for complex spatial boundaries. In this paper, we propose CubeGraph, a novel indexing framework designed to natively integrate vector search with arbitrary spatial constraints. CubeGraph partitions the spatial domain using a hierarchical grid, maintaining modular vector graphs within each cell. During query execution, CubeGraph dynamically stitches together adjacent cube-level indices on the fly whenever their spatial cells intersect with the query filter. This dynamic graph integration restores global connectivity, enabling a unified, single-pass nearest-neighbor traversal that eliminates the overhead of fragmented sub-index invocations. Extensive evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that CubeGraph significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, offering superior query execution performance, scalability, and flexibility for complex hybrid workloads.

</details>


### [157] [LASER: A Data-Centric Method for Low-Cost and Efficient SQL Rewriting based on SQL-GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06804)
*Jiahui Li,Tongwang Wu,Yuren Mao,Rong Kang,Tieying Zhang,Yunjun Gao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LASER框架通过SQL-MCTS生成复杂慢查询数据集，结合SQL-GRPO对齐策略，赋能小型语言模型实现高效SQL优化，超越规则系统和LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询重写方法存在局限性：基于规则的方法缺乏适应性，而基于LLM的方法推理成本高且有隐私风险。小型语言模型(SLMs)提供了灵活性-效率的平衡点，但受限于高质量领域特定训练数据的稀缺。

Method: 1. 构建SQL-MCTS：采用MCTS混合扩展策略，结合规则引导的反模式和LLM变异，将结构表达性种子演化为执行验证的慢查询变体。2. 提出SQL-GRPO：基于组相对策略优化的专门对齐策略，集成锚定组优势改进优势估计，使用复杂度自适应动态rollout高效分配探索预算。

Result: 在Qwen3模型上实现的LASER在执行效率上显著优于基于规则的系统和大语言模型，同时表现出强大的零样本可迁移性和最小开销。

Conclusion: LASER框架成功解决了小型语言模型在SQL优化中的数据稀缺问题，通过数据生成和对齐策略的结合，实现了高效、可迁移的查询重写系统，为数据库优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Query rewriting, the process of transforming queries into semantically equivalent yet more efficient variants, is crucial for database optimization. Existing solutions predominantly rely on either rule-based heuristics or Large Language Models (LLMs). However, traditional rule-based methods lack adaptability, while LLM-based approaches incur prohibitive inference costs and privacy risks. In contrast, Small Language Models (SLMs) present a compelling middle ground, potentially offering both flexibility and efficiency. However, the development of such compact models is severely bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality, domain-specific training data. To bridge this gap, we introduce LASER, a data-centric framework designed to empower small models for robust SQL optimization. First, to address the scarcity of existing benchmarks and the limited optimization headroom of generic synthetic queries, we construct SQL-MCTS, a large-scale corpus of complex slow queries. We employ an MCTS-based hybrid expansion strategy that combines rule-guided anti-patterns with LLM mutations to evolve structurally expressive seeds into execution-verified slow variants. Second, to enable the model to autonomously discover latency-aware rewriting patterns, we propose SQL-GRPO, a specialized alignment strategy adapted from Group Relative Policy Optimization. By integrating Anchored Group Advantage to refine advantage estimation and Complexity-Adaptive Dynamic Rollout to efficiently allocate exploration budgets, this approach effectively empowers compact models to master execution-based optimization logic. Implemented on Qwen3 models, LASER significantly outperforms rule-based systems and LLMs in execution efficiency, while exhibiting robust zero-shot transferability with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.GL'></div>

# cs.GL [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [The Theorems of Dr. David Blackwell and Their Contributions to Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06621)
*Napoleon Paxton*

Main category: cs.GL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了David Blackwell的三个核心理论成果（Rao-Blackwell定理、Blackwell可接近性定理、Blackwell信息性定理）及其对现代人工智能和机器学习的深远影响，展示了这些20世纪中叶的理论如何持续塑造当代AI子领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: David Blackwell作为数学家和统计学家，其理论贡献早于现代人工智能的许多算法突破。本文旨在展示他的三个核心理论成果如何持续影响当代AI和机器学习，证明这些历史理论在现代技术中仍具有重要技术价值。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统分析Blackwell的三个主要理论成果：Rao-Blackwell定理、Blackwell可接近性定理和Blackwell信息性定理（实验比较理论）。通过追踪这些理论在当代AI子领域的具体应用，展示其技术延续性。

Result: 研究发现Blackwell的理论成果在多个现代AI子领域保持技术活跃性，包括马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛推断、自主移动机器人导航（SLAM）、生成模型训练、无遗憾在线学习、人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）、大语言模型对齐和信息设计。NVIDIA 2024年将其旗舰GPU架构命名为"Blackwell"进一步证明了其持久相关性。

Conclusion: Blackwell定理构成了一个统一框架，专门解决信息压缩、不确定性下的序列决策和信息源比较等核心AI问题。这些理论成果不仅在历史上具有重要意义，而且持续塑造着现代人工智能的发展方向，特别是在LLM RLHF管道中的显式Rao-Blackwellized方差减少技术代表了新兴前沿。

Abstract: Dr. David Blackwell was a mathematician and statistician of the first rank, whose contributions to statistical theory, game theory, and decision theory predated many of the algorithmic breakthroughs that define modern artificial intelligence. This survey examines three of his most consequential theoretical results the Rao Blackwell theorem, the Blackwell Approachability theorem, and the Blackwell Informativeness theorem (comparison of experiments) and traces their direct influence on contemporary AI and machine learning. We show that these results, developed primarily in the 1940s and 1950s, remain technically live across modern subfields including Markov Chain Monte Carlo inference, autonomous mobile robot navigation (SLAM), generative model training, no-regret online learning, reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), large language model alignment, and information design. NVIDIAs 2024 decision to name their flagship GPU architecture (Blackwell) provides vivid testament to his enduring relevance. We also document an emerging frontier: explicit Rao Blackwellized variance reduction in LLM RLHF pipelines, recently proposed but not yet standard practice. Together, Blackwell theorems form a unified framework addressing information compression, sequential decision making under uncertainty, and the comparison of information sources precisely the problems at the core of modern AI.

</details>
